local Players = game:GetService("Players")
local LocalPlayer = Players.LocalPlayer
local PlayerGui = LocalPlayer:WaitForChild("PlayerGui")
local VirtualInputManager = game:GetService("VirtualInputManager")
local Camera = workspace.CurrentCamera
local UserInputService = game:GetService("UserInputService")

-- ====================== CONFIG ======================
local trackedFruits = {
	["Apple"] = true,
	["Alluring Apple"] = true,
	["Orange"] = true,
	["Pear"] = true,
	["Mango"] = true,
	["Banana"] = true,
	["Life Up Fruit"] = true,
	["Chakra Fruit"] = true,
	["Fruit Of Forgetfulness"] = true
}

local fruitColors = {
	["Apple"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 40, 40),
	["Alluring Apple"] = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 155),
	["Orange"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 140, 0),
	["Pear"] = Color3.fromRGB(80, 220, 70),
	["Mango"] = Color3.fromRGB(215, 100, 15),
	["Banana"] = Color3.fromRGB(255, 235, 40),
	["Life Up Fruit"] = Color3.fromRGB(40, 255, 40),
	["Chakra Fruit"] = Color3.fromRGB(160, 70, 255),
	["Fruit Of Forgetfulness"] = Color3.fromRGB(120, 0, 0),
}
local DEFAULT_FRUIT_COLOR = Color3.fromRGB(0, 255, 140)

-- color for a fruit (also works when the highlighted object is a child of the named fruit)
local function fruitColorFor(obj)
	local cur = obj
	for _ = 1, 6 do
		if not cur then break end
		local c = fruitColors[cur.Name]
		if c then return c end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
	return DEFAULT_FRUIT_COLOR
end

-- fills the highlight with the fruit's color; Life Up Fruit also gets a shiny, pulsing glow
local function styleFruitHighlight(hl, obj)
	hl.FillColor = fruitColorFor(obj)
	local cur = obj
	for _ = 1, 6 do
		if not cur then break end
		if cur.Name == "Life Up Fruit" then
			hl.FillTransparency = 0.2
			hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(200, 255, 200)
			local tw = TweenService:Create(hl, TweenInfo.new(0.7, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {FillTransparency = 0.6})
			tw:Play()
			hl.Destroying:Connect(function() tw:Cancel() end)
			break
		end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
end

local SummonKey = Enum.KeyCode.J
local MacroActive = false
local runId = 0
local ESPEnabled = true
local PlayerEspRange = 10000
local EspOpts = { name = true, outline = true, studs = true, health = true, team = true }
local activeHighlights = {}
local addConnection = nil
local changingKeybind = false

local Settings = {loading = true, file = "DannyGui_Settings.json", save = function() end}
Settings.DX = {sellBusy = false, clicked = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})}

-- Safe file helpers used by every save file in this GUI:
--  * writeJson keeps the previous version as <file>.bak before overwriting, and never writes if encoding fails
--  * readJson falls back to the .bak copy if the main file is missing / corrupted
Settings.readJson = function(path)
	local result
	pcall(function()
		if not (isfile and readfile) then return end
		for _, f in ipairs({path, path .. ".bak"}) do
			if isfile(f) then
				local ok, d = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONDecode(readfile(f)) end)
				if ok and type(d) == "table" then result = d return end
			end
		end
	end)
	return result
end
Settings.writeJson = function(path, tbl)
	local wrote = false
	pcall(function()
		if not writefile then return end
		local ok, text = pcall(function() return game:GetService("HttpService"):JSONEncode(tbl) end)
		if not ok or type(text) ~= "string" or text == "" then return end
		pcall(function()
			if isfile and readfile and isfile(path) then
				local old = readfile(path)
				if type(old) == "string" and old ~= "" and old ~= text then writefile(path .. ".bak", old) end
			end
		end)
		writefile(path, text)
		wrote = true
	end)
	return wrote
end

local FailsafeRange = 550
local FailsafeMode = "kick"
local MenuButtonPos = Vector2.new(244, 34)

local env = (getgenv and getgenv()) or _G
if env.DannyGuiStop then pcall(env.DannyGuiStop) end

	PlayerGui.DannyFruitFarm_GUI:Destroy()
end

-- ====================== UI HELPERS ======================
local WHITE = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
local RED = Color3.fromRGB(175, 175, 182)
local RED_BRIGHT = Color3.fromRGB(240, 226, 200)
local RED_DARK = Color3.fromRGB(60, 60, 64)

local function tween(obj, time, props)
	TweenService:Create(obj, TweenInfo.new(time, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), props):Play()
end

local function addCorner(parent, radius)
	local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
	c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, radius)
	c.Parent = parent
	return c
end

local function addStroke(parent, color, thickness, transparency)
	local s = Instance.new("UIStroke")
	s.Color = color
	s.Thickness = thickness or 1
	s.Transparency = transparency or 0
	s.Parent = parent
	return s
end

local function addGradient(parent, c0, c1, rotation)
	local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	g.Color = ColorSequence.new(c0, c1)
	g.Rotation = rotation or 90
	g.Parent = parent
	return g
end

local function addFadeLine(parent, rotation)
	local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	g.Rotation = rotation or 0
	g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0.1),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
	})
	g.Parent = parent
	return g
end

-- Hover uses a see-through overlay, so the button keeps whatever color / transparency
-- the chosen GUI style gave it (no more fixed blue hover colors).
local function addHover(btn, normal, hover)
	local overlay = Instance.new("Frame")
	overlay.Name = "HoverOverlay"
	overlay.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	overlay.BorderSizePixel = 0
	overlay.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	overlay.ZIndex = btn.ZIndex
	overlay:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
	overlay:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)

	local hoverAlpha = 0.86
	if hover and hover.R > 0.58 and hover.G < 0.36 then
		-- red hover (close buttons) stays red
		overlay.BackgroundColor3 = hover
		hoverAlpha = 0.45
	else
		overlay.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	end

	local corner = btn:FindFirstChildOfClass("UICorner")
	local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
	c.CornerRadius = corner and corner.CornerRadius or UDim.new(0, 5)
	c.Parent = overlay
	overlay.Parent = btn

	local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
	scale.Parent = btn

	btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
		tween(overlay, 0.18, {BackgroundTransparency = hoverAlpha})
		tween(scale, 0.18, {Scale = 1.04})
	end)
	btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		tween(overlay, 0.22, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
		tween(scale, 0.22, {Scale = 1})
	end)
	if btn:IsA("GuiButton") then
		btn.MouseButton1Down:Connect(function() tween(scale, 0.08, {Scale = 0.96}) end)
		btn.MouseButton1Up:Connect(function() tween(scale, 0.14, {Scale = 1.04}) end)
	end
end

-- ====================== STYLE / ORNATE WHITE BORDER ======================
-- OPTIONAL: upload your border picture to Roblox (Create > Decals), then paste its id here,
-- e.g. "rbxassetid://1234567890". Leave it "" to use the built-in white filigree border.
local BORDER_IMAGE = ""
local BORDER_SLICE = Rect.new(48, 48, 91, 91) -- 9-slice area of your picture (pixels), tweak to taste

-- Anything marked is kept crisp white: it ignores the transparency slider and the color wheel.
local function mark(obj)
	obj:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
	obj:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
	return obj
end

local function ornFrame(parent, size, pos, anchor)
	local f = Instance.new("Frame")
	f.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	f.BorderSizePixel = 0
	f.Size = size
	f.Position = pos or UDim2.new()
	f.AnchorPoint = anchor or Vector2.new()
	f.Parent = parent
	return mark(f)
end

local function ornSolid(parent, size, pos, anchor, round, rot)
	local f = ornFrame(parent, size, pos, anchor)
	f.BackgroundTransparency = 0
	f.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
	if rot then f.Rotation = rot end
	if round then addCorner(f, round) end
	return f
end

local function ornRing(parent, diameter, pos, thickness)
	local r = ornFrame(parent, UDim2.new(0, diameter, 0, diameter), pos, Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5))
	local c = Instance.new("UICorner")
	c.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.5, 0)
	c.Parent = r
	mark(addStroke(r, WHITE, thickness, 0))
	return r
end

local function ornLine(parent, size, pos, anchor, gradRotation)
	local l = ornSolid(parent, size, pos, anchor, 2)
	local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	g.Rotation = gradRotation
	g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1),
	})
	g.Parent = l
	mark(g)
	return l
end

Settings.ShimmerList = {} -- looping border shimmers, so they can be paused while their panel is hidden
local function addOrnateBorder(parent, radius, full)
	local root = ornFrame(parent, UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0))
	root.Name = "OrnateBorder"
	root.ZIndex = 10
	-- Fast path while the GUI is still loading: simple stroke only (prevents execute freeze)
	if Settings.loading and full then
		addCorner(root, radius)
		mark(addStroke(root, WHITE, 2, 0.15))
		return root
	end

	if BORDER_IMAGE ~= "" then
		local img = Instance.new("ImageLabel")
		img.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		img.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		img.Image = BORDER_IMAGE
		img.ScaleType = Enum.ScaleType.Slice
		img.SliceCenter = BORDER_SLICE
		img.ImageColor3 = WHITE
		img.Parent = root
		mark(img)
		return root
	end

	-- soft outer glow
	local glow = ornFrame(root, UDim2.new(1, 4, 1, 4), UDim2.new(0, -2, 0, -2))
	addCorner(glow, radius + 2)
	mark(addStroke(glow, WHITE, 2, 0.94))

	-- main white line + fine inner line
	local outer = ornFrame(root, UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0))
	addCorner(outer, radius)
	local outerStroke = mark(addStroke(outer, WHITE, 2, 0))

	local inner = ornFrame(root, UDim2.new(1, -12, 1, -12), UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6))
	addCorner(inner, math.max(radius - 3, 2))
	mark(addStroke(inner, WHITE, 1, 0.5))

	-- slow shimmer that travels around the border
	local shimmer = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	shimmer.Rotation = 0
	shimmer.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0.55),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.5, 0),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.55),
	})
	shimmer.Parent = outerStroke
	mark(shimmer)
	local shimmerTw = TweenService:Create(shimmer, TweenInfo.new(7, Enum.EasingStyle.Linear, Enum.EasingDirection.In, -1), {
		Rotation = 360,
	})
	shimmerTw:Play()
	Settings.ShimmerList[#Settings.ShimmerList + 1] = { tw = shimmerTw, owner = parent }

	local center = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)

	if not full then
		for _, a in ipairs({{0, 0}, {1, 0}, {0, 1}, {1, 1}}) do
			ornSolid(root, UDim2.new(0, 6, 0, 6), UDim2.new(a[1], 0, a[2], 0), center, nil, 45)
		end
		return root
	end

	-- corner scrolls: ring + dot + two tapering accent lines hugging the edges
	local function cornerOrn(ax, ay)
		local sx = ax == 0 and 1 or -1
		local sy = ay == 0 and 1 or -1
		local cpos = UDim2.new(ax, sx * 7, ay, sy * 7)
		ornRing(root, 17, cpos, 1.5)
		ornSolid(root, UDim2.new(0, 5, 0, 5), cpos, center, 3)
		ornLine(root, UDim2.new(0, 46, 0, 3), UDim2.new(ax, sx * 20, ay, 0), Vector2.new(ax, 0.5), ax == 0 and 0 or 180)
		ornLine(root, UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 46), UDim2.new(ax, 0, ay, sy * 20), Vector2.new(0.5, ay), ay == 0 and 90 or 270)
	end
	for _, a in ipairs({{0, 0}, {1, 0}, {0, 1}, {1, 1}}) do
		cornerOrn(a[1], a[2])
	end

	-- middle-of-edge ornaments (diamond in a ring, flanked by dots)
	local function midOrn(vertical, far)
		local e = far and 1 or 0
		local function at(offset)
			if vertical then return UDim2.new(e, 0, 0.5, offset) end
			return UDim2.new(0.5, offset, e, 0)
		end
		ornRing(root, 13, at(0), 1)
		ornSolid(root, UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7), at(0), center, nil, 45)
		ornSolid(root, UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3), at(-14), center, 2)
		ornSolid(root, UDim2.new(0, 3, 0, 3), at(14), center, 2)
		if not vertical then
			ornLine(root, UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 2), at(-18), Vector2.new(1, 0.5), 180)
			ornLine(root, UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 2), at(18), Vector2.new(0, 0.5), 0)
		end
	end
	midOrn(false, false)
	midOrn(false, true)
	midOrn(true, false)
	midOrn(true, true)

	return root
end

local function addDiamond(parent, pos, size, color)
	local d = Instance.new("Frame")
	d.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	d.Position = pos
	d.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
	d.Rotation = 45
	d.BackgroundColor3 = color or WHITE
	d.BorderSizePixel = 0
	d.Parent = parent
	mark(d)
	return d
end

local function addDivider(parent, y, inset)
	local left = Instance.new("Frame")
	left.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -(inset + 8), 0, 1)
	left.Position = UDim2.new(0, inset, 0, y)
	left.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
	mark(left)
	left.BorderSizePixel = 0
	left.Parent = parent
	local g = Instance.new("UIGradient")
	g.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1),
		NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0.1),
	})
	g.Parent = left

	local right = left:Clone()
	right.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0)
	right.Position = UDim2.new(1, -inset, 0, y)
	right:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIGradient").Rotation = 180
	right.Parent = parent

	addDiamond(parent, UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, y), 7, WHITE)
end

local function createCard(height, order, parent, noHover)
	local card = Instance.new("Frame")
	card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, height)
	card.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
	card.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
	card.BorderSizePixel = 0
	card.LayoutOrder = order
	card.ClipsDescendants = true
	card.Parent = parent or Content

	addCorner(card, 8)
	addGradient(card, Color3.fromRGB(40, 68, 86), Color3.fromRGB(18, 27, 38), 45)
	local stroke = mark(addStroke(card, WHITE, 1.2, 0.55))

	local inner = Instance.new("Frame")
	inner.Size = UDim2.new(1, -8, 1, -8)
	inner.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
	inner.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	inner.BorderSizePixel = 0
	inner.Parent = card
	addCorner(inner, 6)
	mark(addStroke(inner, WHITE, 1, 0.88))

	-- polish: glass sheen + accent bar (skipped while loading to prevent execute freeze)
	if not Settings.loading then
		local sheen = Instance.new("Frame")
		sheen.Name = "Sheen"
		sheen.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 1)
		sheen.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 1)
		sheen.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		sheen.BackgroundTransparency = 0.55
		sheen.BorderSizePixel = 0
		sheen.Parent = card
		addFadeLine(sheen, 0)
		mark(sheen)
	end

	local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
	bar.Name = "AccentBar"
	bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 3, 1, -18)
	bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 9)
	bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(105, 215, 255)
	bar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.7
	bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	bar.Parent = card
	bar:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
	addCorner(bar, 2)

	if not noHover then
		card.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(stroke, 0.2, {Thickness = 2, Transparency = 0.12}) end)
		card.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(stroke, 0.2, {Thickness = 1.2, Transparency = 0.55}) end)
	end

	return card
end

local function createToggleCard(order, iconText, iconSize, titleText, subText, hasTimer)
	local cardHeight = hasTimer and 66 or 58
	local yOff = hasTimer and 4 or 0
	local card = createCard(cardHeight, order)

	local chip = Instance.new("Frame")
	chip.Name = "IconChip"
	chip.Size = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 34)
	chip.Position = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, (cardHeight - 34) / 2)
	chip.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(58, 108, 138)
	chip.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
	chip.BorderSizePixel = 0
	chip.Parent = card
	chip:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
	addCorner(chip, 9)
	addStroke(chip, Color3.fromRGB(105, 215, 255), 1, 0.65)

	local icon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	icon.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 1, 0)
	icon.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
	icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	icon.Text = iconText
	icon.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 196)
	icon.TextSize = iconSize
	icon.Parent = card

	local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 22)
	title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 10 + yOff)
	title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	title.Text = titleText
	title.TextColor3 = CREAM
	title.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
	title.TextSize = 18
	title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	title.TextScaled = true
	title.Parent = card

	local titleSize = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
	titleSize.MaxTextSize = 19
	titleSize.MinTextSize = 10
	titleSize.Parent = title

	local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 16)
	sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 31 + yOff)
	sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	sub.Text = subText
	sub.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
	sub.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	sub.TextSize = 14
	sub.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	sub.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	sub.Parent = card

	local toggle = Instance.new("TextButton")
	toggle.Name = "OnOffToggle"
	toggle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 28)
	toggle.Position = hasTimer and UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, -8) or UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, -14)
	toggle.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(88, 88, 94)
	toggle.Text = ""
	toggle.AutoButtonColor = false
	toggle.Parent = card
	addCorner(toggle, 14)
	addStroke(toggle, Color3.fromRGB(88, 88, 94), 1.5, 0.5)

	local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
	knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
	knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -11)
	knob.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
	knob.Parent = toggle
	addCorner(knob, 11)

	local timerLabel = nil
	if hasTimer then
		timerLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		timerLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 16)
		timerLabel.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 6)
		timerLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		timerLabel.Text = "00:00"
		timerLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 166)
		timerLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
		timerLabel.TextSize = 15
		timerLabel.TextScaled = true
		timerLabel.Parent = card

		local timerSize = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
		timerSize.MaxTextSize = 15
		timerSize.MinTextSize = 8
		timerSize.Parent = timerLabel
	end

	return card, toggle, knob, timerLabel
end

local failsafeOptions = {
	{mode = "kick", text = "Kick if player within " .. FailsafeRange .. " studs"},
	{mode = "hop",  text = "Server hop if player within " .. FailsafeRange .. " studs"},
}
local failsafeRows = {}
local RADIO_OFF = Color3.fromRGB(115, 115, 121)
local TEXT_OFF = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)

local function selectFailsafe(mode)
	FailsafeMode = mode
	for m, row in pairs(failsafeRows) do
		local on = (m == mode)
		tween(row.dot, 0.15, {Size = on and UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8) or UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1})
		tween(row.ring, 0.15, {Color = on and CREAM or RADIO_OFF})
		tween(row.label, 0.15, {TextColor3 = on and CREAM or TEXT_OFF})
	end
	Settings.save()
end

for i, opt in ipairs(failsafeOptions) do
	local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
	row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 25)
	row.Position = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 38 + (i - 1) * 26)
	row.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER
	row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	row.Text = ""
	row.AutoButtonColor = false
	row.Parent = FailsafeCard
	addCorner(row, 5)
	row.MouseEnter:Connect(function() tween(row, 0.15, {BackgroundTransparency = 0.9}) end)
	row.MouseLeave:Connect(function() tween(row, 0.15, {BackgroundTransparency = 1}) end)

	local radio = Instance.new("Frame")
	radio.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
	radio.Position = UDim2.new(0, 6, 0.5, -8)
	radio.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	radio.Parent = row
	addCorner(radio, 8)
	local ring = addStroke(radio, RADIO_OFF, 1.5, 0)

	local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
	dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	dot.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
	dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	dot.BackgroundColor3 = CREAM
	dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
	dot.Parent = radio
	dot:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
	dot:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
	local dotCorner = Instance.new("UICorner")
	dotCorner.CornerRadius = UDim.new(0.5, 0)
	dotCorner.Parent = dot

	local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	label.Size = UDim2.new(1, -34, 1, 0)
	label.Position = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 0)
	label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	label.Text = opt.text
	label.TextColor3 = TEXT_OFF
	label.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	label.TextSize = 15
	label.TextScaled = true
	label.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	label.Parent = row

	local labelSize = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
	labelSize.MaxTextSize = 16
	labelSize.MinTextSize = 8
	labelSize.Parent = label

	failsafeRows[opt.mode] = {dot = dot, ring = ring, label = label}
	row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() selectFailsafe(opt.mode) end)
end
selectFailsafe(FailsafeMode)

do
	local SellPos = Vector3.new(67, 211, -2340)

	local card = createCard(134, 7)

	noSell.TextWrapped = true

	noSell.Parent = card

	local desc = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	desc.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 20)
	desc.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 110)
	desc.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	desc.Text = "USE AT OWN RISK"
	desc.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(220, 50, 50)
	desc.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
	desc.TextSize = 16
	desc.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	desc.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	desc:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
	desc:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
	desc.Parent = card

	local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	btn.Text = ""
	btn.AutoButtonColor = false
	btn.ZIndex = 5
	btn.Parent = card

	-- "Are you sure?" Yes / No overlay when manually clicking Fast Sell
	local confirm = Instance.new("Frame")
	confirm.Name = "FastSellConfirm"
	confirm.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 1, -16)
	confirm.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
	confirm.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 26, 34)
	confirm.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
	confirm.BorderSizePixel = 0
	confirm.Visible = false
	confirm.ZIndex = 25
	confirm.Parent = card
	addCorner(confirm, 6)
	addStroke(confirm, Color3.fromRGB(255, 110, 0), 1.5, 0.15)

	local confirmTitle = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	confirmTitle.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 28)
	confirmTitle.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 18)

	confirmTitle.Text = "Are You Sure?"

	confirmTitle.ZIndex = 26

	confirmTitle:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
	confirmTitle.Parent = confirm

	local confirmSub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	confirmSub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 32)
	confirmSub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 48)
	confirmSub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	confirmSub.Text = "Teleport to merchant, sell fruits, then return."
	confirmSub.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 196)
	confirmSub.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	confirmSub.TextSize = 14
	confirmSub.TextWrapped = true
	confirmSub.ZIndex = 26
	confirmSub:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
	confirmSub.Parent = confirm

	local function makeConfirmBtn(text, xScale, color, hoverColor)
		local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
		b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 90, 0, 30)
		b.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0)
		b.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, 0, 1, -42)
		b.BackgroundColor3 = color
		b.Text = text
		b.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		b.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
		b.TextSize = 16
		b.AutoButtonColor = false
		b.ZIndex = 26
		b:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
		b.Parent = confirm
		addCorner(b, 5)
		addStroke(b, Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0), 1, 0.5)
		addHover(b, color, hoverColor)
		return b
	end
	local yesBtn = makeConfirmBtn("Yes", 0.32, Color3.fromRGB(46, 125, 70), Color3.fromRGB(60, 160, 90))
	local noBtn = makeConfirmBtn("No", 0.68, Color3.fromRGB(150, 45, 45), Color3.fromRGB(200, 60, 60))

	local selling = false

	local function pressE()
		pcall(function()
			VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
			task.wait(0.08)
			VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
		end)
	end

	local function doSell()
		if selling then return false end
		if not SellPos then
			desc.Text = "No sell coords set in script"
			task.delay(1.5, function()
				if desc.Parent then desc.Text = "USE AT OWN RISK" end
			end)
			return false
		end
		local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
		local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not root then return false end

		selling = true
		Settings.DX.sellBusy = true
		task.spawn(function()
			pcall(function()
				local backCFrame = root.CFrame
				root.CFrame = CFrame.new(SellPos + Vector3.new(0, 2, 0))
				-- stay ~5 seconds total: 0.5s to settle, then 5 E presses spaced 0.9s apart
				task.wait(0.5)

				for _ = 1, 5 do
					pressE()
					task.wait(0.9)
				end

				local newCh = LocalPlayer.Character
				local newRoot = newCh and newCh:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if newRoot then newRoot.CFrame = backCFrame end
			end)
			selling = false
			Settings.DX.sellBusy = false
		end)
		return true
	end
	Settings.DX.sell = doSell

	btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if selling or confirm.Visible then return end
		confirm.Visible = true
	end)
	yesBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		confirm.Visible = false
		doSell()
	end)
	noBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		confirm.Visible = false
	end)
end

local currentPage = "home"
local pageFrames = {home = HomePage, farm = FarmPage, qol = QOLPage, guisettings = GUISettingsPage, teleportspectate = TeleportSpectatePage, teleports = Pages:FindFirstChild("TeleportsPage"), bosses = nil, npcquests = nil}
local function showPage(name)
	if name == "bosses" and Settings._ensureBossCards then pcall(Settings._ensureBossCards) end
	if name == "npcquests" and Settings._ensureQuestCards then pcall(Settings._ensureQuestCards) end
	if name == currentPage then return end
	local outgoing = pageFrames[currentPage]
	local incoming = pageFrames[name]
	local forward = (name ~= "home")
	currentPage = name

	incoming.Visible = true
	incoming.Position = UDim2.new(forward and 1 or -1, 0, 0, 0)
	local info = TweenInfo.new(0.46, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
	TweenService:Create(incoming, info, {Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)}):Play()

	local leave = TweenService:Create(outgoing, info, {Position = UDim2.new(forward and -1 or 1, 0, 0, 0)})
	leave.Completed:Connect(function()
		if currentPage == name then outgoing.Visible = false end
	end)
	leave:Play()
end
BackBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showPage("home") end)

xpcall(function()
	for _, left in ipairs({true, false}) do
		local ln = Instance.new("Frame")
		ln.Name = "FeaturesLine"
		ln.AnchorPoint = left and Vector2.new(0, 0) or Vector2.new(1, 0)
		ln.Position = left and UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 146) or UDim2.new(1, -16, 0, 146)
		ln.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -62, 0, 1)
		ln.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER
		ln.BorderSizePixel = 0
		ln.Parent = HomePage
		addFadeLine(ln, left and 0 or 180)
	end
end, function(e) warn("[DannyGui] header lines: " .. tostring(e)) end)

local HK = {esp = {}, fly = {speed = 1}, noclip = {}, nofog = {}, infjump = {}, infzoom = {}, nofall = {}, light = {}}
local hotkeyListening = nil

local function showHotkey(hk)
	hk.box.Text = hk.key and hk.key.Name or "None"
	hk.box.TextColor3 = CREAM
end

local function addHotkeySection(card, toggle, iconText, hk, topY)
	topY = topY or 58
	card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, topY + 48)
	toggle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, 15)
	for _, child in ipairs(card:GetChildren()) do
		if child:IsA("TextLabel") and child.Text == iconText then
			child.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 58)
		end
	end

	local line = Instance.new("Frame")
	line.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
	line.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, topY)
	line.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER
	line.BorderSizePixel = 0
	line.Parent = card
	addFadeLine(line, 0)

	local box = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 30)
	box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, topY + 8)
	box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 30, 40)
	box.Text = "None"
	box.TextColor3 = CREAM
	box.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
	box.TextSize = 17
	box.TextScaled = true
	box.Parent = card
	addCorner(box, 5)
	addStroke(box, SILVER_DIM, 1.2, 0.1)
	local boxSize = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
	boxSize.MaxTextSize = 17
	boxSize.MinTextSize = 8
	boxSize.Parent = box
	local boxPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	boxPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
	boxPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
	boxPad.Parent = box
	hk.box = box

	local changeBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	changeBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 116, 0, 30)
	changeBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, topY + 8)
	changeBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103)
	changeBtn.Text = "Change hotkey"
	changeBtn.TextColor3 = CREAM
	changeBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
	changeBtn.TextSize = 17
	changeBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	changeBtn.Parent = card
	addCorner(changeBtn, 5)
	addStroke(changeBtn, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
	addHover(changeBtn, Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103), Color3.fromRGB(66, 122, 148))

	local xBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	xBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 30, 0, 30)
	xBtn.Position = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, topY + 8)
	xBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 45, 45)
	xBtn.Text = "X"
	xBtn.TextColor3 = WHITE
	xBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
	xBtn.TextSize = 18
	xBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	xBtn.Parent = card
	addCorner(xBtn, 5)
	addStroke(xBtn, Color3.fromRGB(120, 30, 30), 1, 0.2)
	addHover(xBtn, Color3.fromRGB(170, 45, 45), Color3.fromRGB(215, 65, 65))

	changeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if hotkeyListening == hk then
			hotkeyListening = nil
			showHotkey(hk)
			return
		end
		if hotkeyListening then showHotkey(hotkeyListening) end
		hotkeyListening = hk
		box.Text = "..."
		box.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50)
	end)

	xBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if hotkeyListening == hk then hotkeyListening = nil end
		hk.key = nil
		showHotkey(hk)
		Settings.save()
	end)
end

local hotkeyConn = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
	if input.UserInputType ~= Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then return end
	if hotkeyListening then
		if gp then return end
		local hk = hotkeyListening
		hotkeyListening = nil
		for _, other in pairs(HK) do
			if other ~= hk and other.key == input.KeyCode then
				other.key = nil
				showHotkey(other)
			end
		end
		hk.key = input.KeyCode
		showHotkey(hk)
		Settings.save()
		return
	end
	if gp then return end
	for _, hk in pairs(HK) do
		if hk.key and hk.action and input.KeyCode == hk.key then
			hk.action()
			if Settings.hotkeyToast then pcall(Settings.hotkeyToast, hk) end
		end
	end
end)

local function clearHotkeys()
	if hotkeyConn then hotkeyConn:Disconnect() hotkeyConn = nil end
	hotkeyListening = nil
end

	task.wait()

	QOLContent.Parent = QOLPage

	local function setupEspCard()
		PlayerEspCard, PlayerEspToggle, PlayerEspKnob = createToggleCard(1, "👥", 18, "ESP", "Click card for options")
		PlayerEspCard.Parent = QOLContent
		-- allow full-card hover outline (createToggleCard uses createCard with hover on)
		-- but createToggleCard doesn't pass noHover so stroke hover already exists

		local espDropOpen = false
		local BASE_H = 58
		local RANGE_H = 40
		local HOTKEY_H = 48
		local DROP_H = 138
		local animToken = 0

		local function closedHeight()
			return BASE_H + RANGE_H + HOTKEY_H
		end
		local function openHeight()
			return closedHeight() + DROP_H + 8
		end

		-- Range row (under title)
		do
			local rLine = Instance.new("Frame")
			rLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
			rLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 58)
			rLine.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER
			rLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
			rLine.Parent = PlayerEspCard
			addFadeLine(rLine, 0)

			local rLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			rLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 18)
			rLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 68)
			rLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			rLabel.Text = "Range"
			rLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
			rLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
			rLabel.TextSize = 13
			rLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			rLabel.Parent = PlayerEspCard

			local rTrack = Instance.new("Frame")
			rTrack.Size = UDim2.new(1, -112, 0, 6)
			rTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 74)
			rTrack.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 40, 46)
			rTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
			rTrack.Parent = PlayerEspCard
			addCorner(rTrack, 3)

			local rFill = Instance.new("Frame")
			rFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
			rFill.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(66, 122, 148)
			rFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
			rFill.Parent = rTrack
			addCorner(rFill, 3)

			local rKnob = Instance.new("Frame")
			rKnob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 14)
			rKnob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
			rKnob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			rKnob.BackgroundColor3 = CREAM
			rKnob.BorderSizePixel = 0
			rKnob.Parent = rTrack
			addCorner(rKnob, 7)
			addStroke(rKnob, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.2)

			local rValue = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			rValue.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 26)
			rValue.Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 66)
			rValue.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 30, 40)
			rValue.TextColor3 = CREAM
			rValue.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
			rValue.TextSize = 16
			rValue.Parent = PlayerEspCard
			addCorner(rValue, 5)
			addStroke(rValue, SILVER_DIM, 1.2, 0.1)

			local MIN_R, MAX_R = 50, 10000
			local function setRange(v, silent)
				v = math.clamp(math.floor(v / 10 + 0.5) * 10, MIN_R, MAX_R)
				PlayerEspRange = v
				local a = (v - MIN_R) / (MAX_R - MIN_R)
				rFill.Size = UDim2.new(a, 0, 1, 0)
				rKnob.Position = UDim2.new(a, 0, 0.5, 0)
				if v >= MAX_R then
					rValue.Text = "MAP"
				else
					rValue.Text = tostring(v)
				end
				if not silent then Settings.save() end
			end
			HK.esp.setRange = setRange
			setRange(PlayerEspRange, true)

			local rHit = Instance.new("TextButton")
			rHit.Name = "EspRangeHit"
			rHit.Size = UDim2.new(1, -112, 0, 28)
			rHit.Position = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 65)
			rHit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			rHit.Text = ""
			rHit.AutoButtonColor = false
			rHit.ZIndex = 3
			rHit.Parent = PlayerEspCard

			local dragging = false
			local function fromX(x)
				local a = math.clamp((x - rTrack.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(rTrack.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
				setRange(MIN_R + a * (MAX_R - MIN_R), true)
			end
			rHit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
					dragging = true
					fromX(input.Position.X)
				end
			end)
			UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
				if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
					fromX(input.Position.X)
				end
			end)
			UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
				if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
					dragging = false
					Settings.save()
				end
			end)
		end

		-- Hotkey row (Change hotkey) sits under range
		addHotkeySection(PlayerEspCard, PlayerEspToggle, "👥", HK.esp, 100)

		-- Options panel sits BELOW the hotkey row (hidden until expanded)
		local dropPanel = Instance.new("Frame")
		dropPanel.Name = "EspOptionsPanel"
		dropPanel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0)
		dropPanel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, closedHeight() - 4)
		dropPanel.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 30, 40)
		dropPanel.BackgroundTransparency = 0.3
		dropPanel.ClipsDescendants = true
		dropPanel.Visible = true
		dropPanel.ZIndex = 3
		dropPanel.Parent = PlayerEspCard
		addCorner(dropPanel, 6)
		local dropStroke = addStroke(dropPanel, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.35)

		local dropInner = Instance.new("Frame")
		dropInner.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, DROP_H)
		dropInner.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		dropInner.Parent = dropPanel

		local dropList = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
		dropList.Padding = UDim.new(0, 4)
		dropList.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		dropList.Parent = dropInner
		local dropPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
		dropPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 8)
		dropPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8)
		dropPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 10)
		dropPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 10)
		dropPad.Parent = dropInner

		local function paintBox(box, check, on)
			-- Always the same look: dark box; checked = white ✓ only (never green, never grey-out)
			box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 30, 40)
			box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
			if on then
				check.Text = "✓"
				check.TextColor3 = WHITE
				check.TextTransparency = 0
			else
				check.Text = ""
			end
		end

		local function applyEspOptionsNow()
			-- Fully tear down then rebuild so name/outline/studs/health apply immediately
			local toClear = {}
			for plr in pairs(playerEsp) do
				table.insert(toClear, plr)
			end
			for _, plr in ipairs(toClear) do
				removePlayerEsp(plr)
			end
			if PlayerEspEnabled then
				-- run now + next frame so Heartbeat state is clean
				pcall(refreshPlayerEsp)
				task.defer(function()
					if PlayerEspEnabled then
						pcall(refreshPlayerEsp)
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		local function makeEspOption(order, label, key)
			local row = Instance.new("TextButton")
			row.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 22)
			row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			row.Text = ""
			row.AutoButtonColor = false
			row.LayoutOrder = order
			row.ZIndex = 4
			row.Parent = dropInner

			local box = Instance.new("Frame")
			box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 16)
			box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0.5, -8)
			box.BorderSizePixel = 0
			box.ZIndex = 5
			box.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 30, 40)
			box.BackgroundTransparency = 0.35
			box.Parent = row
			box:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
			box:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
			addCorner(box, 4)
			local boxStroke = addStroke(box, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.35)
			boxStroke:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
			boxStroke:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)

			local check = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			check.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			check.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			check.TextColor3 = WHITE
			check.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
			check.TextSize = 13
			check.ZIndex = 6
			check.Parent = box
			check:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
			check:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
			check:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)

			paintBox(box, check, EspOpts[key] == true)

			local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, 0)
			lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 0)
			lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			lbl.Text = label
			lbl.TextColor3 = CREAM
			lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
			lbl.TextSize = 14
			lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			lbl.ZIndex = 5
			lbl.Parent = row

			row.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				EspOpts[key] = not EspOpts[key]
				paintBox(box, check, EspOpts[key] == true)
				Settings.save()
				applyEspOptionsNow()
			end)
		end

		makeEspOption(1, "Player name", "name")
		makeEspOption(2, "Outline", "outline")
		makeEspOption(3, "Studs", "studs")
		makeEspOption(4, "Health", "health")
		makeEspOption(5, "Village", "team")

		local function setExpanded(open)
			espDropOpen = open
			animToken = animToken + (1)
			local token = animToken
			local targetH = open and openHeight() or closedHeight()
			local targetDrop = open and DROP_H or 0
			dropPanel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, closedHeight() - 4)
			tween(PlayerEspCard, 0.22, {Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, targetH)})
			tween(dropPanel, 0.22, {Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, targetDrop)})
			if dropStroke then
				tween(dropStroke, 0.22, {Transparency = open and 0.25 or 1})
			end
		end

		PlayerEspCard.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, closedHeight())
		dropPanel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0)

		-- Click anywhere on the card to expand/collapse (except toggle, range, hotkey, option rows)
		local cardHit = Instance.new("TextButton")
		cardHit.Name = "EspCardHit"
		cardHit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, closedHeight())
		cardHit.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
		cardHit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		cardHit.Text = ""
		cardHit.AutoButtonColor = false
		cardHit.ZIndex = 2
		cardHit.Parent = PlayerEspCard

		-- Keep interactive controls above the hit layer
		PlayerEspToggle.ZIndex = 5
		PlayerEspKnob.ZIndex = 6
		for _, child in ipairs(PlayerEspCard:GetDescendants()) do
			if child:IsA("TextButton") and child ~= cardHit and child.Name ~= "EspCardHit" then
				if child.ZIndex < 4 then child.ZIndex = 4 end
			end
		end

		cardHit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			setExpanded(not espDropOpen)
			-- keep hit area covering the closed portion only

			end
		end)

		fgStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
		fgStroke:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)

		-- results list (card grows with it)
		local fgRes = Instance.new("Frame")
		fgRes.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 0)
		fgRes.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, FG_TOP)

		fgList.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
		fgList.Parent = fgRes
		fgList:GetPropertyChangedSignal("AbsoluteContentSize"):Connect(function()
			local h = fgList.AbsoluteContentSize.Y
			fgRes.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, h)
			fgCard.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, FG_TOP + h + 12)
		end)

		local fgToken = 0
		local function fgClear()
			for _, c in ipairs(fgRes:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("TextLabel") then c:Destroy() end
			end
		end
		local function fgRow(text, order, bold, plr)
			local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			l.Size = UDim2.new(1, plr and -118 or 0, 0, 18)
			l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			l.Text = text
			l.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			l.Font = bold and Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold or Enum.Font.SourceSans
			l.TextSize = 15
			l.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
			l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
			l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
			l.LayoutOrder = order
			l:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
			l.Parent = fgRes

			-- leaderboard / tab-list team: colored dot on the right + the team name
			if plr then
				local team = plr.Team
				local teamColor = team and team.TeamColor.Color or Color3.fromRGB(130, 130, 138)
				local teamName = team and team.Name or "No team"

				local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
				dot.Name = "TeamDot"
				dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 10, 0, 10)
				dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
				dot.Position = UDim2.new(1, 14, 0.5, 0)
				dot.BackgroundColor3 = teamColor
				dot.BackgroundTransparency = 0
				dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
				dot:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
				Instance.new("UICorner", dot).CornerRadius = UDim.new(1, 0)
				local dotStroke = Instance.new("UIStroke")
				dotStroke.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				dotStroke.Thickness = 1
				dotStroke.Transparency = 0.4
				dotStroke:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
				dotStroke.Parent = dot
				dot.Parent = l

				local tl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
				tl.Name = "TeamName"
				tl.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 1, 0)
				tl.Position = UDim2.new(1, 18, 0, 0)
				tl.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
				tl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				tl.Text = teamName
				tl.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				tl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
				tl.TextSize = 14
				tl.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
				tl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
				tl.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
				tl:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
				tl.Parent = l
			end
			return l
		end

		-- best match: name/display starts with the text, else contains it
		local function fgFind(query)
			query = string.lower((tostring(query or ""):gsub("^%s+", ""):gsub("%s+$", "")))
			if query == "" then return nil, {} end
			local starts, contains = {}, {}
			for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
				local n, d = plr.Name:lower(), (plr.DisplayName or plr.Name):lower()
				if n:sub(1, #query) == query or d:sub(1, #query) == query then
					table.insert(starts, plr)
				elseif n:find(query, 1, true) or d:find(query, 1, true) then
					table.insert(contains, plr)
				end
			end
			local all = {}
			for _, p in ipairs(starts) do table.insert(all, p) end
			for _, p in ipairs(contains) do table.insert(all, p) end
			return all[1], all
		end

		local function fgRun()
			fgToken = fgToken + (1)
			local myToken = fgToken
			fgClear()
			local query = fgBox.Text
			if (query:gsub("%s", "")) == "" then
				fgRow("Type a name to see who they're friends with.", 1)
				return
			end
			local target, all = fgFind(query)
			if not target then
				fgRow("No player in this server matches that.", 1)
				return
			end
			fgRow("Checking " .. target.Name .. "...", 1, true)

			task.spawn(function()
				-- fallback list (only fetched if IsFriendsWith isn't usable)
				local friendSet = nil
				local function loadFriendSet()
					friendSet = {}
					pcall(function()
						local pages = Players:GetFriendsAsync(target.UserId)
						while true do
							for _, item in ipairs(pages:GetCurrentPage()) do
								friendSet[item.Id] = true
							end
							if pages.IsFinished then break end
							pages:AdvanceToNextPageAsync()
						end
					end)
				end

				local friends = {}
				for _, other in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
					if other ~= target then
						local ok, res = pcall(function() return target:IsFriendsWith(other.UserId) end)
						if not ok then
							if not friendSet then loadFriendSet() end
							res = friendSet[other.UserId] == true
						end
						if res then table.insert(friends, other) end
					end
				end
				if myToken ~= fgToken then return end -- a newer search started

				table.sort(friends, function(a, b) return a.Name:lower() < b.Name:lower() end)
				fgClear()
				local label = target.Name
				if target.DisplayName and target.DisplayName ~= target.Name then
					label = target.DisplayName .. " (@" .. target.Name .. ")"
				end
				local order = 1
				fgRow("Player: " .. label, order, true, target) order = order + (1)
				if #all > 1 then
					local others = {}
					for i = 2, math.min(#all, 4) do table.insert(others, all[i].Name) end
					fgRow("Also matches: " .. table.concat(others, ", ") .. (#all > 4 and ", ..." or ""), order) order = order + (1)
				end
				if #friends == 0 then
					fgRow("No friends of theirs are in this server.", order)
				else
					fgRow("Friends in this server (" .. #friends .. "):", order, true) order = order + (1)
					for _, f in ipairs(friends) do
						local nm = f.Name
						if f.DisplayName and f.DisplayName ~= f.Name then
							nm = f.DisplayName .. " (@" .. f.Name .. ")"
						end
						fgRow("•  " .. nm, order, false, f) order = order + (1)
					end
				end
			end)
		end

		local fgX = Instance.new("TextButton")
		fgX.Name = "ClearX"
		fgX.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 0, 24)
		fgX.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(1, 0.5)
		fgX.Position = UDim2.new(1, -20, 0, 81)
		fgX.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		fgX.BackgroundTransparency = 0.8
		fgX.BorderSizePixel = 0
		fgX.Text = "X"
		fgX.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		fgX.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
		fgX.TextSize = 16
		fgX.TextStrokeTransparency = 1
		fgX.AutoButtonColor = false
		fgX.ZIndex = 5
		fgX.Visible = true
		fgX:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
		fgX:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
		Instance.new("UICorner", fgX).CornerRadius = UDim.new(0, 5)
		fgX.Parent = fgCard
		fgX.MouseEnter:Connect(function() fgX.TextTransparency = 0.35 end)
		fgX.MouseLeave:Connect(function() fgX.TextTransparency = 0 end)
		fgX.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			fgBox.Text = ""
			fgRun() -- back to the "nobody typed" state
		end)
		fgBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			fgX.Visible = true
		end)

		fgRun()
		fgBox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			local snap = fgBox.Text
			task.delay(0.35, function()
				if fgBox.Parent and fgBox.Text == snap then fgRun() end
			end)
		end)
		fgBox.FocusLost:Connect(function() fgRun() end)
	end

	HK.light.card, HK.light.toggle, HK.light.knob = createToggleCard(4, "💡", 18, "LET THERE BE LIGHT", "Lights up dark places")

		local valueBox = Instance.new("TextLabel")

		local function setLevel(v, silent)

			valueBox.Text = string.format("%.1f", v)
			if lt.refresh then lt.refresh() end
			if not silent then Settings.save() end
		end
		lt.setLevel = setLevel
		setLevel(2, true)

		local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
		hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, -116, 0, 28)
		hit.Position = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 65)
		hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		hit.Text = ""
		hit.AutoButtonColor = false
		hit.ZIndex = 3
		hit.Parent = lt.card

		local dragging = false
		local function fromX(x)
			local a = math.clamp((x - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / math.max(track.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1)
			setLevel(LMIN + a * (LMAX - LMIN), true)
		end
		hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging = true
				fromX(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				fromX(input.Position.X)
			end
		end)
		UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				dragging = false
				Settings.save()
			end
		end)

		addHotkeySection(lt.card, lt.toggle, "💡", lt, 100)
	end

	for _, ch in ipairs(QOLContent:GetChildren()) do

	HK.infzoom.card, HK.infzoom.toggle, HK.infzoom.knob = createToggleCard(5, "🔭", 18, "Inf Zoom", "Unlimited camera zoom")
	HK.infzoom.card.Parent = QOLContent
	do
		local iz = HK.infzoom
		iz.mode = "normal"
		local card = iz.card

		local mLine = Instance.new("Frame")
		mLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
		mLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 58)
		mLine.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER
		mLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
		mLine.Parent = card
		addFadeLine(mLine, 0)

		local mLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		mLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 30)
		mLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 66)
		mLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		mLabel.Text = "Mode"
		mLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 196)
		mLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		mLabel.TextSize = 17
		mLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		mLabel.Parent = card

		local seg = Instance.new("Frame")
		seg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 30)
		seg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 66)
		seg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		seg.BorderSizePixel = 0
		seg.Parent = card

		local modeBtns = {}
		local function mkMode(id, text, xScale, xOff, wScale)
			local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
			b.Size = UDim2.new(wScale, -3, 1, 0)
			b.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, xOff, 0, 0)
			b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103)
			b.Text = text
			b.TextColor3 = CREAM
			b.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
			b.TextSize = 16
			b.AutoButtonColor = false
			b.Parent = seg
			addCorner(b, 6)
			addStroke(b, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
			addHover(b, Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103), Color3.fromRGB(66, 122, 148))

			-- bright outline = the selected mode (kept white, ignores theme / transparency)
			local sel = Instance.new("Frame")
			sel.Name = "SelectedRing"
			sel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			sel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			sel.BorderSizePixel = 0
			sel.ZIndex = b.ZIndex + 2
			sel.Parent = b
			addCorner(sel, 6)
			local ring = addStroke(sel, WHITE, 2, 1)
			mark(sel)
			mark(ring)

			local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
			dot.Name = "SelectedDot"
			dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7)
			dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)
			dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0.5, 0)
			dot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(46, 210, 90)
			dot.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
			dot.ZIndex = b.ZIndex + 2
			dot.Parent = b
			addCorner(dot, 4)
			mark(dot)

			modeBtns[id] = {btn = b, ring = ring, dot = dot}
			b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if iz.setMode then iz.setMode(id) end
			end)
		end
		mkMode("normal", "Normal", 0, 0, 0.4)   -- shorter word = narrower button
		mkMode("walls", "Through Walls", 0.4, 3, 0.6)   -- wider so the dot has room next to the text

		iz.refreshMode = function()
			for id, m in pairs(modeBtns) do
				local on = (id == iz.mode)
				tween(m.ring, 0.18, {Transparency = on and 0 or 1})
				tween(m.dot, 0.18, {BackgroundTransparency = on and 0 or 1})
			end
		end
		iz.refreshMode()
	end
	addHotkeySection(HK.infzoom.card, HK.infzoom.toggle, "🔭", HK.infzoom, 100)

	-- ---------- NO FALL DAMAGE (sits right under Inf Zoom) ----------
	for _, ch in ipairs(QOLContent:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.LayoutOrder >= 6 and ch.LayoutOrder < 20 then ch.LayoutOrder = ch.LayoutOrder + 1 end
	end
	HK.nofall.card, HK.nofall.toggle, HK.nofall.knob = createToggleCard(6, "🪂", 18, "NoFallDamage", "No fall damage from any height")
	HK.nofall.card.Parent = QOLContent
	addHotkeySection(HK.nofall.card, HK.nofall.toggle, "🪂", HK.nofall)

	HK.infjump.card, HK.infjump.toggle, HK.infjump.knob = createToggleCard(7, "🦘", 18, "Inf Jump", "Jump unlimited times")
	HK.infjump.card.Parent = QOLContent
	do

		addFadeLine(mLine, 0)

		local mLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		mLabel.Size = UDim2.new(0, 44, 0, 30)
		mLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 66)
		mLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		mLabel.Text = "Mode"
		mLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 196)
		mLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		mLabel.TextSize = 17
		mLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		mLabel.Parent = card

		local seg = Instance.new("Frame")
		seg.Size = UDim2.new(1, -72, 0, 30)
		seg.Position = UDim2.new(0, 58, 0, 66)
		seg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		seg.BorderSizePixel = 0
		seg.Parent = card

		local modeBtns = {}
		local function mkMode(id, text, xScale, xOff)
			local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
			b.Size = UDim2.new(0.5, -3, 1, 0)
			b.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, xOff, 0, 0)
			b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103)
			b.Text = text
			b.TextColor3 = CREAM

			b.Parent = seg
			addCorner(b, 6)
			addStroke(b, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)

			-- bright outline = the selected mode (kept white, ignores theme / transparency)
			local sel = Instance.new("Frame")
			sel.Name = "SelectedRing"
			sel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
			sel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			sel.BorderSizePixel = 0
			sel.ZIndex = b.ZIndex + 2

			mark(sel)

			local dot = Instance.new("Frame")

			dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 7, 0, 7)
			dot.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0.5)

xpcall(function()
	for _, c in ipairs(HomePage:GetChildren()) do
		if c:IsA("Frame") and c.Size.Y.Offset == 60 and c.LayoutOrder >= 1 and c.LayoutOrder <= 6 then
			for _, l in ipairs(c:GetChildren()) do
				if l:IsA("TextLabel") and l.Position.X.Offset == 76 then
					if l.Position.Y.Offset <= 20 then
						l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 7)
					else
						l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 29)
						l.Size = UDim2.new(l.Size.X.Scale, l.Size.X.Offset, 0, 28)
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end
end, function(e) warn("[DannyGui] home layout: " .. tostring(e)) end)

-- scrollable FEATURES list (canvas is only as tall as the cards, so it scrolls just enough)
xpcall(function()
	local TOP = 158
	local feat = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	feat.Name = "HomeFeatures"
	feat.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, TOP)
	feat.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, -TOP)
	feat.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	feat.BorderSizePixel = 0
	feat.ScrollBarThickness = 3
	feat.ScrollBarImageColor3 = SILVER
	feat.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
	feat.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	feat.Parent = HomePage
	local bottom = 0
	for _, c in ipairs(HomePage:GetChildren()) do
		if c:IsA("Frame") and c.Size.Y.Offset == 60 and c.LayoutOrder >= 1 and c.LayoutOrder <= 6 then
			local y = c.Position.Y.Offset - TOP
			c.Position = UDim2.new(c.Position.X.Scale, c.Position.X.Offset, 0, y)
			c.Parent = feat
			bottom = math.max(bottom, y + 60)
		end
	end
	feat.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, bottom + 10)
end, function(e) warn("[DannyGui] home scroll: " .. tostring(e)) end)

refreshGuiLook = function() end

	-- ---------- COLOR WHEEL SECTION ----------
	local card = createCard(432, 2, settingsScroll, true)

	local help = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	help.Size = UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 34)
	help.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 8)
	help.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	help.Text = "Drag the circle to pick a color, then fine-tune Saturation and Brightness below."
	help.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 205, 214)
	help.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	help.TextSize = 15
	help.TextWrapped = true
	help.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	help.Parent = card

	-- A 360-step hue ring. Each segment is colored using HSV; the selected
	-- hue is converted directly to Color3, so the preview and applied RGB match.
	local wheelSize = 184
	local wheel = Instance.new("Frame")
	wheel.Name = "HueWheel"
	wheel.Size = UDim2.new(0, wheelSize, 0, wheelSize)
	wheel.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, -wheelSize/2, 0, 44)
	wheel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	wheel.ClipsDescendants = false
	wheel.Active = true
	wheel.Parent = card

	local center = wheelSize / 2
	local ringRadius = 77
	for degree = 0, 359, 3 do
		local segment = Instance.new("Frame")
		segment.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		local angle = math.rad(degree)
		segment.Position = UDim2.new(0, center + math.cos(angle) * ringRadius, 0, center + math.sin(angle) * ringRadius)
		segment.Size = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 8)
		segment.Rotation = degree
		segment.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromHSV(degree / 360, 1, 1)
		segment.BorderSizePixel = 0
		segment.ZIndex = 2
		segment.Parent = wheel
		addCorner(segment, 4)
	end

	local selectedHue = 0.53
	local selectedColor = Color3.fromHSV(selectedHue, 1, 1)
	local selectedSat, selectedVal = 1, 1
	local selector = Instance.new("Frame")
	selector.Name = "HueSelector"
	selector.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
	selector.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
	selector.BackgroundColor3 = selectedColor
	selector.BorderSizePixel = 0
	selector.ZIndex = 5
	selector.Parent = wheel
	addCorner(selector, 9)
	addStroke(selector, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), 2, 0)

	local preview = Instance.new("Frame")
	preview.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28)
	preview.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 318)
	preview.BackgroundColor3 = selectedColor
	preview.BorderSizePixel = 0
	preview.Parent = card
	preview:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
	addCorner(preview, 5)
	addStroke(preview, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.1)

	local rgbLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	rgbLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 20)
	rgbLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 350)

	rgbLabel.TextSize = 14
	rgbLabel.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	rgbLabel.Parent = card

	local apply = Instance.new("TextButton")
	apply.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 32)
	apply.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 372)
	apply.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(35, 57, 70)

	apply.TextSize = 17

	apply:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)

	-- ---------- SATURATION + BRIGHTNESS SLIDERS ----------
	local function makeColorSlider(y)
		local lbl = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		lbl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 16)
		lbl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y)
		lbl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		lbl.TextColor3 = CREAM
		lbl.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
		lbl.TextSize = 14
		lbl.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		lbl.Parent = card

		local track = Instance.new("Frame")
		track.Size = UDim2.new(1, -40, 0, 10)
		track.Position = UDim2.new(0, 20, 0, y + 28)
		track.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		track.BorderSizePixel = 0
		track.Parent = card
		track:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
		addCorner(track, 5)
		addStroke(track, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.2)
		local grad = Instance.new("UIGradient")
		grad:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
		grad.Parent = track

		local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
		knob.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
		knob.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0.5, 0)
		knob.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(245, 250, 255)
		knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
		knob.ZIndex = 3
		knob.Parent = track
		addCorner(knob, 9)
		addStroke(knob, Color3.fromRGB(30, 30, 34), 2, 0)

		local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
		hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 20, 0, 26)
		hit.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
		hit.Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0.5, 0)
		hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		hit.Text = ""
		hit.AutoButtonColor = false
		hit.ZIndex = 4
		hit.Parent = track
		return lbl, track, grad, knob, hit
	end

	local satLabel, satTrack, satGrad, satKnob, satHit = makeColorSlider(232)
	local valLabel, valTrack, valGrad, valKnob, valHit = makeColorSlider(272)

	local function refreshColor()
		selectedColor = Color3.fromHSV(selectedHue, selectedSat, selectedVal)
		local angle = selectedHue * math.pi * 2
		selector.Position = UDim2.new(0, center + math.cos(angle) * ringRadius, 0, center + math.sin(angle) * ringRadius)
		selector.BackgroundColor3 = selectedColor
		preview.BackgroundColor3 = selectedColor
		satGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromHSV(selectedHue, 0, selectedVal), Color3.fromHSV(selectedHue, 1, selectedVal))
		valGrad.Color = ColorSequence.new(Color3.fromHSV(selectedHue, selectedSat, 0), Color3.fromHSV(selectedHue, selectedSat, 1))
		satKnob.Position = UDim2.new(selectedSat, 0, 0.5, 0)
		valKnob.Position = UDim2.new(selectedVal, 0, 0.5, 0)
		satLabel.Text = string.format("Saturation: %d%%   (0 = white / gray)", math.floor(selectedSat * 100 + 0.5))
		valLabel.Text = string.format("Brightness: %d%%   (0 = black)", math.floor(selectedVal * 100 + 0.5))
		local r = math.floor(selectedColor.R * 255 + 0.5)
		local g = math.floor(selectedColor.G * 255 + 0.5)
		local b = math.floor(selectedColor.B * 255 + 0.5)
		rgbLabel.Text = string.format("RGB: %d, %d, %d    Hex: #%02X%02X%02X", r, g, b, r, g, b)
	end

	local function refreshHue(hue)
		selectedHue = hue % 1
		refreshColor()
	end
	refreshHue(selectedHue)

	local function bindColorSlider(track, hit, onValue)
		local dragging = false
		local function update(position)
			local w = track.AbsoluteSize.X
			if w <= 0 then return end
			onValue(math.clamp((position.X - track.AbsolutePosition.X) / w, 0, 1))
		end
		hit.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
			if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
				dragging = true
				update(input.Position)
			end
		end)
		UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
			if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				update(input.Position)
			end
		end)
		UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
			if dragging and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
				dragging = false
			end
		end)
	end
	bindColorSlider(satTrack, satHit, function(v) selectedSat = v refreshColor() end)
	bindColorSlider(valTrack, valHit, function(v) selectedVal = v refreshColor() end)

	local draggingHue = false
	local function updateFromPointer(position)
		local localX = position.X - wheel.AbsolutePosition.X - center
		local localY = position.Y - wheel.AbsolutePosition.Y - center
		if math.abs(localX) + math.abs(localY) < 3 then return end
		refreshHue(math.atan2(localY, localX) / (math.pi * 2))
	end
	wheel.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch then
			draggingHue = true
			updateFromPointer(input.Position)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputChanged:Connect(function(input)
		if draggingHue and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseMovement or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			updateFromPointer(input.Position)
		end
	end)
	UserInputService.InputEnded:Connect(function(input)
		if draggingHue and (input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.MouseButton1 or input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Touch) then
			draggingHue = false
		end
	end)

	-- Cache the original UI colors once, so changing green -> pink (or any
	-- other hue) always starts from the same original palette instead of
	-- trying to recolor already-recolored values.
	local originalColors = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local themeFile = "DannyGui_Theme.json"
	local transparentOn = true   -- default look: transparent GUI + white text
	local alpha = 0.75           -- how transparent (0 = solid, 1 = fully clear)
	local function getOriginal(obj, property)
		originalColors[obj] = originalColors[obj] or {}
		if originalColors[obj][property] == nil then
			originalColors[obj][property] = obj[property]
		end
		return originalColors[obj][property]
	end

	-- Restore a saved hue before showing the picker.
	pcall(function()
		do
			local saved = Settings.readJson(themeFile)
			if type(saved) == "table" and type(saved.hue) == "number" then
				selectedHue = saved.hue % 1
				selectedColor = Color3.fromHSV(selectedHue, 1, 1)
			end
			if type(saved) == "table" then
				if type(saved.sat) == "number" then selectedSat = math.clamp(saved.sat, 0, 1) end
				if type(saved.val) == "number" then selectedVal = math.clamp(saved.val, 0, 1) end
				if type(saved.transparent) == "boolean" then transparentOn = saved.transparent end
				if saved.ver == 2 and type(saved.alpha) == "number" then alpha = math.clamp(saved.alpha, 0, 1) end
			end
		end
	end)
	refreshHue(selectedHue)

	-- The color that is actually APPLIED (what gets saved). Moving the picker without
	-- pressing Apply never overwrites the saved color.
	local applied = {h = selectedHue, s = selectedSat, v = selectedVal}
	local function writeTheme()
		pcall(function()
			local c = Color3.fromHSV(applied.h, applied.s, applied.v)
			Settings.writeJson(themeFile, {
				hue = applied.h, sat = applied.s, val = applied.v,
				r = math.floor(c.R * 255 + 0.5), g = math.floor(c.G * 255 + 0.5), b = math.floor(c.B * 255 + 0.5),
				transparent = transparentOn, alpha = alpha, ver = 2,
			})
		end)
	end

	-- Cached GUI descendant list + cached "skip" flags (the old code walked every ancestor of every
	-- GUI object on every theme / transparency change, which is what made the slider and startup lag).
	local guiList, guiListDirty = {}, true
	ScreenGui.DescendantAdded:Connect(function() guiListDirty = true end)
	ScreenGui.DescendantRemoving:Connect(function() guiListDirty = true end)
	local function getGuiList()
		if guiListDirty then
			guiList = ScreenGui:GetDescendants()
			guiListDirty = false
		end
		return guiList
	end
	local themeSkipCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local function themeSkip(obj)
		local c = themeSkipCache[obj]
		if c ~= nil then return c end
		local r = false
		if obj == ScreenGui then
			r = false
		elseif obj == wheel or obj.Name == "OnOffToggle" or obj:IsA("BillboardGui") then
			r = true
		else
			local p = obj.Parent
			r = (p ~= nil and p ~= ScreenGui) and themeSkip(p) or false
		end
		themeSkipCache[obj] = r
		return r
	end

	local function applyTheme(newColor, persist, onlyList)
		local styleHook = Settings.StyleHook
		if styleHook and styleHook.on then return styleHook.apply(onlyList) end -- a GUI Style (not Default) is active
		local hue = selectedHue
		local _, userS, userV = newColor:ToHSV()
		-- "lift" pushes panels toward white when the picked color is light / unsaturated
		local lift = ((1 - userS) ^ 2) * userV
		local darkText = lift > 0.55
		local DARK_TEXT = Color3.fromRGB(18, 18, 22)
		local function themed(c, kind)
			local _, cs, cv = c:ToHSV()
			local result = c
			if cs > 0.12 then
				local nv
				if kind == "text" then
					nv = math.max(cv * userV, cv * 0.6)      -- keep text readable on dark themes
				elseif kind == "stroke" then
					nv = math.max(cv * userV, cv * 0.2)
				else
					nv = cv * userV
				end
				result = Color3.fromHSV(hue, math.clamp(cs * userS, 0, 1), nv)
			elseif kind == "bg" and cv < 0.52 then
				-- Give neutral dark panels/cards a visible tint too.
				local tint = Color3.fromHSV(hue, 0.58 * userS, math.max(cv, 0.12) * userV)
				result = c:Lerp(tint, 0.78)
			end
			if kind == "bg" and lift > 0 then
				result = result:Lerp(Color3.new(1, 1, 1), lift)
			end
			return result
		end

		for _, obj in ipairs(onlyList or getGuiList()) do
			if not themeSkip(obj) and obj:GetAttribute("KeepColor") ~= true then
				if obj:IsA("UIGradient") then
					local original = getOriginal(obj, "Color")
					local points = {}
					for _, point in ipairs(original.Keypoints) do
						table.insert(points, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(point.Time, themed(point.Value, "bg")))
					end
					obj.Color = ColorSequence.new(points)
				elseif obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
					obj.Color = themed(getOriginal(obj, "Color"), "stroke")
				elseif obj:IsA("GuiObject") then
					obj.BackgroundColor3 = themed(getOriginal(obj, "BackgroundColor3"), "bg")
					if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
						if obj:GetAttribute("KeepText") then
							-- locked text color (e.g. search names stay pure white)
						else
							local originalText = getOriginal(obj, "TextColor3")
							local _, sat = originalText:ToHSV()
							if darkText then
								obj.TextColor3 = DARK_TEXT
							else
								obj.TextColor3 = sat > 0.12 and themed(originalText, "text") or originalText
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end

		if onlyList then return end -- subtree pass (toast popups): skip the whole-GUI extras below

		-- The right-side DANNY tab uses mostly neutral colors, so explicitly
		-- sync its border, arrow, and vertical lettering to the selected accent.
		local sideTab = ScreenGui:FindFirstChild("Tab", true)
		if sideTab and sideTab:IsA("GuiObject") then
			local tabStroke = sideTab:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			local accentText = Color3.fromHSV(hue, userS, math.max(userV, 0.6))
			local accentStroke = Color3.fromHSV(hue, userS, math.max(userV, 0.25))
			if tabStroke then tabStroke.Color = accentStroke end
			local tabLetters = sideTab:FindFirstChild("TabLetters", true)
			if tabLetters and tabLetters:IsA("TextLabel") then tabLetters.TextColor3 = accentText end
			local tabArrow = sideTab:FindFirstChild("TabArrow", true)
			if tabArrow and tabArrow:IsA("TextLabel") then tabArrow.TextColor3 = accentText end
			sideTab.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(25, 25, 29):Lerp(newColor, 0.22)
		end

		preview.BackgroundColor3 = newColor
		apply.BackgroundColor3 = newColor:Lerp(Color3.new(0, 0, 0), 0.58)
		applyStroke.Color = Color3.fromHSV(hue, userS, math.max(userV, 0.25))
		status.TextColor3 = darkText and DARK_TEXT or Color3.fromHSV(hue, userS, math.max(userV, 0.6))
		status.Text = string.format("Applied RGB(%d, %d, %d).",
			math.floor(newColor.R * 255 + 0.5),
			math.floor(newColor.G * 255 + 0.5),
			math.floor(newColor.B * 255 + 0.5))
		if persist then
			writeTheme()
			Settings.save()
		end
	end

	-- ---------- TRANSPARENT / DEFAULT LOOK ----------
	local originalFade = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local fadeSkipCache = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local function skipFade(obj)
		local c = fadeSkipCache[obj]
		if c ~= nil then return c end
		local r = false
		if obj == ScreenGui then
			r = false
		elseif obj == wheel or obj.Name == "OnOffToggle" or obj:IsA("BillboardGui")
			or obj:GetAttribute("NoFade") or obj:GetAttribute("KeepColor") then
			r = true
		else
			local p = obj.Parent
			r = (p ~= nil and p ~= ScreenGui) and skipFade(p) or false
		end
		fadeSkipCache[obj] = r
		return r
	end
	local function faded(original)
		return original + (1 - original) * alpha
	end

	local function applyTransparency(onlyList)
		local styleHook = Settings.StyleHook
		if styleHook and styleHook.on then return end -- GUI Styles handle their own transparency
		for _, obj in ipairs(onlyList or getGuiList()) do
			if not skipFade(obj) then
				if obj:IsA("GuiObject") then
					local o = originalFade[obj]
					if not o then
						o = {bg = obj.BackgroundTransparency}
						if obj:IsA("ImageLabel") or obj:IsA("ImageButton") then
							o.img = obj.ImageTransparency
						end
						originalFade[obj] = o
					end
					obj.BackgroundTransparency = transparentOn and faded(o.bg) or o.bg
					if o.img then
						obj.ImageTransparency = transparentOn and faded(o.img) or o.img
					end
					if obj:IsA("TextLabel") or obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextBox") then
						if obj:GetAttribute("KeepText") then
							-- leave locked colors alone (search text stays white)
						else
							getOriginal(obj, "TextColor3")
							if transparentOn then obj.TextColor3 = WHITE end
						end
					end
				elseif obj:IsA("UIStroke") then
					local o = originalFade[obj]
					if not o then
						o = {stroke = obj.Transparency}
						originalFade[obj] = o
					end
					obj.Transparency = transparentOn and faded(o.stroke) or o.stroke
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function saveLook()
		writeTheme()
	end

	local function refreshControls()
		checkMark.Visible = transparentOn
		defaultBoxStroke.Color = transparentOn and Color3.fromRGB(46, 210, 90) or SILVER_DIM
		alphaLabel.Text = string.format("Transparency: %d%%", math.floor(alpha * 100 + 0.5))
		sliderFill.Size = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 1, 0)
		sliderKnob.Position = UDim2.new(alpha, 0, 0.5, 0)
	end

	local function setTransparentMode(state, persist)
		transparentOn = state
		if not state then
			-- put the normal (themed) colors back, then restore the original transparencies
			applyTheme(selectedColor, false)
		end
		applyTransparency()
		refreshControls()
		if persist then
			saveLook()
			Settings.save()
		end
	end

	-- Whole-GUI refresh (also called once at startup after every part of the GUI exists)
	refreshGuiLook = function()
		refreshControls()
		applyTheme(Color3.fromHSV(applied.h, applied.s, applied.v), false)
		applyTransparency()
	end

	-- Apply the CURRENT color / transparency to just one new piece of GUI (used by the hotkey popups)
	Settings.themeSubtree = function(root)
		local list = root:GetDescendants()
		table.insert(list, root)
		applyTheme(Color3.fromHSV(applied.h, applied.s, applied.v), false, list)
		applyTransparency(list)
	end

	defaultBox.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		setTransparentMode(not transparentOn, true)
	end)

	-- ====================== GUI STYLES (full themes, picked in GUI SETTINGS) ======================
	-- "Default" = your normal look (color wheel + transparency). The other styles re-skin the whole GUI
	-- (colors, fonts, corners, borders) and add their own animated decorations. Everything is built once,
	-- animated with a handful of looping tweens (paused automatically while the panel is closed), and
	-- applied in small chunks behind a quick fade so switching never stutters.
	task.wait()
	Settings.StyleHook = {on = false}
	Settings.StyleEngine = (function()
		local SH = Settings.StyleHook
		local STYLE_FILE = "DannyGui_Style.json"
		local Sty = {name = "Default", cur = nil, token = 0, busy = false, tweens = {}, tiles = {}, decorFor = nil}
		local origProps = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
		local warned = false

		local function C(r, g, b) return Color3.fromRGB(r, g, b) end
		local function font(name)
			local ok, v = pcall(function() return Enum.Font[name] end)
			if ok then return v end
			return nil
		end
		local SINE, QUAD, BACK = Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingStyle.Back
		local V2, U2, UD = Vector2.new, UDim2.new, UDim.new
		local NSK, CSK = NumberSequenceKeypoint.new, ColorSequenceKeypoint.new

		-- ---------------------------------------------------------------- style definitions
		local STYLES = {
			{name = "Default", icon = "💠", sub = "Classic Danny blue", isDefault = true,
				bgLo = C(12, 19, 28), bgHi = C(40, 68, 84), accent = C(105, 205, 245), text = C(205, 235, 250), dim = C(150, 170, 182)},
			{name = "Halloween", icon = "🎃", sub = "Spooky glowing night",
