local panelOpen = false
TabArrow.Text = "›"
local function setPanelOpen(state)
	panelOpen = state
	TabArrow.Text = state and "‹" or "›"
	if Settings.toastSlide then pcall(Settings.toastSlide, state) end
	-- performance: while the panel is slid away it is fully hidden and its shimmer is paused (zero render cost)
	if state then Panel.Visible = true end
	for _, e in ipairs(Settings.ShimmerList) do
		if e.owner == Panel then pcall(function() e.tw:Play() end) end
	end
	TweenService:Create(Main, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
		Position = UDim2.new(0, state and OPEN_X or CLOSED_X, 0.5, 0),
	}):Play()
	if not state then
		task.delay(0.5, function()
			if panelOpen then return end
			Panel.Visible = false
			for _, e in ipairs(Settings.ShimmerList) do
				if e.owner == Panel then pcall(function() e.tw:Pause() end) end
			end
		end)
	end
end
Tab.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setPanelOpen(not panelOpen) end)

TweenService:Create(Main, TweenInfo.new(0.6, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
	Position = UDim2.new(0, CLOSED_X, 0.5, 0),
}):Play()
task.delay(0.7, function()
	if panelOpen then return end
	Panel.Visible = false
	for _, e in ipairs(Settings.ShimmerList) do
		if e.owner == Panel then pcall(function() e.tw:Pause() end) end
	end
end)

local StatusPulse = TweenService:Create(
	StatusDot,
	TweenInfo.new(0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true),
	{BackgroundTransparency = 0.6}
)

local function setStatus(active)
	MenuSub.Text = active and "Running" or "Stopped"
	MenuSub.TextColor3 = active and Color3.fromRGB(92, 178, 116) or Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
	if active then
		StatusDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(40, 200, 90)
		StatusPulse:Play()
	else
		StatusPulse:Cancel()
		StatusDot.BackgroundTransparency = 0
		StatusDot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(176, 72, 72)
	end
end

local timerRun = 0

local function formatTime(sec)
	local h = math.floor(sec / 3600)
	local m = math.floor((sec % 3600) / 60)
	local sc = sec % 60
	if h > 0 then
		return string.format("%d:%02d:%02d", h, m, sc)
	end
	return string.format("%02d:%02d", m, sc)
end

local function setTimer(active)
	timerRun = timerRun + (1)
	local id = timerRun
	if active then
		FarmTimer.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(70, 210, 110)
		local startTime = os.clock()
		task.spawn(function()
			local lastShown = -1
			while timerRun == id and FarmTimer.Parent do
				local elapsed = math.floor(os.clock() - startTime)
				if elapsed ~= lastShown then
					lastShown = elapsed
					FarmTimer.Text = formatTime(elapsed)
				end
				task.wait(0.5)
			end
		end)
	else
		FarmTimer.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(160, 160, 166)
		FarmTimer.Text = "00:00"
	end
end

local function setToggle(toggle, knob, state)
	local color = state and Color3.fromRGB(46, 190, 85) or Color3.fromRGB(88, 88, 94)
	tween(toggle, 0.25, {BackgroundColor3 = color})
	tween(knob, 0.25, {Position = state and UDim2.new(1, -25, 0.5, -11) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -11)})
	local stroke = toggle:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
	if stroke then
		tween(stroke, 0.25, {Color = color})
	end
	local glowBar = toggle.Parent and toggle.Parent:FindFirstChild("AccentBar")
	if glowBar then
		tween(glowBar, 0.25, {BackgroundTransparency = state and 0.05 or 0.7})
	end
	if toggle == MainToggle then
		setStatus(state)
		setTimer(state)
	end
end

-- ====================== CORE LOGIC ======================
local releaseF
local clearAll
local runKeyLoop
local clearEsp
local startEsp
local function __initCoreLogic()
local function releaseF()
	pcall(function()
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game)
		task.wait(0.02)
		VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game)
	end)
end

local fruitConns = {}

local function clearAll()
	if addConnection then
		addConnection:Disconnect()
		addConnection = nil
	end
	for obj, conn in pairs(fruitConns) do
		conn:Disconnect()
		fruitConns[obj] = nil
	end
	for _, hl in pairs(activeHighlights) do
		if hl and typeof(hl) ~= "boolean" then hl:Destroy() end
	end
	activeHighlights = {}
	TrackerText.Text = "Picking: None\nFound: 0 fruits"
end

-- Walks the tree in small time slices (about 4ms per frame) instead of building one giant
-- workspace:GetDescendants() table, which was the big freeze on execute.
local function sliceScan(root, visit, alive)
	local stack, top = {root}, 1
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while top > 0 do
		local inst = stack[top]
		stack[top] = nil
		top = top - (1)
		if inst.Parent then
			for _, child in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do
				visit(child)
				top = top + (1)
				stack[top] = child
			end
		end
		if os.clock() - t0 > 0.002 then -- [perf] 2ms slices
			task.wait()
			if alive and not alive() then return false end
			t0 = os.clock()
		end
	end
	return true
end

local function isFruit(obj)
	if not obj then return false end
	if trackedFruits[obj.Name] then return true end
	local current = obj
	for _ = 1, 5 do
		current = current.Parent
		if not current then break end
		if trackedFruits[current.Name] then return true end
	end
	return false
end

local function getClickPosition(obj)
	if not obj then return nil end
	if obj:IsA("BasePart") then return obj.Position end
	if obj:IsA("Model") then
		if obj.PrimaryPart then return obj.PrimaryPart.Position end
		local part = obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
		if part then return part.Position end
	end
	return nil
end

local function clickOnFruit(obj)
	if not obj or not obj.Parent then return end
	local pos = getClickPosition(obj)
	if not pos then return end
	Settings.DX.clicked[obj] = true

	local screenPos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(pos)
	if onScreen and screenPos.Z > 0 then
		VirtualInputManager:SendMouseMoveEvent(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y, game)
		task.wait(0.03)
		VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y, 0, true, game, 1)
		task.wait(0.05)
		VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(screenPos.X, screenPos.Y, 0, false, game, 1)
		task.wait(0.04)
	end
end

local updateTracker
local trackerQueued = false
local function queueTracker()
	if trackerQueued then return end
	trackerQueued = true
	task.delay(0.1, function()
		trackerQueued = false
		if MacroActive then updateTracker() end
	end)
end

local function addFruit(obj)
	if not obj then return false end
	if not (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model")) then return false end
	if not isFruit(obj) then return false end
	if activeHighlights[obj] then return false end

	local anc = obj.Parent
	for _ = 1, 5 do
		if not anc then break end
		if activeHighlights[anc] then return false end
		anc = anc.Parent
	end

	local character = LocalPlayer.Character
	local root = character and character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then return false end

	local pos = getClickPosition(obj)
	if not pos then return false end
	if (root.Position - pos).Magnitude > 55 then return false end

	if ESPEnabled then
		local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
		hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		hl.FillTransparency = 0.4
		styleFruitHighlight(hl, obj)
		hl.Adornee = obj
		hl.Parent = ScreenGui
		activeHighlights[obj] = hl
	else
		activeHighlights[obj] = true
	end

	fruitConns[obj] = obj.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
		if not obj:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
			if MacroActive and Settings.DX.onPicked and Settings.DX.clicked[obj] then
				Settings.DX.clicked[obj] = nil
				pcall(Settings.DX.onPicked, obj.Name)
			end
			if activeHighlights[obj] and typeof(activeHighlights[obj]) ~= "boolean" then
				activeHighlights[obj]:Destroy()
			end
			activeHighlights[obj] = nil
			if fruitConns[obj] then
				fruitConns[obj]:Disconnect()
				fruitConns[obj] = nil
			end
			queueTracker()
		end
	end)
	return true
end

updateTracker = function()
	local counts, names = {}, {}
	local count = 0
	for obj, _ in pairs(activeHighlights) do
		if obj and obj.Parent then
			count = count + (1)
			local name = obj.Name
			if not counts[name] then
				counts[name] = 0
				table.insert(names, name)
			end
			counts[name] = counts[name] + (1)
		end
	end

	if count == 0 then
		TrackerText.Text = "Picking: None\nFound: 0 fruits"
		TrackerText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(170, 170, 176)
	else
		table.sort(names, function(a, b)
			if counts[a] ~= counts[b] then return counts[a] > counts[b] end
			return a < b
		end)
		local parts = {}
		for _, name in ipairs(names) do
			parts[#parts + 1] = name .. " x" .. counts[name]
		end
		TrackerText.Text = "Picking: " .. table.concat(parts, ", ") .. "\nFound: " .. count .. (count == 1 and " fruit" or " fruits")
		TrackerText.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(240, 226, 200)
	end
end

local function quickScan()
	if sliceScan(workspace, addFruit, function() return MacroActive end) then
		updateTracker()
	end
end

-- ====================== FAILSAFE LOGIC ======================
local failsafeTriggered = false

local function getRootPart(character)
	if not character then return nil end
	return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or character.PrimaryPart
end

local function findNearbyPlayer()
	local myRoot = getRootPart(LocalPlayer.Character)
	if not myRoot then return nil end
	local myPos = myRoot.Position

	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player ~= LocalPlayer then
			local otherRoot = getRootPart(player.Character)
			if otherRoot and (otherRoot.Position - myPos).Magnitude <= FailsafeRange then
				return player
			end
		end
	end
	return nil
end

local function serverHop()
	local placeId = game.PlaceId
	local queue = {}

	local ok, body = pcall(function()
		return game:HttpGet("https://games.roblox.com/v1/games/" .. placeId .. "/servers/Public?sortOrder=Asc&limit=100")
	end)
	if ok and body then
		local decoded
		pcall(function() decoded = HttpService:JSONDecode(body) end)
		if decoded and decoded.data then
			for _, server in ipairs(decoded.data) do
				if server.id ~= game.JobId and server.playing and server.maxPlayers and server.playing < server.maxPlayers - 1 then
					table.insert(queue, server.id)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	for i = #queue, 2, -1 do
		local j = math.random(1, i)
		queue[i], queue[j] = queue[j], queue[i]
	end

	local idx = 0
	local failConn
	local function tryNext()
		idx = idx + (1)
		if idx <= #queue then
			pcall(function() TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, queue[idx], LocalPlayer) end)
		elseif idx == #queue + 1 then
			pcall(function() TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, LocalPlayer) end)
		else
			if failConn then failConn:Disconnect() end
		end
	end

	failConn = TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed:Connect(function(player)
		if player == LocalPlayer then
			task.wait(1)
			tryNext()
		end
	end)
	tryNext()
end
Settings.DX.serverHop = serverHop

local function isReallyVisible(gui)
	local cur = gui
	while cur and cur ~= CoreGui and cur ~= PlayerGui do
		if cur:IsA("GuiObject") and not cur.Visible then return false end
		if cur:IsA("ScreenGui") and not cur.Enabled then return false end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
	return true
end

local function guiCenter(gui)
	local pos = gui.AbsolutePosition + gui.AbsoluteSize / 2
	local sg = gui:FindFirstAncestorWhichIsA("ScreenGui")
	if sg and not sg.IgnoreGuiInset then
		pos = pos + GuiService:GetGuiInset()
	end
	return pos
end

local function normalizeText(t)
	t = tostring(t or "")
	t = t:gsub("^%s+", "")
	t = t:gsub("%s+$", "")
	return t:lower()
end

local function getTargetButton(textObj)
	if textObj:IsA("GuiButton") then return textObj end
	local cur = textObj.Parent
	for _ = 1, 3 do
		if not cur or not cur:IsA("GuiObject") then break end
		if cur:IsA("GuiButton") and cur.AbsoluteSize.X <= 220 and cur.AbsoluteSize.Y <= 110 then
			return cur
		end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
	return textObj
end

local function findGameMenuButton()
	local best, bestScore
	for _, root in ipairs({PlayerGui, CoreGui}) do
		pcall(function()
			for _, obj in ipairs(root:GetDescendants()) do
				if (obj:IsA("TextButton") or obj:IsA("TextLabel")) and not obj:IsDescendantOf(ScreenGui) then
					if normalizeText(obj.Text) == "menu" and obj.AbsoluteSize.X > 0 and isReallyVisible(obj) then
						local pos = obj.AbsolutePosition
						if pos.Y < 150 and pos.X < 700 then
							local score = pos.X + pos.Y
							if not best or score < bestScore then
								best, bestScore = obj, score
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end)
	end
	if best then return getTargetButton(best) end
	return nil
end

local function clickAt(pos)
	pcall(function()
		VirtualInputManager:SendMouseMoveEvent(pos.X, pos.Y, game)
		task.wait(0.05)
		VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(pos.X, pos.Y, 0, true, game, 1)
		task.wait(0.06)
		VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(pos.X, pos.Y, 0, false, game, 1)
	end)
end

local function isHovering(btn)
	local ok, result = pcall(function()
		local st = btn.GuiState
		return st == Enum.GuiState.Hover or st == Enum.GuiState.Press
	end)
	if ok then return result end
	return nil
end

local function fireButton(btn)
	if not btn:IsA("GuiButton") then return false end
	for _, name in ipairs({"MouseButton1Click", "Activated", "MouseButton1Down"}) do
		local signal = btn[name]
		if getconnections then
			local ok, conns = pcall(getconnections, signal)
			if ok and conns and #conns > 0 then
				for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
					pcall(function() c:Fire() end)
				end
				return true
			end
		elseif firesignal then
			if pcall(firesignal, signal) then return true end
		end
	end
	return false
end

local function addCandidate(list, pos)
	for _, existing in ipairs(list) do
		if (existing - pos).Magnitude < 1 then return end
	end
	table.insert(list, pos)
end

local function clickMenuButton()
	local btn = findGameMenuButton()
	if not btn then
		clickAt(MenuButtonPos)
		return
	end

	local raw = btn.AbsolutePosition + btn.AbsoluteSize / 2
	local candidates = {}
	addCandidate(candidates, guiCenter(btn))
	addCandidate(candidates, raw)
	addCandidate(candidates, raw + GuiService:GetGuiInset())

	local chosen, canVerify = nil, false
	for _, pos in ipairs(candidates) do
		pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendMouseMoveEvent(pos.X, pos.Y, game) end)
		task.wait(0.12)
		local hovering = isHovering(btn)
		if hovering == nil then break end
		canVerify = true
		if hovering then
			chosen = pos
			break
		end
	end

	if chosen then
		clickAt(chosen)
	elseif not canVerify then
		clickAt(candidates[1])
	else
		if not fireButton(btn) then
			clickAt(candidates[1])
		end
	end
end

local function triggerFailsafe(player, reason)
	if failsafeTriggered then return end
	failsafeTriggered = true

	local name = player and player.Name or "?"
	local mode = FailsafeMode
	local why = reason or (name .. " got within " .. FailsafeRange .. " studs")

	MacroActive = false
	setToggle(MainToggle, MainKnob, false)
	releaseF()
	clearAll()
	-- Write macro=false to disk NOW so a kick/hop/rejoin never auto-restarts farm
	pcall(function()
		if Settings.save then Settings.save() end
	end)

	local actionText = (mode == "kick" and "Kicking you") or (mode == "hop" and "Server hopping") or "Clicking Menu"
	TrackerText.Text = "FAILSAFE: " .. why .. "\n" .. actionText
	TrackerText.TextColor3 = RED_BRIGHT

	if mode == "kick" then
		task.wait(0.2)
		LocalPlayer:Kick("Kicked because " .. why)
	elseif mode == "hop" then
		serverHop()
	else
		clickMenuButton()
	end
end

Settings.DX.triggerFailsafe = triggerFailsafe

local function startAntiPlayer(id)
	failsafeTriggered = false
	task.spawn(function()
		while MacroActive and runId == id do
			local nearby = nil
			if not Settings.DX.failsafePause then nearby = findNearbyPlayer() end
			if nearby then
				triggerFailsafe(nearby)
				return
			end
			task.wait(0.15)
		end
	end)
end

-- Instant scan of everything within range of the player (the only fruits addFruit accepts anyway),
-- so picking can start right away. The full workspace scan then runs in the background.
local function nearScan()
	local char = LocalPlayer.Character
	local root = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	if not root then return end
	local params = OverlapParams.new()
	params.MaxParts = 1500
	local found = false
	for _, part in ipairs(workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(root.Position, 60, params)) do
		local chain, inst = {}, part
		for _ = 1, 6 do
			if not inst or inst == workspace then break end
			chain[#chain + 1] = inst
			inst = inst.Parent
		end
		-- top-down, so a Model gets the highlight instead of each of its parts
		for i = #chain, 1, -1 do
			if addFruit(chain[i]) then found = true end
		end
	end
	if found then updateTracker() end
end

local scanBusy = false
local function bgScan()
	if scanBusy then return end
	scanBusy = true
	task.spawn(function()
		quickScan()
		scanBusy = false
	end)
end

local function startFruitSystem(id)
	clearAll()

	addConnection = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
		if not MacroActive then return end
		if not (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model")) then return end -- [perf] same filter addFruit uses, without a defer per instance
		task.defer(function()
			if addFruit(obj) then updateTracker() end
		end)
	end)

	nearScan()
	bgScan()

	task.spawn(function()
		while MacroActive and runId == id do
			while Settings.DX.sellBusy and MacroActive and runId == id do task.wait(0.2) end
			local list = {}
			for obj, _ in pairs(activeHighlights) do
				if obj and obj.Parent then
					table.insert(list, obj)
				end
			end

			if #list == 0 then
				task.wait(0.25)
			else
				for _, fruit in ipairs(list) do
					if not (MacroActive and runId == id) then break end
					if fruit and fruit.Parent then
						clickOnFruit(fruit)
						task.wait(0.6)
					end
				end
			end
			task.wait(0.05)
		end
	end)
end

local function runKeyLoop()
	runId = runId + (1)
	local myId = runId
	local function alive() return MacroActive and runId == myId end

	task.spawn(function()
		startFruitSystem(myId)
		startAntiPlayer(myId)

		while alive() do
			if Settings.DX.cycleHook then pcall(Settings.DX.cycleHook, alive) end
			if not alive() then break end
			releaseF()
			task.wait(0.05)
			if not alive() then break end

			VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, SummonKey, false, game)
			task.wait(0.06)
			VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, SummonKey, false, game)

			task.wait(0.4)
			if alive() then
				nearScan()
				bgScan()
			end
			if not alive() then break end

			VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game)

			local holdEnd = tick() + 13.5
			while tick() < holdEnd and alive() do
				VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.F, false, game)
				task.wait(0.4)
			end

			releaseF()
			if not alive() then break end
			task.wait(0.45)
		end

		releaseF()
		if runId == myId then clearAll() end
	end)
end

-- ====================== ESP (also works while the farm is off) ======================
local espOnly = {}
local espConn = nil
local espToken = 0
local espCount = 0

local function clearEsp()
	espToken = espToken + (1)
	if espConn then espConn:Disconnect() espConn = nil end
	for _, hl in pairs(espOnly) do
		if hl then hl:Destroy() end
	end
	espOnly = {}
	espCount = 0
end

local function espTry(obj)
	if espCount >= 25 or espOnly[obj] then return end
	if not trackedFruits[obj.Name] then return end
	if not (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model")) then return end
	if obj.Parent and espOnly[obj.Parent] then return end

	local root = getRootPart(LocalPlayer.Character)
	local pos = getClickPosition(obj)
	if not root or not pos or (root.Position - pos).Magnitude > 250 then return end

	local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
	hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	hl.FillTransparency = 0.4
	styleFruitHighlight(hl, obj)
	hl.Adornee = obj
	hl.Parent = ScreenGui
	espOnly[obj] = hl
	espCount = espCount + (1)

	local ac
	ac = obj.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
		if not obj:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then
			if espOnly[obj] then
				espOnly[obj]:Destroy()
				espOnly[obj] = nil
				espCount = espCount - (1)
			end
			ac:Disconnect()
		end
	end)
end

local function startEsp()
	clearEsp()
	if not ESPEnabled or MacroActive then return end
	local token = espToken

	espConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
		if espCount >= 25 or not trackedFruits[obj.Name] then return end -- [perf] cheap reject before defer (streaming adds thousands of instances)
		task.defer(function()
			if espToken == token then espTry(obj) end
		end)
	end)

	task.spawn(function()
		sliceScan(workspace, espTry, function() return espToken == token end)
	end)
end


	return {
		releaseF = releaseF,
		clearAll = clearAll,
		runKeyLoop = runKeyLoop,
		clearEsp = clearEsp,
		startEsp = startEsp,
	}
end
do local _c = __initCoreLogic()
	releaseF = _c.releaseF
	clearAll = _c.clearAll
	runKeyLoop = _c.runKeyLoop
	clearEsp = _c.clearEsp
	startEsp = _c.startEsp
end

-- Shared root helper (must be global — used by ESP + Fly)
local function getRootPart(character)
	if not character then return nil end
	return character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		or character:FindFirstChild("Torso")
		or character:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso")
		or character.PrimaryPart
end

-- ====================== PLAYER ESP (QOL) ======================
-- Live players only: outline + name + distance + health (no last-seen ghosts)
local PlayerEspEnabled = false
local playerEsp = {}
local playerEspLoop = nil
local playerEspLeaving = nil
local playerEspStreamIdx = 0
local lastKnownEspPos = {}

local function removePlayerEsp(player)
	local d = playerEsp[player]
	if d then
		if d.healthTween then d.healthTween:Cancel() end
		if d.hl then d.hl:Destroy() end
		if d.gui then d.gui:Destroy() end
		playerEsp[player] = nil
	end
end

local function playerEspColor(player)
	if player.Team then return player.TeamColor.Color end
	return CREAM
end

local function buildPlayerEsp(player, char)
	removePlayerEsp(player)
	local root = getRootPart(char)
	local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head") or root
	if not head then return end
	local color = playerEspColor(player)

	local hl = nil
	if EspOpts.outline then
		hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
		hl.FillColor = color
		hl.FillTransparency = 0.65
		hl.OutlineColor = color
		hl.OutlineTransparency = 0
		hl.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
		hl.Adornee = char
		hl.Parent = ScreenGui
	end

	local gui, nameLabel, distLabel, healthLabel, teamLabel = nil, nil, nil, nil, nil
	if EspOpts.name or EspOpts.studs or EspOpts.health or EspOpts.team then
		local rows = 0
		if EspOpts.team then rows = rows + (1)end
		if EspOpts.name then rows = rows + (1)end
		if EspOpts.studs then rows = rows + (1)end
		if EspOpts.health then rows = rows + (1)end
		gui = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
		gui.Size = UDim2.new(0, 220, 0, math.max(20, rows * 18))
		gui.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
		gui.AlwaysOnTop = true
		gui.MaxDistance = math.huge
		gui.Adornee = head
		gui:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
		gui:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
		gui:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
		gui.Parent = ScreenGui

		local y = 0
		if EspOpts.name then
			nameLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			nameLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 18)
			nameLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
			nameLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			nameLabel.Text = player.Name
			nameLabel.TextColor3 = color
			nameLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
			nameLabel.TextSize = 17
			nameLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
			nameLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
			nameLabel:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
			nameLabel:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
			nameLabel:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
			nameLabel.Parent = gui
			y = y + (18)
		end
		if EspOpts.team then
			-- leaderboard team / rank, in the team's color
			teamLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			teamLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
			teamLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
			teamLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			teamLabel.Text = player.Team and player.Team.Name or "No team"
			teamLabel.TextColor3 = player.Team and player.TeamColor.Color or Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 196)
			teamLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
			teamLabel.TextSize = 15
			teamLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
			teamLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
			teamLabel:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
			teamLabel:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
			teamLabel:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
			teamLabel.Parent = gui
			y = y + (16)
		end
		if EspOpts.studs then
			distLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			distLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
			distLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
			distLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			distLabel.Text = ""
			distLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(235, 235, 240)
			distLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
			distLabel.TextSize = 15
			distLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
			distLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
			distLabel:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
			distLabel:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
			distLabel:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
			distLabel.Parent = gui
			y = y + (16)
		end
		if EspOpts.health then
			healthLabel = Instance.new("TextLabel")
			healthLabel.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 16)
			healthLabel.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
			healthLabel.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			healthLabel.Text = "HP: --/--"
			healthLabel.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(180, 230, 255)
			healthLabel.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
			healthLabel.TextSize = 15
			healthLabel.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
			healthLabel.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
			healthLabel:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
			healthLabel:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
			healthLabel:SetAttribute("NoFade", true)
			healthLabel.Parent = gui
		end
	end

	playerEsp[player] = {
		char = char, hl = hl, gui = gui, name = nameLabel,
		dist = distLabel, health = healthLabel, team = teamLabel, lastTeam = player.Team, color = color,
		lastDist = -1, lastHealth = nil,
		opts = (EspOpts.name and 1 or 0)
			+ (EspOpts.outline and 2 or 0)
			+ (EspOpts.studs and 4 or 0)
			+ (EspOpts.health and 8 or 0)
			+ (EspOpts.team and 16 or 0),
	}
end

local function refreshPlayerEsp()
	if not PlayerEspEnabled then return end
	local myRoot = getRootPart(LocalPlayer.Character)
	local range = PlayerEspRange
	if range >= 10000 then
		range = math.huge
	end

	for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
		if player ~= LocalPlayer then
			local char = player.Character
			local d = playerEsp[player]
			local theirRoot = char and getRootPart(char)

			if theirRoot then
				lastKnownEspPos[player] = theirRoot.Position
			end

			local tooFar = false
			if myRoot and theirRoot and range ~= math.huge then
				tooFar = (theirRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude > range
			end

			if char and char.Parent and theirRoot and not tooFar then
				local curOpts = (EspOpts.name and 1 or 0)
					+ (EspOpts.outline and 2 or 0)
					+ (EspOpts.studs and 4 or 0)
					+ (EspOpts.health and 8 or 0)
					+ (EspOpts.team and 16 or 0)
				local stale = not d or d.char ~= char or d.opts ~= curOpts
				if not stale and d then
					if EspOpts.outline and (not d.hl or not d.hl.Parent) then stale = true end
					if (EspOpts.name or EspOpts.studs or EspOpts.health or EspOpts.team) and (not d.gui or not d.gui.Parent) then stale = true end
				end
				if stale then
					buildPlayerEsp(player, char)
					d = playerEsp[player]
				end
				if d then
					local color = playerEspColor(player)
					if d.color ~= color then
						d.color = color
						if d.hl then
							d.hl.FillColor = color
							d.hl.OutlineColor = color
						end
						if d.name then d.name.TextColor3 = color end
					end
					if d.team and d.lastTeam ~= player.Team then
						d.lastTeam = player.Team
						d.team.Text = player.Team and player.Team.Name or "No team"
						d.team.TextColor3 = player.Team and player.TeamColor.Color or Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 196)
					end
					if myRoot and d.dist then
						local dist = math.floor((theirRoot.Position - myRoot.Position).Magnitude + 0.5)
						if dist ~= d.lastDist then
							d.lastDist = dist
							d.dist.Text = "[" .. dist .. " studs]"
						end
					end
					if d.health then
						local humanoid = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
						if humanoid then
							local hp = math.max(0, math.floor(humanoid.Health + 0.5))
							local maxHp = math.max(1, math.floor(humanoid.MaxHealth + 0.5))
							local healthText = string.format("HP: %d/%d", hp, maxHp)
							if healthText ~= d.lastHealth then
								d.lastHealth = healthText
								d.health.Text = healthText
								local ratio = humanoid.MaxHealth > 0 and humanoid.Health / humanoid.MaxHealth or 0
								local healthColor
								if ratio > 0.65 then
									healthColor = Color3.fromRGB(70, 235, 95)
								elseif ratio > 0.50 then
									healthColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 165, 45)
								elseif ratio > 0.25 then
									healthColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 225, 55)
								else
									healthColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 55, 55)
								end
								if d.healthTween then d.healthTween:Cancel() end
								d.healthTween = TweenService:Create(d.health,
									TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out),
									{TextColor3 = healthColor})
								d.healthTween:Play()
							end
						else
							d.health.Text = "HP: --/--"
							d.lastHealth = nil
						end
					end
				end
			else
				if d then
					removePlayerEsp(player)
				end
			end
		end
	end
end

local function clearPlayerEsp()
	if playerEspLoop then playerEspLoop:Disconnect() playerEspLoop = nil end
	if playerEspLeaving then playerEspLeaving:Disconnect() playerEspLeaving = nil end
	local toClear = {}
	for player in pairs(playerEsp) do
		table.insert(toClear, player)
	end
	for _, player in ipairs(toClear) do
		removePlayerEsp(player)
	end
end

local function setPlayerEsp(state)
	PlayerEspEnabled = state
	clearPlayerEsp()
	if state then
		local acc = 0
		playerEspLoop = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
			acc = acc + (dt)
			if acc < 0.08 then return end
			acc = 0
			refreshPlayerEsp()
		end)
		playerEspLeaving = Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(plr)
			removePlayerEsp(plr)
			lastKnownEspPos[plr] = nil
		end)
		task.spawn(function()
			while PlayerEspEnabled do
				local list = Players:GetPlayers()
				if #list > 1 then
					playerEspStreamIdx = (playerEspStreamIdx % #list) + 1
					local plr = list[playerEspStreamIdx]
					if plr and plr ~= LocalPlayer then
						local pos = lastKnownEspPos[plr]
						local root = plr.Character and getRootPart(plr.Character)
						if root then
							pos = root.Position
							lastKnownEspPos[plr] = pos
						end
						if pos then
							pcall(function()
								if LocalPlayer.RequestStreamAroundAsync then
									LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(pos)
								end
							end)
						end
					end
				end
				task.wait(0.75)
			end
		end)
		task.spawn(function()
			for _ = 1, 8 do
				if not PlayerEspEnabled then return end
				refreshPlayerEsp()
				task.wait(0.15)
			end
		end)
	end
	Settings.save()
end

-- ====================== FLY (QOL) ======================
local function initFly()
	local conn, charConn = nil, nil
	local bv, gyro = nil, nil

	local function dropBodies()
		if bv then pcall(function() bv:Destroy() end) bv = nil end
		if gyro then pcall(function() gyro:Destroy() end) gyro = nil end
		local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
		local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum then
			pcall(function()
				hum.PlatformStand = false
				hum.Sit = false
			end)
		end
	end

	local function ensureBodies(root)
		if bv and bv.Parent == root and gyro and gyro.Parent == root then
			return true
		end
		dropBodies()
		bv = Instance.new("BodyVelocity")
		bv.Name = "DannyFlyBV"
		bv.MaxForce = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
		bv.P = 9e4
		bv.Velocity = Vector3.zero
		bv.Parent = root
		gyro = Instance.new("BodyGyro")
		gyro.Name = "DannyFlyGyro"
		gyro.MaxTorque = Vector3.new(9e9, 9e9, 9e9)
		gyro.P = 9e4
		gyro.D = 500
		gyro.CFrame = root.CFrame
		gyro.Parent = root
		return true
	end

	local function key(k)
		return UserInputService:IsKeyDown(k) and 1 or 0
	end

	local function step(dt)
		if not HK.fly.on then return end
		local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
		if not ch then return end
		local root = getRootPart(ch)
		local hum = ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not root then return end

		ensureBodies(root)
		if hum then
			if not hum.PlatformStand then hum.PlatformStand = true end
			if hum.Sit then hum.Sit = false end
			-- cancel physics states that kill fly
			if hum:GetState() ~= Enum.HumanoidStateType.Flying then
				pcall(function()
					hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Flying)
				end)
			end
		end

		local cam = workspace.CurrentCamera
		if not cam then return end

		local dir = Vector3.zero
		if not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
			local f = key(Enum.KeyCode.W) - key(Enum.KeyCode.S)
			local r = key(Enum.KeyCode.D) - key(Enum.KeyCode.A)
			local u = key(Enum.KeyCode.E) + key(Enum.KeyCode.Space) - key(Enum.KeyCode.Q) - key(Enum.KeyCode.LeftControl)
			dir = cam.CFrame.LookVector * f + cam.CFrame.RightVector * r + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0) * u
		end

		local speed = (HK.fly.speed or 1) * 50
		local target = Vector3.zero
		if dir.Magnitude > 0.01 then
			target = dir.Unit * speed
		end
		if bv and bv.Parent then
			bv.Velocity = target
		end
		if gyro and gyro.Parent then
			gyro.CFrame = cam.CFrame
		end
	end

	local function bindCharacter()
		if charConn then charConn:Disconnect() charConn = nil end
		charConn = LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function()
			task.wait(0.2)
			if HK.fly.on then
				-- re-enable bodies on new character
				dropBodies()
			end
		end)
	end

	HK.fly.set = function(state)
		HK.fly.on = state == true
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		if state then
			bindCharacter()
			conn = RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(step)
			-- immediate kick so fly starts even before first frame ends
			task.defer(function()
				if HK.fly.on then step(1 / 60) end
			end)
		else
			dropBodies()
		end
		if HK.fly.toggle and HK.fly.knob then
			setToggle(HK.fly.toggle, HK.fly.knob, HK.fly.on)
		end
		Settings.save()
	end

	HK.fly.clear = function()
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		if charConn then charConn:Disconnect() charConn = nil end
		HK.fly.on = false
		dropBodies()
	end
end
initFly()

-- ====================== NOCLIP (QOL) ======================
local setNoclip, clearNoclip
local function initNoclip()
	local conn = nil
	local parts = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})

	local function restore()
		for part in pairs(parts) do
			if part and part.Parent then part.CanCollide = true end
			parts[part] = nil
		end
	end

	setNoclip = function(state)
		HK.noclip.on = state
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		if state then
			local list, listChar, nextRefresh = {}, nil, 0
			conn = RunService.Stepped:Connect(function()
				local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
				if not ch then return end
				local now = os.clock()
				if ch ~= listChar or now >= nextRefresh then
					listChar, nextRefresh = ch, now + 0.5
					list = {}
					for _, part in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
						if part:IsA("BasePart") then list[#list + 1] = part end
					end
				end
				for i = 1, #list do
					local part = list[i]
					if part.CanCollide then
						parts[part] = true
						part.CanCollide = false
					end
				end
			end)
		else
			restore()
		end
		setToggle(NoclipToggle, NoclipKnob, state)
		Settings.save()
	end

	clearNoclip = function()
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		HK.noclip.on = false
		restore()
	end
end
initNoclip()

-- ====================== NOFOG (QOL) ======================
local setNofog, clearNofog
local function initNofog()
	local conn = nil
	local savedEnd, savedStart = nil, nil
	local atmos = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local FOG_END, FOG_START = 100000, 99999

	local function apply()
		local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
		if lighting.FogEnd ~= FOG_END then
			savedEnd = lighting.FogEnd
			lighting.FogEnd = FOG_END
		end
		if lighting.FogStart ~= FOG_START then
			savedStart = lighting.FogStart
			lighting.FogStart = FOG_START
		end
		for _, obj in ipairs(lighting:GetChildren()) do
			if obj:IsA("Atmosphere") and (obj.Density ~= 0 or obj.Haze ~= 0) then
				local saved = atmos[obj] or {}
				if obj.Density ~= 0 then saved.density = obj.Density end
				if obj.Haze ~= 0 then saved.haze = obj.Haze end
				atmos[obj] = saved
				obj.Density = 0
				obj.Haze = 0
			end
		end
	end

	local function restore()
		local lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
		if savedEnd then lighting.FogEnd = savedEnd end
		if savedStart then lighting.FogStart = savedStart end
		savedEnd, savedStart = nil, nil
		for obj, saved in pairs(atmos) do
			if obj and obj.Parent then
				if saved.density then obj.Density = saved.density end
				if saved.haze then obj.Haze = saved.haze end
			end
			atmos[obj] = nil
		end
	end

	setNofog = function(state)
		HK.nofog.on = state
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		if state then
			apply()
			local acc = 0
			conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
				acc = acc + (dt)
				if acc < 0.25 then return end
				acc = 0
				apply()
			end)
		else
			restore()
		end
		setToggle(NofogToggle, NofogKnob, state)
		Settings.save()
	end

	clearNofog = function()
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		if HK.nofog.on then restore() end
		HK.nofog.on = false
	end
end
initNofog()

-- ====================== LET THERE BE LIGHT (QOL) ======================
local function initLight()
	local Lighting = game:GetService("Lighting")
	local conn = nil
	local saved = nil

	local function apply()
		-- one "level" slider (0.5 - 10) drives everything; 2.0 is the default look
		local level = HK.light.level or 2
		local grey = math.clamp(math.floor(100 + level * 45), 0, 255)
		local ambient = Color3.fromRGB(grey, grey, grey)
		local exposure = math.max(0, (level - 3) * 0.5)

		if Lighting.Brightness ~= level then Lighting.Brightness = level end
		if Lighting.ClockTime ~= 14 then Lighting.ClockTime = 14 end
		if Lighting.GlobalShadows then Lighting.GlobalShadows = false end
		if Lighting.Ambient ~= ambient then Lighting.Ambient = ambient end
		if Lighting.OutdoorAmbient ~= ambient then Lighting.OutdoorAmbient = ambient end
		if Lighting.ExposureCompensation ~= exposure then Lighting.ExposureCompensation = exposure end

		-- a light that follows you, so caves / interiors with custom lighting are lit too
		local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
		local root = ch and (ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or ch.PrimaryPart)
		if root then
			local pl = root:FindFirstChild("DannyLight")
			if not pl then
				pl = Instance.new("PointLight")
				pl.Name = "DannyLight"
				pl.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 244, 225)
				pl.Shadows = false
				pl.Range = 60
				pl.Parent = root
			end
			local pb = level * 1.5
			if pl.Brightness ~= pb then pl.Brightness = pb end
		end
	end

	local function removeLight()
		local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
		if ch then
			for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
				if d.Name == "DannyLight" and d:IsA("PointLight") then d:Destroy() end
			end
		end
	end

	local function restore()
		if saved then
			for prop, value in pairs(saved) do
				pcall(function() Lighting[prop] = value end)
			end
			saved = nil
		end
		removeLight()
	end

	HK.light.set = function(state)
		state = state == true
		HK.light.on = state
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		if state then
			if not saved then
				saved = {
					Brightness = Lighting.Brightness,
					ClockTime = Lighting.ClockTime,
					GlobalShadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows,
					Ambient = Lighting.Ambient,
					OutdoorAmbient = Lighting.OutdoorAmbient,
					ExposureCompensation = Lighting.ExposureCompensation,
				}
			end
			apply()
			local acc = 0
			conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
				acc = acc + (dt)
				if acc < 0.25 then return end
				acc = 0
				apply()
			end)
		else
			restore()
		end
		if HK.light.toggle and HK.light.knob then
			setToggle(HK.light.toggle, HK.light.knob, state)
		end
		Settings.save()
	end

	HK.light.refresh = function()
		if HK.light.on then apply() end
	end

	HK.light.clear = function()
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		if HK.light.on then restore() end
		HK.light.on = false
	end
end
initLight()

-- ====================== INF JUMP (QOL) ======================
do
	local conn = nil

	local function jumpNow()
		local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
		local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if hum and hum.Health > 0 then
			hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Jumping)
		end
	end

	local function connect()
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		if not HK.infjump.on then return end
		if HK.infjump.mode == "hold" then
			-- HOLD SPACE: keep jumping the whole time Space is held down
			local acc = 0
			conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function(dt)
				acc = acc + (dt)
				if acc < 0.08 then return end
				acc = 0
				if UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then return end
				if UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then jumpNow() end
			end)
		else
			-- TAP SPACE: one extra jump per fresh key press (holding Space does nothing extra, you have to tap it).
			-- JumpRequest keeps firing while Space is held, so it is only used when Space is NOT down (mobile / gamepad).
			local c1 = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
				if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Space and not UserInputService:GetFocusedTextBox() then
					jumpNow()
				end
			end)
			local c2 = UserInputService.JumpRequest:Connect(function()
				if not UserInputService:IsKeyDown(Enum.KeyCode.Space) then jumpNow() end
			end)
			conn = {Disconnect = function() c1:Disconnect() c2:Disconnect() end}
		end
	end

	HK.infjump.set = function(state)
		HK.infjump.on = state
		connect()
		setToggle(HK.infjump.toggle, HK.infjump.knob, state)
		Settings.save()
	end

	HK.infjump.setMode = function(mode, silent)
		if mode ~= "tap" and mode ~= "hold" then return end
		HK.infjump.mode = mode
		if HK.infjump.refreshMode then HK.infjump.refreshMode() end
		connect()
		if not silent then Settings.save() end
	end

	HK.infjump.clear = function()
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		HK.infjump.on = false
	end
end

-- ====================== INF ZOOM (QOL) ======================
-- Normal        = the camera zoom-out limit is removed
-- Through Walls = same, plus walls between you and the camera turn see-through (Invisicam) instead of
--                 pushing the camera in toward your character
do
	local BIG = 100000
	local origMax, origOcc = nil, nil
	local connZoom, connOcc = nil, nil

	local function enforce()
		-- the game (or a respawn) may put the settings back; keep them while the feature is on
		if not HK.infzoom.on then return end
		if LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance ~= BIG then
			pcall(function() LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = BIG end)
		end
		if HK.infzoom.mode == "walls" and LocalPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode ~= Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam then
			pcall(function() LocalPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode = Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Invisicam end)
		end
	end

	local function restoreOcc()
		if origOcc ~= nil then
			pcall(function() LocalPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode = origOcc end)
			origOcc = nil
		end
	end

	-- puts the camera occlusion setting where the chosen mode wants it
	local function applyMode()
		if not HK.infzoom.on then return end
		if HK.infzoom.mode == "walls" then
			if origOcc == nil then
				local ok, cur = pcall(function() return LocalPlayer.DevCameraOcclusionMode end)
				origOcc = ok and cur or Enum.DevCameraOcclusionMode.Zoom
			end
		else
			restoreOcc()
		end
		enforce()
	end

	local function stop()
		if connZoom then connZoom:Disconnect() connZoom = nil end
		if connOcc then connOcc:Disconnect() connOcc = nil end
		if origMax then
			pcall(function() LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance = origMax end)
			origMax = nil
		end
		restoreOcc()
	end

	HK.infzoom.set = function(state)
		state = state == true
		HK.infzoom.on = state
		if state then
			if not origMax then
				local cur = LocalPlayer.CameraMaxZoomDistance
				origMax = (cur and cur < BIG) and cur or 128
			end
			if not connZoom then
				connZoom = LocalPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("CameraMaxZoomDistance"):Connect(enforce)
			end
			if not connOcc then
				connOcc = LocalPlayer:GetPropertyChangedSignal("DevCameraOcclusionMode"):Connect(enforce)
			end
			applyMode()
		else
			stop()
		end
		setToggle(HK.infzoom.toggle, HK.infzoom.knob, state)
		Settings.save()
	end

	HK.infzoom.setMode = function(mode, silent)
		if mode ~= "normal" and mode ~= "walls" then return end
		HK.infzoom.mode = mode
		if HK.infzoom.refreshMode then HK.infzoom.refreshMode() end
		applyMode()
		if not silent then Settings.save() end
	end

	HK.infzoom.clear = function()
		HK.infzoom.on = false
		stop()
	end
end

-- ====================== NO FALL DAMAGE (QOL) ======================
-- Most games do NOT judge fall damage by landing speed, they measure how far you fell (height when you left
-- the ground vs height when you land) or fire a "fall damage" remote to the server. So this works two ways:
--   1) FALL RESET: while you are falling, every few studs it re-triggers the Humanoid "Landed" state, so the
--      game thinks you only ever fell a few studs, no matter how high you jumped from. You keep falling at
--      normal speed (no slow-fall).
--   2) REMOTE BLOCK: any FireServer / InvokeServer whose remote name (or first text argument) looks like fall
--      damage is blocked while this is on (needs an executor with hookmetamethod).
-- DIAGNOSTIC: if you still lose health right after a fall, it prints (F9 console) which remotes the game fired
-- in the last second, so the exact fall-damage remote can be added.
-- Skipped while Fly is on.
do
	local RESET_DROP = 5 -- studs you may fall before the "fall height" is reset
	local FALL_WORDS = {"fall", "landdam", "landdmg", "hitground", "groundhit"}
	local conn, healthConn, watchedHum = nil, nil, nil
	local lastY, lastAir = nil, 0
	local hookTried = false
	local recent = {} -- last remotes the game fired {t, name}

	local function looksLikeFall(s)
		s = string.lower(tostring(s))
		for _, w in ipairs(FALL_WORDS) do
			if string.find(s, w, 1, true) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function installHook()
		if hookTried then return end
		hookTried = true
		if not (hookmetamethod and getnamecallmethod) then return end
		pcall(function()
			local old
			local wrap = newcclosure or function(f) return f end
			old = hookmetamethod(game, "__namecall", wrap(function(self, ...)
				local method = getnamecallmethod()
				if HK.nofall.on and (method == "FireServer" or method == "InvokeServer") and not (checkcaller and checkcaller()) then
					local ok, name = pcall(function() return self.Name end)
					if ok then
						recent[#recent + 1] = {t = os.clock(), name = tostring(name)}
						if #recent > 40 then table.remove(recent, 1) end
						local blocked = looksLikeFall(name)
						if not blocked then
							local first = ...
							if type(first) == "string" and looksLikeFall(first) then blocked = true end
						end
						if blocked then return nil end
					end
				end
				return old(self, ...)
			end))
		end)
	end

	local function watchHealth(hum)
		if hum == watchedHum then return end
		watchedHum = hum
		if healthConn then healthConn:Disconnect() healthConn = nil end
		local last = hum.Health
		healthConn = hum.HealthChanged:Connect(function(h)
			local lost = last - h
			last = h
			if lost > 0 and HK.nofall.on and os.clock() - lastAir < 0.6 then
				local seen, names = {}, {}
				for _, r in ipairs(recent) do
					if os.clock() - r.t < 1 and not seen[r.name] then
						seen[r.name] = true
						names[#names + 1] = r.name
					end
				end
				warn(string.format("[NoFallDamage] lost %.0f HP right after a fall. Remotes fired in the last second: %s",
					lost, #names > 0 and table.concat(names, ", ") or "(none seen)"))
			end
		end)
	end

	local function step()
		if not HK.nofall.on then return end
		if HK.fly and HK.fly.on then lastY = nil return end
		local char = LocalPlayer.Character
		local hum = char and char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		local root = hum and (hum.RootPart or char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart"))
		if not (hum and root) or hum.Health <= 0 then lastY = nil return end
		watchHealth(hum)

		if hum:GetState() == Enum.HumanoidStateType.Freefall then
			lastAir = os.clock()
			local y = root.Position.Y
			if not lastY then lastY = y end
			if lastY - y >= RESET_DROP then
				lastY = y
				pcall(function() hum:ChangeState(Enum.HumanoidStateType.Landed) end)
			end
		else
			lastY = nil
		end
	end

	local function stop()
		if conn then conn:Disconnect() conn = nil end
		if healthConn then healthConn:Disconnect() healthConn = nil end
		watchedHum, lastY = nil, nil
	end

	HK.nofall.set = function(state)
		state = state == true
		HK.nofall.on = state
		if state then
			installHook()
			if not conn then conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(step) end
		else
			stop()
		end
		setToggle(HK.nofall.toggle, HK.nofall.knob, state)
		Settings.save()
	end

	HK.nofall.clear = function()
		HK.nofall.on = false
		stop()
	end
end

HK.esp.action = function()
	local state = not PlayerEspEnabled
	setPlayerEsp(state)
	setToggle(PlayerEspToggle, PlayerEspKnob, state)
end
HK.fly.action = function() HK.fly.set(not HK.fly.on) end
HK.noclip.action = function() setNoclip(not HK.noclip.on) end
HK.nofog.action = function() setNofog(not HK.nofog.on) end
HK.infjump.action = function() HK.infjump.set(not HK.infjump.on) end
HK.infzoom.action = function() HK.infzoom.set(not HK.infzoom.on) end
HK.nofall.action = function() HK.nofall.set(not HK.nofall.on) end
HK.light.action = function() HK.light.set(not HK.light.on) end

-- ---------- small UI helpers (same look as the rest of the GUI) ----------
local function mkLabel(parent, text, pos, size, ts, color, font)
	local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	l.Position = pos
	l.Size = size
	l.Text = text
	l.TextColor3 = color or DIM
	l.Font = font or FONT
	l.TextSize = ts or 14
	l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	l.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	l.Parent = parent
	return l
end

	local hdr = mkLabel(card, text, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 5), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 26), 19, CREAM, FONT_B)

	line.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER

	line.Parent = card
	addFadeLine(line, 0)
	return hdr
end

local function mkSwitch(parent, pos, onChange, confirmOn)
	local t = Instance.new("TextButton")
	t.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 28)
	t.Position = pos
	t.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(88, 88, 94)
	t.Text = ""
	t.AutoButtonColor = false
	t.Parent = parent
	addCorner(t, 14)
	addStroke(t, Color3.fromRGB(88, 88, 94), 1.5, 0.5)
	local k = Instance.new("Frame")
	k.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
	k.Position = UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -11)
	k.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
	k.Parent = t
	addCorner(k, 11)
	local sw = {on = false, button = t}
	function sw.set(v)
		sw.on = v and true or false
		setToggle(t, k, sw.on)
		if onChange then onChange(sw.on) end
	end
	t.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		-- optional "Are you sure?" gate, only when turning ON by clicking (loading saved state skips it)
		if confirmOn and not sw.on then
			confirmOn(function() sw.set(true) end)
		else
			sw.set(not sw.on)
		end
	end)
	return sw
end

local function mkRow(card, y, title, sub, onChange, confirmOn)

	local sw = mkSwitch(card, UDim2.new(1, -70, 0, y + 3), onChange, confirmOn)
	return sw, subL, titleL
end

	b.Size = size
	b.Position = pos
	b.BackgroundColor3 = color or BTN
	b.Text = text
	b.TextColor3 = CREAM

	addStroke(b, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
	addHover(b, color or BTN, hover or BTN_HOVER)
	return b
end

local function flash(btn, txt, orig)
	btn.Text = txt
	task.delay(1.4, function()
		if btn.Parent then btn.Text = orig end
	end)
end

local function mkStepper(card, y, title, list, getV, setV, fmt)
	mkLabel(card, title, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y + 4), UDim2.new(1, -160, 0, 20), 16, DIM, FONT)
	local minus = mkButton(card, "-", UDim2.new(1, -142, 0, y), UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 28), nil, nil, 20)
	local box = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	box.Size = UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 28)
	box.Position = UDim2.new(1, -106, 0, y)
	box.BackgroundColor3 = FIELD
	box.TextColor3 = CREAM
	box.Font = FONT_B
	box.TextSize = 16
	box.Text = ""
	box.Parent = card
	addCorner(box, 5)
	addStroke(box, SILVER_DIM, 1.2, 0.1)
	local plus = mkButton(card, "+", UDim2.new(1, -46, 0, y), UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 28), nil, nil, 20)

	local function show() box.Text = fmt(getV()) end
	local function nearest(v)
		local bi, bd = 1, math.huge
		for i, x in ipairs(list) do
			local d = math.abs(x - v)
			if d < bd then bi, bd = i, d end
		end
		return bi
	end
	minus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local i = nearest(getV())
		if i > 1 then setV(list[i - 1]) end
		show()
	end)
	plus.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local i = nearest(getV())
		if i < #list then setV(list[i + 1]) end
		show()
	end)
	show()
	return {refresh = show}
end

local function rootOf()
	local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
	return ch and (ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or ch:FindFirstChild("Torso") or ch:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso"))
end

local function posOf(obj)
	if obj:IsA("BasePart") then return obj.Position end
	if obj:IsA("Model") and obj.PrimaryPart then return obj.PrimaryPart.Position end
	local part = obj:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("BasePart", true)
	return part and part.Position or nil
end

-- time-sliced walk of the whole workspace (no big freeze)
local function scanWorkspace(visit, aliveFn)
	local stack, top = {workspace}, 1
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while top > 0 and not stopped and (not aliveFn or aliveFn()) do
		local inst = stack[top]
		stack[top] = nil
		top = top - (1)
		if inst.Parent then
			for _, child in ipairs(inst:GetChildren()) do
				visit(child)
				top = top + (1)
				stack[top] = child
			end
		end
		if os.clock() - t0 > 0.002 then -- [perf] 2ms slices
			task.wait()
			t0 = os.clock()
		end
	end
end

bannerWpBtn = bannerBtn(WP_TXT, 0, 0)

local function bannerWpActive()
	local SPx = Settings.SP
	return SPx and SPx.active and bannerWpData and SPx.active.sp == bannerWpData
end

bannerWpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	local SPx = Settings.SP
	local obj = bannerTarget
	local p = obj and obj.Parent and posOf(obj)
	if not SPx or not SPx.start or not p then
		bannerWpBtn.Text = "Fruit is gone"
		task.delay(1.4, function() if bannerWpBtn.Parent then bannerWpBtn.Text = WP_TXT end end)
		return
	end
	if bannerWpActive() then
		SPx.clear()
		bannerWpBtn.Text = WP_TXT
		return
	end
	bannerWpData = {name = obj.Name, x = p.X, y = p.Y, z = p.Z}
	SPx.start(bannerWpData)
	bannerWpBtn.Text = WP_CLEAR_TXT
end)

bannerTpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	local obj = bannerTarget
	local root = rootOf()
	local p = obj and obj.Parent and posOf(obj)
	if not root or not p then
		bannerTpBtn.Text = "Fruit is gone"
		task.delay(1.4, function() if bannerTpBtn.Parent then bannerTpBtn.Text = "Teleport" end end)
		return
	end
	pcall(function() root.CFrame = CFrame.new(p + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) end)
	bannerTpBtn.Text = "Teleported!"
	task.delay(1.4, function() if bannerTpBtn.Parent then bannerTpBtn.Text = "Teleport" end end)
end)

local function showBanner(text, color, target)
	bannerToken = bannerToken + (1)
	local my = bannerToken
	bannerText.Text = text
	bannerStroke.Color = color or WHITE
	bannerTarget = target
	bannerActions.Visible = target ~= nil
	if target then
		bannerWpData = nil
		bannerWpBtn.Text = WP_TXT
		bannerTpBtn.Text = "Teleport"
	end
	banner.Visible = true
	tween(banner, 0.35, {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, 14)})
	task.delay(target and 15 or 7, function()
		if my == bannerToken and not stopped then
			tween(banner, 0.35, {Position = UDim2.new(0.5, 0, 0, -70)})
			task.delay(0.4, function()
				if my == bannerToken then banner.Visible = false end
			end)
		end
	end)
end

local function playPing()
	task.spawn(function()
		pcall(function()
			local snd = Instance.new("Sound")
			snd.SoundId = "rbxasset://sounds/electronicpingshort.wav"
			snd.Volume = 2
			snd.Parent = game:GetService("SoundService")
			for _ = 1, 3 do
				snd.TimePosition = 0
				snd:Play()
				task.wait(0.45)
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
			snd:Destroy()
		end)
	end)
end

local setAntiAfk, resetSellTimer

		idleConn:Disconnect()

	end

		idleConn = LocalPlayer.Idled:Connect(function()
			pcall(function()
				VirtualUser:CaptureController()
				VirtualUser:ClickButton2(Vector2.new(0, 0))
			end)
		end)
	end
end

resetSellTimer = function()
	nextSellAt = os.clock() + S.sellMins * 60
end
