-- the farm loop calls this at the start of every cycle (see runKeyLoop)
local hookRun = -1
Settings.DX.cycleHook = function(aliveFn)
	if hookRun ~= runId then
		-- the farm loop just (re)started: begin counting from now, don't sell immediately
		hookRun = runId
		Settings.DX.failsafePause = false
		resetSellTimer()
		return
	end
	if not S.autoSell then return end
	if os.clock() < nextSellAt then return end
	-- failsafes OFF for the whole trip, back ON the moment you're teleported back to your farming spot
	Settings.DX.failsafePause = true
	local okSell, started = pcall(function() return Settings.DX.sell and Settings.DX.sell() end)
	if okSell and started then
		local t0 = os.clock()
		while Settings.DX.sellBusy and aliveFn() and os.clock() - t0 < 40 do
			task.wait(0.2)
		end
	end
	Settings.DX.failsafePause = false
	resetSellTimer()
end

-- ====================== FARM PAGE: RARE FRUIT ALERT ======================
local RARE = {["Life Up Fruit"] = true, ["Chakra Fruit"] = true, ["Fruit Of Forgetfulness"] = true}
local rareLive = {}
local rareCount = 0
local rareSw, rareStatus, tpRare
local TP_RARE_TEXT = "Teleport to nearest rare fruit"

local function ownedByPlayer(obj)
	local cur = obj.Parent
	for _ = 1, 8 do
		if not cur or cur == workspace then return false end
		if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(cur) then return true end
		cur = cur.Parent
	end
	return false
end

local function distTo(obj)
	local root = rootOf()
	local p = posOf(obj)
	if root and p then return (root.Position - p).Magnitude end
	return nil
end

local function announce(obj)
	local d = distTo(obj)
	local txt = obj.Name .. " spawned!"
	if d then txt = txt .. "  (" .. math.floor(d) .. " studs)" end
	showBanner("⭐ " .. txt, fruitColorFor(obj), obj)
	playPing()
	pcall(function()
		game:GetService("StarterGui"):SetCore("SendNotification", {Title = "Rare fruit!", Text = obj.Name, Duration = 6})
	end)
end

local function rareTry(obj, silent)
	if rareLive[obj] or not RARE[obj.Name] then return end
	if not (obj:IsA("BasePart") or obj:IsA("Model") or obj:IsA("Tool")) then return end
	local p = obj.Parent
	for _ = 1, 5 do
		if not p then break end
		if rareLive[p] then return end
		p = p.Parent
	end
	if ownedByPlayer(obj) then return end
	rareLive[obj] = true
	rareCount = rareCount + (1)
	local c
	c = obj.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
		if not obj:IsDescendantOf(workspace) or ownedByPlayer(obj) then
			if rareLive[obj] then
				rareLive[obj] = nil
				rareCount = rareCount - (1)
			end
			c:Disconnect()
		end
	end)
	if not silent and S.rareAlert then announce(obj) end
end

local function nearestRare()
	local root = rootOf()
	if not root then return nil end
	local best, bd
	for obj in pairs(rareLive) do
		if obj.Parent then
			local p = posOf(obj)
			if p then
				local d = (p - root.Position).Magnitude
				if not bd or d < bd then best, bd = obj, d end
			end
		end
	end
	return best, bd
end

do
	-- make room: everything on the QOL page from order 4 up (Let There Be Light, Inf Jump, Fly, Noclip) moves down one
	for _, ch in ipairs(QOLContent:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.LayoutOrder >= 4 and ch.LayoutOrder < 20 then
			ch.LayoutOrder = ch.LayoutOrder + 1
		end
	end
	local card = createCard(148, 4, QOLContent)
	cardHeader(card, "🔔  Rare Fruit Alert")
	rareSw = mkRow(card, 42, "Alert", "Life Up, Chakra, Forgetfulness", function(on)
		S.rareAlert = on
		if on then
			local names = {}
			for obj in pairs(rareLive) do
				if obj.Parent and #names < 3 then names[#names + 1] = obj.Name end
			end
			if #names > 0 then
				local near = nearestRare()
				showBanner("⭐ Rare fruit on map: " .. table.concat(names, ", "), Color3.fromRGB(255, 220, 80), near)
				playPing()
			end
		end
		Settings.save()
	end)
	-- the theme recolor skips switches named OnOffToggle; without this name the ON color got repainted gray
	if rareSw.button then rareSw.button.Name = "OnOffToggle" end
	rareStatus = mkLabel(card, "Rare fruits on map: 0", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 84), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 18), 15, DIM, FONT)
	tpRare = mkButton(card, TP_RARE_TEXT, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 108), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 30), nil, nil, 15)
	tpRare.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local obj = nearestRare()
		local root = rootOf()
		local p = obj and posOf(obj)
		if not obj or not root or not p then
			flash(tpRare, "No rare fruit found", TP_RARE_TEXT)
			return
		end
		pcall(function() root.CFrame = CFrame.new(p + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)) end)
		flash(tpRare, "Teleported to " .. obj.Name, TP_RARE_TEXT)
	end)
end

-- ====================== QOL PAGE: ANTI VOID ======================
-- Sits right above "Let There Be Light". Watches for "no floor under you" and rescues you before the void kills you.
-- Works in any map (it checks the real world under you, it does not rely on one fixed height).
pcall(function()
	local AV = {on = false, mode = "tp", drop = 30, strict = false, token = 0}
	Settings.AV = AV
	-- only the newest copy of the script may ever run Anti Void (an old copy left running can no longer keep rescuing you),
	-- and any platform left behind by an older copy is removed
	env.DannyAVGen = (env.DannyAVGen or 0) + 1
	local myGen = env.DannyAVGen
	pcall(function()
		for _, o in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
			if o.Name == "DannyAntiVoidPlatform" then o:Destroy() end
		end
	end)

	-- take Let There Be Light's slot; it (and everything below it) moves down one
	local slot = (HK.light and HK.light.card and HK.light.card.LayoutOrder) or 5
	for _, ch in ipairs(QOLContent:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.LayoutOrder >= slot and ch.LayoutOrder < 20 then
			ch.LayoutOrder = ch.LayoutOrder + 1
		end
	end
	local card = createCard(322, slot, QOLContent)
	cardHeader(card, "🛡️  Anti Void")

	local sw = mkRow(card, 42, "Anti Void", "Catches you before the void gets you", function(on)
		AV.on = on
		AV.token = AV.token + 1
		if on then AV.start() else AV.reset() end
		Settings.save()
	end)

	-- recovery mode
	mkLabel(card, "When you fall into the void", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 84), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 20), 16, DIM, FONT)
	local tpBtn = mkButton(card, "", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 106), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 30), nil, nil, 14)
	local plBtn = mkButton(card, "", UDim2.new(0.5, 4, 0, 106), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 30), nil, nil, 14)
	local function refreshMode()
		tpBtn.Text = (AV.mode == "tp" and "●  " or "○  ") .. "Teleport Back"
		plBtn.Text = (AV.mode == "platform" and "●  " or "○  ") .. "Platform"
	end

	-- how far you may fall (over nothing) before it steps in
	local DROPS = {10, 20, 30, 45, 60, 100, 150, 250}
	local stepper = mkStepper(card, 146, "Void drop (studs)", DROPS,
		function() return AV.drop end,
		function(v) AV.drop = v Settings.save() end,
		function(v) return tostring(v) end)

	local strictSw = mkRow(card, 184, "Strict mode", "Also rescues long falls over any floor", function(on)
		AV.strict = on
		Settings.save()
	end)

	-- the GUI theme repaints plain buttons, which could make an ON switch look OFF; this name makes it leave them alone
	for _, d in ipairs(card:GetChildren()) do
		if d:IsA("TextButton") and d.Size.X.Offset == 54 and d.Size.Y.Offset == 28 then d.Name = "OnOffToggle" end
	end
	local safeBtn = mkButton(card, "SET SAFE SPOT HERE", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 228), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 30), nil, nil, 15)
	local status = mkLabel(card, "Off", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 266), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 20), 14, DIM, FONT)
	status:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
	local function setStatus(t) status.Text = t end

	-- live readout so you can see exactly what Anti Void is doing (also shows which build is running)
	local diag = mkLabel(card, "", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 290), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 18), 13, DIM, FONT)
	diag:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
	task.spawn(function()
		while card.Parent and not Settings.stopped and env.DannyAVGen == myGen do
			local r = rootOf()
			local plat = 0
			pcall(function()
				for _, o in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
					if o.Name == "DannyAntiVoidPlatform" then plat = plat + 1 end
				end
			end)
			pcall(function()
				diag.Text = "v7  switch:" .. (AV.on and "ON" or "OFF") .. "  platforms:" .. plat
					.. "  Y:" .. (r and tostring(math.floor(r.Position.Y)) or "-")
					.. "  killY:" .. tostring(math.floor(workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight))
			end)
			task.wait(1)
		end
	end)

	-- ---------- state ----------
	local params = RaycastParams.new()
	params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
	pcall(function() params.RespectCanCollide = true end) -- only solid floors count as ground (water counts too)
	local hist = {}            -- recent solid-ground spots {cf, feetY, t}
	local manual = nil         -- spot you set by hand {cf, feetY}
	local platform, platY = nil, nil
	local lastChar, lastPos, lastStepT = nil, nil, nil
	local graceUntil, histReset, lastRec = 0, false, 0

	local function killPlatform()
		if platform then
			pcall(function() platform:Destroy() end)
			platform = nil
		end
	end
	-- removes EVERY Anti Void platform in the map (used whenever Anti Void is switched off)
	local function purgeAll()
		platform = nil
		pcall(function()
			for _, o in ipairs(workspace:GetChildren()) do
				if o.Name == "DannyAntiVoidPlatform" then
					o.CanCollide = false
					o:Destroy()
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	local function feetY(ch, root)
		local ok, cf, size = pcall(ch.GetBoundingBox, ch)
		if ok and cf and size then return cf.Position.Y - size.Y / 2 end
		return root.Position.Y - 3
	end

	-- newest spot that is at least 1s old (so you come back to solid ground, not the crumbling edge)
	local function pickSafe(now)
		if manual then return manual end
		for i = #hist, 1, -1 do
			if now - hist[i].t >= 1 then return hist[i] end
		end
		return hist[1]
	end

	local function recover(ch, root, now)
		local e = pickSafe(now)
		root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
		root.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
		if AV.mode == "tp" and e then
			root.CFrame = e.cf + Vector3.new(0, 3, 0)
			setStatus("Rescued - teleported back to safe ground")
		else
			-- platform at the height of the last safe ground, so you can walk straight back
			local pos = root.Position
			local off = pos.Y - feetY(ch, root)
			local sy = e and e.feetY or (pos.Y - off)
			killPlatform()
			local p = Instance.new("Part")
			p.Name = "DannyAntiVoidPlatform"
			p.Size = Vector3.new(26, 1, 26)
			p.Anchored = true
			p.CanCollide = true
			p.Transparency = 1
			p.CastShadow = false
			p.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
			p.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, sy - 0.5, pos.Z)
			p.Parent = workspace
			platform, platY = p, sy
			root.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, sy + off + 0.4, pos.Z) * (root.CFrame - root.CFrame.Position)
			setStatus("Caught on a platform - walk back to the ground")
		end
		graceUntil = now + 1.2
		lastPos = root.Position
	end

	local function step()
		if not AV.on or env.DannyAVGen ~= myGen then return end
		local now = os.clock()
		local dt = now - (lastStepT or now)
		lastStepT = now
		local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
		local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		local hum = ch and ch:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
		if not (root and hum) or hum.Health <= 0 then
			killPlatform()
			lastPos = nil
			return
		end
		if ch ~= lastChar then -- new character (respawn): start clean and give the map a moment to load
			lastChar = ch
			hist = {}
			killPlatform()
			lastPos = nil
			histReset = false
			graceUntil = now + 3
		end
		local pos = root.Position
		if lastPos and (pos - lastPos).Magnitude > 70 + 250 * dt then
			-- you were teleported (by this GUI or the game): the map may not be loaded yet, so wait before judging
			graceUntil = now + 4
			histReset = true
			killPlatform()
		end
		lastPos = pos

		-- fly / noclip / seats are meant to leave the ground, so leave them alone
		if (HK.fly and HK.fly.on) or (HK.noclip and HK.noclip.on) or hum.Sit then return end

		-- standing on our platform: keep it under you, drop it once real ground is below you again
		if platform then
			if pos.Y < platY - 25 then
				killPlatform()
			else
				platform.CFrame = CFrame.new(pos.X, platY - 0.5, pos.Z)
				params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {ch, platform}
				local hit = workspace:Raycast(pos, Vector3.new(0, -400, 0), params)
				if hit and hit.Position.Y >= platY - 4 then
					killPlatform()
					setStatus("Back on solid ground")
				end
				return
			end
		end

		if hum.FloorMaterial ~= Enum.Material.Air then
			-- solid ground: remember it as a safe spot
			if now - lastRec >= 0.25 then
				lastRec = now
				if histReset then hist = {} histReset = false end
				hist[#hist + 1] = {cf = root.CFrame, feetY = feetY(ch, root), t = now}
				if #hist > 12 then table.remove(hist, 1) end
			end
			return
		end

		if now < graceUntil then return end

		params.FilterDescendantsInstances = {ch}
		local hit = workspace:Raycast(pos, Vector3.new(0, -4000, 0), params)
		local e = manual or hist[#hist]
		local fell = e and (e.cf.Position.Y - pos.Y) or 0
		local vy = root.AssemblyLinearVelocity.Y
		local fp = workspace.FallenPartsDestroyHeight
		local hardY = (fp == fp and fp > -100000) and (fp + 80) or nil

		local void = false
		if not hit then
			-- nothing solid anywhere under you
			void = (fell >= AV.drop) or (vy < -90) or ((not e) and vy < -40)
		elseif hardY and pos.Y < hardY then
			void = true -- about to hit the map's kill height
		elseif AV.strict and fell >= math.max(AV.drop, 40) and vy < -40 then
			void = true
		end
		if void then recover(ch, root, now) end
	end

	AV.start = function()
		local token = AV.token
		setStatus("Watching for the void...")
		task.spawn(function()
			while AV.on and AV.token == token and not Settings.stopped and env.DannyAVGen == myGen and card.Parent do
				pcall(step)
				task.wait(0.05)
			end
			if AV.token == token or not AV.on then killPlatform() end
			if not AV.on then purgeAll() end
		end)
	end
	AV.reset = function()
		AV.on = false
		killPlatform()
		purgeAll()
		hist = {}
		lastChar, lastPos, lastStepT = nil, nil, nil
		setStatus("Off - the void will kill you")
	end

	tpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		AV.mode = "tp"
		refreshMode()
		killPlatform()
		Settings.save()
	end)
	plBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		AV.mode = "platform"
		refreshMode()
		Settings.save()
	end)
	refreshMode()

	safeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if manual then
			manual = nil
			safeBtn.Text = "SET SAFE SPOT HERE"
			setStatus(AV.on and "Using automatic safe spots" or "Off")
			return
		end
		local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
		local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not root then
			flash(safeBtn, "No character yet", "SET SAFE SPOT HERE")
			return
		end
		manual = {cf = root.CFrame, feetY = feetY(ch, root), t = os.clock()}
		safeBtn.Text = "CLEAR MANUAL SAFE SPOT"
		setStatus("Manual safe spot saved")
	end)

	Settings.AVsave = {
		get = function() return {on = false, mode = AV.mode, drop = AV.drop, strict = AV.strict} end,
		apply = function(d)
			if d.mode == "tp" or d.mode == "platform" then AV.mode = d.mode refreshMode() end
			if tonumber(d.drop) then AV.drop = math.clamp(tonumber(d.drop), 5, 500) stepper.refresh() end
			if d.strict ~= nil and (d.strict == true) ~= strictSw.on then strictSw.set(d.strict == true) end
			if d.on ~= nil and (d.on == true) ~= sw.on then sw.set(d.on == true) end
		end,
	}

	local prevStop = Settings.DX.stop
	Settings.DX.stop = function()
		AV.on = false
		AV.token = AV.token + 1
		killPlatform()
		if prevStop then pcall(prevStop) end
	end
end)

-- always tracking quietly in the background (so the teleport button works even with the alert off)
track(workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(obj)
	if RARE[obj.Name] then
		task.defer(function()
			if not stopped then pcall(rareTry, obj) end
		end)
	end
end))
task.spawn(function()
	task.wait(3) -- let the GUI render first
	scanWorkspace(function(o)
		if RARE[o.Name] then pcall(rareTry, o, true) end
	end)
end)

-- ====================== FARM PAGE: STATS ======================
local stats = {picked = 0, byName = {}, activeSecs = 0}
local statsLabel

Settings.DX.onPicked = function(name)
	stats.picked = stats.picked + (1)
	stats.byName[name] = (stats.byName[name] or 0) + 1
end

do
	local card = createCard(132, 3)
	cardHeader(card, "📊  Farm Stats")
	statsLabel = mkLabel(card, "", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 40), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 54), 15, Color3.fromRGB(235, 222, 198), FONT)
	statsLabel.TextWrapped = true
	statsLabel.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
	statsLabel.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
	local reset = mkButton(card, "Reset stats", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 98), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 26), nil, nil, 15)
	reset.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		stats.picked = 0
		stats.byName = {}
		stats.activeSecs = 0
	end)
end

local function fmtHMS(s)
	s = math.floor(s)
	return string.format("%02d:%02d:%02d", math.floor(s / 3600), math.floor((s % 3600) / 60), s % 60)
end

local function refreshStats()
	local rate = "--"
	if stats.activeSecs >= 15 then
		rate = string.format("%d/hr", math.floor(stats.picked / (stats.activeSecs / 3600) + 0.5))
	end
	local names = {}
	for n in pairs(stats.byName) do names[#names + 1] = n end
	table.sort(names, function(a, b)
		if stats.byName[a] ~= stats.byName[b] then return stats.byName[a] > stats.byName[b] end
		return a < b
	end)
	local parts = {}
	for i = 1, math.min(3, #names) do
		parts[#parts + 1] = names[i] .. " x" .. stats.byName[names[i]]
	end
	statsLabel.Text = "Picked: " .. stats.picked .. "   •   Rate: " .. rate
		.. "\nFarm time: " .. fmtHMS(stats.activeSecs)
		.. "\nTop: " .. (#parts > 0 and table.concat(parts, ", ") or "none yet")
end

-- ====================== QOL PAGE: FPS BOOSTER ======================
local partOrig = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local fxOrig = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
local fpsSaved = nil
local fpsToken = 0
local fpsConn = nil
local FXC = {ParticleEmitter = true, Trail = true, Smoke = true, Fire = true, Sparkles = true}

local function fpsHandle(obj)
	if FXC[obj.ClassName] then
		if fxOrig[obj] == nil then fxOrig[obj] = obj.Enabled end
		obj.Enabled = false
	elseif obj:IsA("PostEffect") then
		if fxOrig[obj] == nil then fxOrig[obj] = obj.Enabled end
		obj.Enabled = false
	elseif obj:IsA("BasePart") and not obj:IsA("Terrain") then
		if not partOrig[obj] then partOrig[obj] = {obj.Material, obj.Reflectance, obj.CastShadow} end
		obj.Material = Enum.Material.SmoothPlastic
		obj.Reflectance = 0
		obj.CastShadow = false
	end
end
local function fpsSafe(obj) pcall(fpsHandle, obj) end

local function fpsOn()
	fpsToken = fpsToken + (1)
	local token = fpsToken
	pcall(function()
		if not fpsSaved then
			fpsSaved = {shadows = Lighting.GlobalShadows}
			pcall(function() fpsSaved.quality = settings().Rendering.QualityLevel end)
			pcall(function()
				local t = workspace.Terrain
				fpsSaved.wave = {t.WaterWaveSize, t.WaterWaveSpeed, t.WaterReflectance}
			end)
		end
		Lighting.GlobalShadows = false
	end)
	pcall(function() settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = Enum.QualityLevel.Level01 end)
	pcall(function()
		local t = workspace.Terrain
		t.WaterWaveSize = 0
		t.WaterWaveSpeed = 0
		t.WaterReflectance = 0
	end)
	for _, holder in ipairs({Lighting, workspace.CurrentCamera}) do
		for _, o in ipairs(holder:GetChildren()) do fpsSafe(o) end
	end
	if fpsConn then fpsConn:Disconnect() end
	fpsConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(fpsSafe)
	task.spawn(function()
		scanWorkspace(fpsSafe, function() return fpsToken == token end)
	end)
end

local function fpsOff()
	fpsToken = fpsToken + (1)
	local token = fpsToken
	if fpsConn then
		fpsConn:Disconnect()
		fpsConn = nil
	end
	pcall(function()
		if fpsSaved then
			Lighting.GlobalShadows = fpsSaved.shadows
			if fpsSaved.quality then settings().Rendering.QualityLevel = fpsSaved.quality end
			if fpsSaved.wave then
				local t = workspace.Terrain
				t.WaterWaveSize = fpsSaved.wave[1]
				t.WaterWaveSpeed = fpsSaved.wave[2]
				t.WaterReflectance = fpsSaved.wave[3]
			end
			fpsSaved = nil
		end
	end)
	task.spawn(function()
		local items = {}
		for o, v in pairs(partOrig) do items[#items + 1] = {o, v, true} end
		for o, v in pairs(fxOrig) do items[#items + 1] = {o, v, false} end
		for i, it in ipairs(items) do
			if fpsToken ~= token then return end
			local o = it[1]
			pcall(function()
				if it[3] then
					o.Material = it[2][1]
					o.Reflectance = it[2][2]
					o.CastShadow = it[2][3]
					partOrig[o] = nil
				else
					o.Enabled = it[2]
					fxOrig[o] = nil
				end
			end)
			if i % 300 == 0 then task.wait() end
		end
	end)
end

local fpsSw
do
	-- lives at the bottom of the Fruit Summoning (AFK) page
	local card, toggle, knob = createToggleCard(30, "⚡", 18, "FPS Booster", "Very good For Fruit Farm AFK")
	card.Parent = Content
	fpsSw = {on = false}
	function fpsSw.set(v)
		fpsSw.on = v and true or false
		S.fps = fpsSw.on
		setToggle(toggle, knob, fpsSw.on)
		if fpsSw.on then fpsOn() else fpsOff() end
		Settings.save()
	end
	toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() fpsSw.set(not fpsSw.on) end)
end

-- ====================== GUI SETTINGS PAGE: OPEN / CLOSE GUI HOTKEY ======================
HK.hide = {}
do
	local card, tog = createToggleCard(4, "🙈", 18, "Hide GUI", "Opens/Closes GUI")
	local scroll = GUISettingsPage:FindFirstChild("GuiSettingsScroll", true)
	card.Parent = scroll or QOLContent
	addHotkeySection(card, tog, "🙈", HK.hide)
	tog:Destroy() -- no on/off switch for this one, just the hotkey
	HK.hide.key = Enum.KeyCode.RightShift
	showHotkey(HK.hide)
	-- same thing that happens when you click the DANNY tab on the side
	HK.hide.action = function() setPanelOpen(not panelOpen) end
end

-- ====================== HOTKEY POPUPS (slide in from the right) ======================
-- Pressing a hotkey shows a small popup on the right side: "Fly Enabled" / "Fly Disabled", etc.
-- The popup is built from the same card as the rest of the GUI and is run through the GUI SETTINGS
-- theme (color + transparency), so it always matches your look.
xpcall(function()
	local TOP_Y, GAP, CARD_W, CARD_H = 16, 8, 270, 58
	local SHOW_SECS, MAX_SHOWN = 3, 4
	local GOOD, BAD = Color3.fromRGB(70, 225, 110), Color3.fromRGB(240, 85, 85)

	local host = Instance.new("Frame")
	host.Name = "HotkeyToasts"
	local PAD = 8
	-- left edge of the popup area: screen edge while the menu is closed, past the panel while it is open
	local function hostXFor(open) return open and (OPEN_X + PANEL_W + TAB_GAP + TAB_W + 10) or 0 end   -- closed: far left edge, open: past the menu
	local hx = hostXFor(panelOpen)
	host.Position = UDim2.new(0, hx, 0, 0)
	host.Size = UDim2.new(1, -hx, 1, 0)
	host.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	host.BorderSizePixel = 0
	host.Active = false
	host.ZIndex = 300
	host.ClipsDescendants = true
	-- NOTE: the host must NOT be marked NoFade/KeepColor. The transparency pass skips anything under a
	-- NoFade parent, which made the popups ignore the GUI SETTINGS transparency slider.
	host.Parent = ScreenGui

	local shown = {}   -- list of toast records, oldest first
	local byKey = {}   -- key -> record (same feature pressed again = update, don't stack)

	-- popups sit just UNDER the DANNY tab (the tab is 140 tall, centered on screen); extra ones stack downward
	local function yFor(i) return 70 + 14 + (i - 1) * (CARD_H + GAP) end
	local function relayout()
		for i, t in ipairs(shown) do
			TweenService:Create(t.card, TweenInfo.new(0.3, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
				Position = UDim2.new(0, PAD, 0.5, yFor(i)),
			}):Play()
		end
	end
	local function remove(t, fast)
		local idx = table.find(shown, t)
		if not idx then return end
		table.remove(shown, idx)
		if byKey[t.key] == t then byKey[t.key] = nil end
		local tw = TweenService:Create(t.card, TweenInfo.new(fast and 0.15 or 0.32, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, -(CARD_W + 30), t.card.Position.Y.Scale, t.card.Position.Y.Offset),
		})
		tw.Completed:Connect(function() if t.card.Parent then t.card:Destroy() end end)
		tw:Play()
		relayout()
	end

	local ON_COL, OFF_COL = Color3.fromRGB(46, 190, 85), Color3.fromRGB(88, 88, 94)   -- same as the real toggles
	local SUB_ON, SUB_OFF = Color3.fromRGB(92, 178, 116), Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)

	local function paint(t, enabled, text, animate)
		t.sub.Text = text
		local col = enabled and ON_COL or OFF_COL
		local info = TweenInfo.new(animate and 0.25 or 0)
		TweenService:Create(t.toggle, info, { BackgroundColor3 = col }):Play()
		TweenService:Create(t.tstroke, info, { Color = col }):Play()
		TweenService:Create(t.knob, info, { Position = enabled and UDim2.new(1, -25, 0.5, -11) or UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -11) }):Play()
	end

	local function arm(t)
		t.token = t.token + 1
		local my = t.token
		task.delay(SHOW_SECS, function()
			if t.token == my then remove(t) end
		end)
	end

	-- Built exactly like the toggle cards in the menu (same card, icon, title, sub text and on/off pill)
	local function show(key, icon, name, enabled, text)
		local t = byKey[key]
		if t and t.card.Parent then
			t.enabled, t.text = enabled, text
			if Settings.themeSubtree then pcall(Settings.themeSubtree, t.card) end   -- pick up any color / transparency change
			paint(t, enabled, text, true)
			-- tiny pop so you can see it changed
			TweenService:Create(t.scale, TweenInfo.new(0.08), { Scale = 1.04 }):Play()
			task.delay(0.08, function()
				if t.scale.Parent then TweenService:Create(t.scale, TweenInfo.new(0.18, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint), { Scale = 1 }):Play() end
			end)
			arm(t)
			return
		end

		while #shown >= MAX_SHOWN do remove(shown[1], true) end

		local card = createCard(CARD_H, 1, host, true)
		card.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
		card.Size = UDim2.new(0, CARD_W, 0, CARD_H)
		card.Position = UDim2.new(0, -(CARD_W + 30), 0.5, yFor(#shown + 1))

		local scale = Instance.new("UIScale")
		scale.Parent = card

		local ic = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 32, 1, 0)
		ic.Position = UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 0)
		ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		ic.Text = icon
		ic.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(190, 190, 196)
		ic.TextSize = 18
		ic.Parent = card

		local title = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		title.Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 22)
		title.Position = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 10)
		title.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		title.Text = name
		title.TextColor3 = CREAM
		title.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
		title.TextSize = 18
		title.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		title.TextScaled = true
		title.Parent = card
		local titleSize = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
		titleSize.MaxTextSize = 19
		titleSize.MinTextSize = 10
		titleSize.Parent = title

		local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 16)
		sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 31)
		sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		sub.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		sub.TextSize = 14
		sub.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)   -- same grey as the sub text on the menu cards (themed / faded the same way)
		sub.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		sub.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		sub.Parent = card

		-- the on/off pill (named like the real ones so the theme passes leave its colors alone)
		local toggle = Instance.new("Frame")
		toggle.Name = "OnOffToggle"
		toggle.Size = UDim2.new(0, 54, 0, 28)
		toggle.Position = UDim2.new(1, -70, 0.5, -14)
		toggle.BackgroundColor3 = enabled and OFF_COL or ON_COL
		toggle.BorderSizePixel = 0
		toggle.Parent = card
		addCorner(toggle, 14)
		local tstroke = addStroke(toggle, enabled and OFF_COL or ON_COL, 1.5, 0.5)

		local knob = Instance.new("Frame")
		knob.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
		knob.Position = enabled and UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -11) or UDim2.new(1, -25, 0.5, -11)
		knob.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
		knob.BorderSizePixel = 0
		knob.Parent = toggle
		addCorner(knob, 11)

		t = { key = key, card = card, scale = scale, sub = sub, toggle = toggle, tstroke = tstroke, knob = knob,
			token = 0, enabled = enabled, text = text }
		paint(t, enabled, text, false)
		-- paint() set the final look; put the pill back in the opposite spot so it visibly flips after sliding in
		toggle.BackgroundColor3 = enabled and OFF_COL or ON_COL
		tstroke.Color = toggle.BackgroundColor3
		knob.Position = enabled and UDim2.new(0, 3, 0.5, -11) or UDim2.new(1, -25, 0.5, -11)
		shown[#shown + 1] = t
		byKey[key] = t

		-- match the current GUI SETTINGS look (color + transparency) before it appears
		if Settings.themeSubtree then pcall(Settings.themeSubtree, card) end

		-- slide in from the left (smooth, no bounce), then the pill flips
		TweenService:Create(card, TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, PAD, 0.5, yFor(#shown)),
		}):Play()
		task.delay(0.3, function()
			if t.card.Parent then paint(t, t.enabled, t.text, true) end
		end)
		arm(t)
	end

	-- which state each hotkey reports
	local INFO = {
		[HK.esp]     = { "👥", "Player ESP",         function() return PlayerEspEnabled == true end },
		[HK.fly]     = { "🚀", "Fly",                function() return HK.fly.on == true end },
		[HK.noclip]  = { "👻", "Noclip",             function() return HK.noclip.on == true end },
		[HK.nofog]   = { "🌫", "Nofog",              function() return HK.nofog.on == true end },
		[HK.infjump] = { "🦘", "Inf Jump",           function() return HK.infjump.on == true end },
		[HK.infzoom] = { "🔭", "Inf Zoom",           function() return HK.infzoom.on == true end },
		[HK.nofall]  = { "🪂", "NoFallDamage",       function() return HK.nofall.on == true end },
		[HK.light]   = { "💡", "Let There Be Light", function() return HK.light.on == true end },
		[HK.hide]    = { "🙈", "GUI",                function() return panelOpen == true end, "Opened", "Hidden" },
	}

	Settings.toastsOn = true
	Settings.toastSlide = function(open)
		local x = hostXFor(open)
		TweenService:Create(host, TweenInfo.new(0.45, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, 0), Size = UDim2.new(1, -x, 1, 0),
		}):Play()
	end
	Settings.showToast = show
	Settings.hotkeyToast = function(hk)
		if not Settings.toastsOn then return end
		local info = INFO[hk]
		if not info then return end
		-- read the state a moment later so features that finish on the next frame report correctly
		task.delay(0.06, function()
			local on = info[3]()
			local word = on and (info[4] or "Enabled") or (info[5] or "Disabled")
			show(info[2], info[1], info[2], on, word)
		end)
	end
end, function(e) warn("[DannyGui] hotkey popups error: " .. tostring(e)) end)

-- ====================== TELEPORTS PAGE (saved spots, own box on Home) ======================
-- own page: Back button, title, scroll area that holds the Teleports card

do
local TeleportsPage = Pages:FindFirstChild("TeleportsPage")
local teleBack = Instance.new("TextButton")
teleBack.Size = UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 24)
teleBack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 14)
teleBack.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(31, 49, 61)
teleBack.Text = "‹  Back"
teleBack.TextColor3 = CREAM
teleBack.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
teleBack.TextSize = 17
teleBack.AutoButtonColor = false
teleBack.Parent = TeleportsPage
addCorner(teleBack, 5)
addStroke(teleBack, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.2)

teleBack.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showPage("home") end)

spotsCard = createCard(122, 1, tpPageScroll)
end
cardHeader(spotsCard, "📍  Custom Teleports Saved")
local nameBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
nameBox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -124, 0, 30)
nameBox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 42)
nameBox.BackgroundColor3 = FIELD
nameBox.Text = ""

nameBox.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 118)
