addCorner(nameBox, 5)
addStroke(nameBox, SILVER_DIM, 1.2, 0.1)
local nbPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
nbPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 8)
nbPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 8)
nbPad.Parent = nameBox
local saveBtn = mkButton(spotsCard, "Save here", UDim2.new(1, -104, 0, 42), UDim2.new(0, 90, 0, 30), nil, nil, 15)
local listFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
listFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
listFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
listFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 80)
listFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 32)
listFrame.Parent = spotsCard

local rebuildSpots

-- own box for the pinned spots, sits right under the Custom Teleports Saved box
local pvpCard = createCard(100, 2, spotsCard.Parent)
cardHeader(pvpCard, "\226\154\148\239\184\143  PVP SPOTS")
local pvpList = Instance.new("Frame")
pvpList.BackgroundTransparency = 1
pvpList.BorderSizePixel = 0
pvpList.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 44)
pvpList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 62)
pvpList.Parent = pvpCard

-- ---------- pinned preset spots: TP + WAYPOINT buttons, no X ----------
-- (kept in one table so the script doesn't run out of local variables)
local SP = {active = nil}
Settings.SP = SP -- shared so the Bosses page can use the same waypoint
SP.PRESETS = {"Durana PVP BIG", "Durana PVP Spot", "Fort Melo PVP Spot", "Snow PVP", "Akatsuki PVP Spot"}
SP.key = function(s) return (tostring(s):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")) end
SP.byKey, SP.order = {}, {}
for i, nm in ipairs(SP.PRESETS) do
    SP.byKey[SP.key(nm)] = nm
    SP.order[nm] = i
end
-- old saved name "Akatski PVP" (and spelling variants) becomes the pinned "Akatsuki PVP Spot", keeping its coordinates
for _, old in ipairs({"Akatski PVP", "Akatski PVP Spot", "Akatsuki PVP"}) do
    SP.byKey[SP.key(old)] = "Akatsuki PVP Spot"
end
SP.canon = function(name) return SP.byKey[SP.key(name)] end

-- makes sure every preset exists; an already-saved spot with the same name keeps its coordinates
-- (and gets the clean name, e.g. "Durana PVP SPot" -> "Durana PVP Spot")
SP.ensure = function()
    for i = #S.spots, 1, -1 do
        local e = S.spots[i]
        local k = SP.key(e.name)
        if k == "hereyes" or (e.unset and not SP.byKey[k]) then table.remove(S.spots, i) end
    end
    local have = {}
    for _, sp in ipairs(S.spots) do
        local c = SP.canon(sp.name)
        if c then
            sp.name = c
            have[c] = true
        end
    end
    for _, nm in ipairs(SP.PRESETS) do
        if not have[nm] then table.insert(S.spots, {name = nm, unset = true}) end
    end
end

SP.clear = function()
    local w = SP.active
    SP.active = nil
    if w then pcall(w.stop) end
end

-- fancy waypoint: tall glowing beam, pulsing ground rings, floating marker with live distance,
-- and a glowing guide line from you to the spot. Clears itself when you arrive.
SP.start = function(sp)
    SP.clear()
    local pos = Vector3.new(sp.x, sp.y, sp.z)
    local WP = Color3.fromRGB(90, 210, 255)
    local folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = "DannyWaypoint"
    folder.Parent = workspace.CurrentCamera or workspace

    local function mkPart(size, cf, color, transp, shape)
        local p = Instance.new("Part")
        p.Anchored = true
        p.CanCollide = false
        p.CanQuery = false
        p.CanTouch = false
        p.CastShadow = false
        p.Material = Enum.Material.Neon
        p.Color = color
        p.Transparency = transp
        if shape then p.Shape = shape end
        p.Size = size
        p.CFrame = cf
        p.Parent = folder
        return p
    end

    local flat = CFrame.Angles(0, 0, math.rad(90))
    local anchor = mkPart(Vector3.new(1, 1, 1), CFrame.new(pos), WP, 1)
    local beam = mkPart(Vector3.new(0.8, 300, 0.8), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 150, 0)), WP, 0.4)
    mkPart(Vector3.new(0.3, 300, 0.3), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 150, 0)), Color3.new(1, 1, 1), 0.1)
    mkPart(Vector3.new(0.2, 4, 4), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.2, 0)) * flat, WP, 0.5, Enum.PartType.Cylinder)
    local rings = {
        mkPart(Vector3.new(0.3, 6, 6), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0)) * flat, WP, 0.3, Enum.PartType.Cylinder),
        mkPart(Vector3.new(0.3, 6, 6), CFrame.new(pos + Vector3.new(0, 0.3, 0)) * flat, WP, 0.3, Enum.PartType.Cylinder),
    }

    -- floating marker (arrow, name, distance)
    local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    bb.Adornee = anchor
    bb.Size = UDim2.fromOffset(220, 72)
    bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 12, 0)
    bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
    bb.MaxDistance = math.huge
    bb.ResetOnSpawn = false
    bb.Parent = anchor
    local function bbLabel(text, y, h, ts, color)
        local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
        l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
        l.Position = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y)
        l.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, h)
        l.Text = text
        l.TextColor3 = color
        l.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansBold
        l.TextSize = ts
        l.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.3
        l.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(0, 20, 30)
        l.Parent = bb
        return l
    end
    bbLabel("\226\150\188", 0, 26, 26, WP)
    bbLabel(sp.name, 26, 24, 22, Color3.new(1, 1, 1))
    local distL = bbLabel("", 50, 20, 17, Color3.fromRGB(190, 235, 255))

    -- glowing guide line from you to the spot
    local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment")
    a1.Parent = anchor
    local line = Instance.new("Beam")
    line.Attachment1 = a1
    line.Color = ColorSequence.new(WP)
    line.LightEmission = 1
    line.FaceCamera = true
    line.Width0 = 0.35
    line.Width1 = 0.35
    line.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.35)
    line.Segments = 1
    line.Parent = anchor

    local a0, curRoot
    local conn
    local lastAnim, lastDist = 0, 0
    conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local t = os.clock()
        if t - lastAnim >= 0.033 then -- [perf] ~30Hz animation
            lastAnim = t
            beam.Transparency = 0.4 + 0.12 * math.sin(t * 3)
            bb.StudsOffset = Vector3.new(0, 12 + math.sin(t * 2) * 0.7, 0)
            for k, ring in ipairs(rings) do
                local ph = (t * 0.7 + (k - 1) * 0.5) % 1
                local d = 4 + ph * 16
                ring.Size = Vector3.new(0.3, d, d)
                ring.Transparency = 0.15 + 0.85 * ph
            end
        end
        local root = rootOf()
        if root ~= curRoot then
            if a0 then pcall(function() a0:Destroy() end) a0 = nil end
            curRoot = root
            if root then
                a0 = Instance.new("Attachment")
                a0.Parent = root
                line.Attachment0 = a0
            else
                line.Attachment0 = nil
            end
        end
        if root then
            local d = (root.Position - pos).Magnitude
            if t - lastDist >= 0.15 then -- [perf] label ~7Hz
                lastDist = t
                distL.Text = string.format("%d studs", math.floor(d + 0.5))
            end
            if d < 7 then
                SP.clear()
                pcall(rebuildSpots)
            end
        end
    end)

    SP.active = {sp = sp, stop = function()
        conn:Disconnect()
        if a0 then pcall(function() a0:Destroy() end) end
        folder:Destroy()
    end}
end

rebuildSpots = function()
    for _, ch in ipairs(listFrame:GetChildren()) do ch:Destroy() end
    for _, ch in ipairs(pvpList:GetChildren()) do ch:Destroy() end
    SP.ensure()
    SP.rows = {}
    local pinned, custom = {}, {}
    for _, sp in ipairs(S.spots) do
        if SP.canon(sp.name) then pinned[#pinned + 1] = sp else custom[#custom + 1] = sp end
    end
    table.sort(pinned, function(a, b) return SP.order[a.name] < SP.order[b.name] end)

    local PIN_H, ROW_H = 62, 32
    local customH = (#custom > 0) and (#custom * ROW_H) or 30
    local total = customH
    listFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, total)
    spotsCard.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 80 + total + 10)
    local pinTotal = #pinned * PIN_H
    pvpList.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, pinTotal)
    pvpCard.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 44 + pinTotal + 8)

    local y = 0
    for _, sp in ipairs(pinned) do
        local isSet = sp.x ~= nil
        SP.mkHL(pvpList, y, PIN_H - 6, sp)
        mkLabel(pvpList, isSet and sp.name or (sp.name .. "  (not set)"), UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, y), UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 24), 16, isSet and CREAM or DIM, FONT_B)
        if isSet then
            local tp = mkButton(pvpList, "TP", UDim2.new(0.62, 3, 0, y + 27), UDim2.new(0.38, -3, 0, 28), nil, nil, 15)
            local active = SP.active and SP.active.sp == sp
            local wp = mkButton(pvpList, active and "CLEAR WAYPOINT" or "WAYPOINT", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y + 27), UDim2.new(0.62, -3, 0, 28), active and BTN_RED or nil, active and BTN_RED_HOVER or nil, 15)
            tp.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local root = rootOf()
                if root then pcall(function() root.CFrame = CFrame.new(sp.x, sp.y, sp.z) end) end
            end)
            wp.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                if SP.active and SP.active.sp == sp then
                    SP.clear()
                else
                    SP.start(sp)
                end
                rebuildSpots()
            end)
        else
            local setBtn = mkButton(pvpList, "Set here", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, y + 27), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 28), nil, nil, 15)
            setBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                local root = rootOf()
                if not root then return end
                local p = root.Position
                sp.x, sp.y, sp.z, sp.unset = p.X, p.Y, p.Z, nil
                rebuildSpots()
                Settings.save()
            end)
        end
        y = y + PIN_H
    end

    y = 0
    if #custom == 0 then
        local e = mkLabel(listFrame, "Your own spots show here. Stand somewhere, then press Save here.", UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, y), UDim2.new(1, -4, 0, 30), 14, DIM, FONT)
        e.TextWrapped = true
        e.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
        return
    end
    for _, sp in ipairs(custom) do
        SP.mkHL(listFrame, y, 28, sp)
        mkLabel(listFrame, sp.name, UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, y), UDim2.new(1, -152, 0, 28), 16, CREAM, FONT_B)
        local tp = mkButton(listFrame, "TP", UDim2.new(1, -146, 0, y), UDim2.new(0, 52, 0, 28), nil, nil, 15)
        local wpActive = SP.active and SP.active.sp == sp
        local wpBtn = mkButton(listFrame, wpActive and "CLEAR" or "WP", UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, y), UDim2.new(0, 56, 0, 28), wpActive and BTN_RED or nil, wpActive and BTN_RED_HOVER or nil, wpActive and 13 or 15)
        local del = mkButton(listFrame, "X", UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, y), UDim2.new(0, 28, 0, 28), BTN_RED, BTN_RED_HOVER, 15)
        wpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            if SP.active and SP.active.sp == sp then
                SP.clear()
            else
                SP.start(sp)
            end
            rebuildSpots()
        end)
        tp.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local root = rootOf()
            if root then pcall(function() root.CFrame = CFrame.new(sp.x, sp.y, sp.z) end) end
        end)
        del.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
            local idx = table.find(S.spots, sp)
            if idx then table.remove(S.spots, idx) end
            rebuildSpots()
            Settings.save()
        end)
        y = y + ROW_H
    end
end

SP.rows = {}

-- soft highlight behind a spot's row (used when a search result is picked)
SP.mkHL = function(parent, y, h, sp)
    local hl = Instance.new("Frame")
    hl.Name = "SpotHL"
    hl.Position = UDim2.new(0, -4, 0, y - 2)
    hl.Size = UDim2.new(1, 8, 0, h + 2)
    hl.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
    hl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    hl.BorderSizePixel = 0
    hl.Parent = parent
    addCorner(hl, 6)
    mark(hl)
    SP.rows[sp] = hl
end

SP.flash = function(sp)
    local hl = SP.rows[sp]
    if not hl or not hl.Parent then return end
    task.spawn(function()
        for _ = 1, 3 do
            if not hl.Parent then return end
            hl.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
            tween(hl, 0.5, {BackgroundTransparency = 1})
            task.wait(0.6)
        end
    end)
end

-- picking a result in the main GUI search: scroll to that spot in its box, flash it. You then press TP / WAYPOINT yourself.
SP.jump = function(sp)
    task.delay(0.1, function()
        local hl = SP.rows[sp]
        local scroll = spotsCard.Parent
        if not hl or not hl.Parent or not scroll then return end
        local target = hl.AbsolutePosition.Y - scroll.AbsolutePosition.Y + scroll.CanvasPosition.Y - 10
        tween(scroll, 0.35, {CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, target))})
        SP.flash(sp)
    end)
end

-- new buttons are created with the default colors, so after every rebuild re-run the GUI look
-- (color / saturation / brightness / transparency from GUI Settings) so they match everything else
do
    local rawRebuild = rebuildSpots
    rebuildSpots = function()
        rawRebuild()
        pcall(refreshGuiLook)
    end
end
rebuildSpots()
Settings.DX.getSpots = function() return S.spots end
Settings.DX.spotIsPinned = function(sp) return SP.canon(sp.name) ~= nil end
Settings.DX.jumpSpot = function(sp) SP.jump(sp) end

saveBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
    local root = rootOf()
    if not root then return end
    local nm = nameBox.Text
    nm = nm:gsub("^%s+", "")
    nm = nm:gsub("%s+$", "")
    nm = nm:sub(1, 18)
    local p = root.Position
    -- typing a preset name (e.g. "Snow PVP") sets / re-sets that pinned spot
    local canon = (nm ~= "") and SP.canon(nm) or nil
    if canon then
        for _, sp in ipairs(S.spots) do
            if sp.name == canon then
                sp.x, sp.y, sp.z, sp.unset = p.X, p.Y, p.Z, nil
                break
            end
        end
    else
        local customN = 0
        for _, sp in ipairs(S.spots) do
            if not SP.canon(sp.name) then customN = customN + 1 end
        end
        if customN >= 12 then
            flash(saveBtn, "Full (12)", "Save here")
            return
        end
        if nm == "" then nm = "Spot " .. (customN + 1) end
        table.insert(S.spots, {name = nm, x = p.X, y = p.Y, z = p.Z})
    end
    nameBox.Text = ""
    rebuildSpots()
    flash(saveBtn, "Saved!", "Save here")
    Settings.save()
end)

-- ====================== CHAKRA POINTS ======================
-- Every chakra point with a WAYPOINT and a TP button (coordinates are built in below).
pcall(function()
    local CP = {}
    Settings.CP = CP
    S.chakra = S.chakra or {}

    -- every chakra point, in the order they show in the box
    CP.NAMES = {
        "Sorythia Village", "Windy Plains 1", "Biyo Bay", "Rosemary Pit",
        "Training Grounds", "Windy Plains 2", "Windy Plains 3",
        "Rain Village",
        "Passageway", "The Oasis", "Durana Village",
        "Serpent's Cove Entrance", "Foot of Serpent's Cove",
        "The Expanse", "Fort Melo", "Combat Hall",
        "Chakra's Edge",
        "Rose Bay", "Arctic Cove",
        "Twin Peaks", "The Ascent", "Permafrost Plain", "Snow Village",
        "Flaming Depths",
    }
    CP.key = function(n) return (tostring(n or ""):lower():gsub("[^%w]", "")) end
    CP.order = {}
    for i, nm in ipairs(CP.NAMES) do CP.order[CP.key(nm)] = i end
    -- built-in coordinates (collected in-game)
    CP.DEFAULTS = {
        {"Sorythia Village", 123.4, -36.8, -69.4},
        {"Windy Plains 1", -374.9, -134.8, 189.4},
        {"Biyo Bay", -837.1, -128.3, -509.6},
        {"Rosemary Pit", -50.4, -180.4, -657.8},
        {"Training Grounds", 398.5, -214.1, 837.0},
        {"Windy Plains 2", -698.0, -214.3, 514.6},
        {"Windy Plains 3", -678.9, -294.8, 875.4},
        {"Rain Village", -2026.9, -10.9, 328.0},
        {"Passageway", 688.6, 58.2, -329.1},
        {"The Oasis", 1494.4, -120.7, 166.1},
        {"Durana Village", 1884.9, -120.3, 1018.8},
        {"Serpent's Cove Entrance", 850.0, -288.7, 1235.9},
        {"Foot of Serpent's Cove", 1532.8, -490.1, 1150.9},
        {"The Expanse", -300.6, 63.4, -1260.0},
        {"Fort Melo", 28.9, 215.8, -2334.9},
        {"Combat Hall", 730.0, 84.6, -1309.0},
        {"Chakra's Edge", 2638.0, -157.8, -499.0},
        {"Rose Bay", -1914.0, -144.2, -1277.8},
        {"Arctic Cove", -2238.0, -70.6, -2188.7},
        {"Twin Peaks", -3375.2, 274.4, -2828.9},
        {"The Ascent", -4038.9, 223.9, -2935.7},
        {"Permafrost Plain", -3345.1, -13.1, -3596.3},
        {"Snow Village", -2750.0, -50.9, -5028.1},
        {"Flaming Depths", 2840.9, 217.4, -9979.2},
    }
    CP.def = {}
    for _, d in ipairs(CP.DEFAULTS) do CP.def[CP.key(d[1])] = d end
    -- name of the Chakra Point closest to a world position (used by the "TAKE <name>" waypoint buttons)
    Settings.nearestChakra = function(pos)
        if not pos then return nil end
        local list = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(S.chakra or {}) do
            if e.x then list[#list + 1] = { e.name, e.x, e.y, e.z } end
        end
        if #list == 0 then list = CP.DEFAULTS end
        local best, bd
        for _, e in ipairs(list) do
            local d = (Vector3.new(e[2], e[3], e[4]) - pos).Magnitude
            if not bd or d < bd then bd, best = d, e[1] end
        end
        return best
    end
    CP.ensure = function()
        local have = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(S.chakra) do
            have[CP.key(e.name)] = true
            local d = CP.def[CP.key(e.name)]
            if not e.x and d then e.x, e.y, e.z, e.unset = d[2], d[3], d[4], nil end
        end
        for _, nm in ipairs(CP.NAMES) do
            if not have[CP.key(nm)] then
                local d = CP.def[CP.key(nm)]
                if d then
                    S.chakra[#S.chakra + 1] = {name = nm, x = d[2], y = d[3], z = d[4]}
                else
                    S.chakra[#S.chakra + 1] = {name = nm, unset = true}
                end
            end
        end
    end
    CP.count = function()
        local n = 0
        for _, e in ipairs(S.chakra) do if e.x then n = n + 1 end end
        return n
    end

    CP.card = createCard(120, 3, spotsCard.Parent)
    cardHeader(CP.card, "🌀  CHAKRA POINTS")
    -- ---------- closest-chakra live waypoint (one by one) ----------
    CP.RADIUS = 8                        -- studs: get this close and the point is marked Found
    CP.GREEN = Color3.fromRGB(110, 235, 140)
    CP.WPCOL = Color3.fromRGB(90, 210, 255)
    CP.running = false
    CP.loopId = 0
    CP.target = nil                      -- the chakra entry the live waypoint is on right now
    CP.found = {}                        -- [key] = true once you got within CP.RADIUS
    CP.dist = {}                         -- [key] = last measured distance (studs)
    local SHOW_TXT, SHOW_TXT_ON = "Show Closest Chakra Points", "Live Waypoint ON"
    local LIST_Y = 212

    CP.ctrl = Instance.new("Frame")
    CP.ctrl.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    CP.ctrl.BorderSizePixel = 0
    CP.ctrl.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 42)
    CP.ctrl.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 32)
    CP.ctrl.Parent = CP.card

    local function fitText(b, maxSize)
        b.TextScaled = true
        local c = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
        c.MaxTextSize = maxSize or 15
        c.MinTextSize = 8
        c.Parent = b
    end
    -- layout:  [ RESET ]  [ PAUSE ]  [ Show Closest Chakra Points ]
    CP.resetBtn = mkButton(CP.ctrl, "RESET", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.22, -3, 1, 0), nil, nil, 15)
    CP.pauseBtn = mkButton(CP.ctrl, "PAUSE", UDim2.new(0.22, 3, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.28, -6, 1, 0), BTN_RED, BTN_RED_HOVER, 15)
    CP.showBtn = mkButton(CP.ctrl, SHOW_TXT, UDim2.new(0.5, 3, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.5, -3, 1, 0), nil, nil, 15)
    fitText(CP.resetBtn); fitText(CP.showBtn); fitText(CP.pauseBtn)

    CP.markAllBtn = mkButton(CP.card, "Mark All Chakra Points as Found", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 80), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28), nil, nil, 15)
    fitText(CP.markAllBtn)

    CP.status = mkLabel(CP.card, "", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 112), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 20), 14, DIM, FONT)
    CP.status:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)

    -- ---------- TP TO ALL POINTS:  [ TP TO ALL POINTS ] [ PAUSE ] [ STOP ] ----------
    do
        local sep = Instance.new("Frame")
        sep.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
        sep.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 136)
        sep.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER_DIM
        sep.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
        sep.BorderSizePixel = 0
        sep.Parent = CP.card
    end
    CP.tpRow = Instance.new("Frame")
    CP.tpRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    CP.tpRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
    CP.tpRow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 144)
    CP.tpRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 32)
    CP.tpRow.Parent = CP.card
    -- default button look, so the GUI Settings theme / transparency applies to all three
    CP.tpStartBtn = mkButton(CP.tpRow, "TP TO ALL POINTS", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.46, -3, 1, 0), nil, nil, 15)
    CP.tpPauseBtn = mkButton(CP.tpRow, "PAUSE", UDim2.new(0.46, 3, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.27, -6, 1, 0), nil, nil, 15)
    CP.tpStopBtn = mkButton(CP.tpRow, "STOP", UDim2.new(0.73, 3, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.27, -3, 1, 0), nil, nil, 15)
    fitText(CP.tpStartBtn); fitText(CP.tpPauseBtn); fitText(CP.tpStopBtn)

    CP.tpStatus = mkLabel(CP.card, "Teleports to every Chakra Point, presses E twice at each", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 180), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 18), 14, DIM, FONT)
    CP.tpStatus:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
    CP.tpTrack = Instance.new("Frame")
    CP.tpTrack.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 202)
    CP.tpTrack.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 6)
    CP.tpTrack.BackgroundColor3 = FIELD
    CP.tpTrack.BorderSizePixel = 0
    CP.tpTrack.Parent = CP.card
    addCorner(CP.tpTrack, 3)
    CP.tpFill = Instance.new("Frame")
    CP.tpFill.Size = UDim2.new(0, 0, 1, 0)
    CP.tpFill.BackgroundColor3 = CP.GREEN
    CP.tpFill.BorderSizePixel = 0
    CP.tpFill:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
    CP.tpFill.Parent = CP.tpTrack
    addCorner(CP.tpFill, 3)

    CP.listFrame = Instance.new("Frame")
    CP.listFrame.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    CP.listFrame.BorderSizePixel = 0
    CP.listFrame.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, LIST_Y)
    CP.listFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 30)
    CP.listFrame.Parent = CP.card

    CP.counts = function()
        local total, got = 0, 0
        for _, e in ipairs(S.chakra) do
            if e.x then
                total = total + 1
                if CP.found[CP.key(e.name)] then got = got + 1 end
            end
        end
        return got, total
    end

    CP.updateStatus = function()
        local got, total = CP.counts()
        if CP.running and CP.target then
            local d = CP.dist[CP.key(CP.target.name)]
            CP.status.TextColor3 = CP.WPCOL
            CP.status.Text = "Next: " .. CP.target.name .. (d and ("  -  " .. math.floor(d + 0.5) .. " studs") or "")
                .. "   |   " .. got .. " / " .. total .. " found"
        elseif total > 0 and got >= total then
            CP.status.TextColor3 = CP.GREEN
            CP.status.Text = "All " .. total .. " found!  Press RESET to go again"
        elseif got > 0 then
            CP.status.TextColor3 = DIM
            CP.status.Text = "Paused  -  " .. got .. " / " .. total .. " found"
        else
            CP.status.TextColor3 = DIM
            CP.status.Text = "Press Show Closest Chakra Points to start"
        end
    end

    -- turns ONE row green in place (cheap) - used while teleporting so the GUI doesn't hitch
    CP.markFast = function(e)
        local k = CP.key(e.name)
        CP.found[k] = true
        local ref = CP.rowRefs and CP.rowRefs[k]
        if ref then
            pcall(function()
                if ref.pulse then ref.pulse:Cancel() end
                ref.dot.BackgroundColor3 = CP.GREEN
                ref.ring.Color = CP.GREEN
                ref.ring.Thickness = 1.5
                ref.ring.Transparency = 0.35
                if ref.name then
                    ref.name.TextColor3 = CP.GREEN
                    ref.name:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
                end
            end)
        end
        CP.updateStatus()
    end

    -- removes the live waypoint, but only if it is the one this tracker put down
    CP.dropWaypoint = function()
        if CP.target and SP.active and SP.active.sp == CP.target then SP.clear() end
        CP.target = nil
    end

    CP.sorted = function()
        local rows = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(S.chakra) do rows[#rows + 1] = e end
        table.sort(rows, function(a, b)
            local oa, ob = CP.order[CP.key(a.name)] or 1000, CP.order[CP.key(b.name)] or 1000
            if oa ~= ob then return oa < ob end
            return a.name < b.name
        end)
        return rows
    end

    CP.rebuild = function()
        CP.ensure()
        CP.rowRefs = {}
        for _, ch in ipairs(CP.listFrame:GetChildren()) do ch:Destroy() end
        local rows = CP.sorted()
        local ROW_H = 32
        local y = 0
        for _, e in ipairs(rows) do
            local isSet = e.x ~= nil
            local isFound = isSet and CP.found[CP.key(e.name)] == true
            local isNext = CP.running and CP.target == e
            local nameCol = isSet and CREAM or DIM
            if isFound then nameCol = CP.GREEN elseif isNext then nameCol = CP.WPCOL end
            -- status circle at the start of the row: blue = going to it now, green = found, red = not found yet
            -- (KeepColor stops the GUI theme / transparency pass from repainting these)
            do
                local col = isFound and CP.GREEN or (isNext and CP.WPCOL or (isSet and Color3.fromRGB(235, 60, 60) or Color3.fromRGB(110, 110, 118)))
                local dot = Instance.new("Frame")
                dot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 11)
                dot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, y + 9)
                dot.BorderSizePixel = 0
                dot.BackgroundColor3 = col
                dot:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
                dot.Parent = CP.listFrame
                addCorner(dot, 6)
                -- soft glow ring
                local ring = addStroke(dot, col, 1.5, 0.35)
                ring:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
                -- light spot so it reads as a glossy bead
                local shine = Instance.new("Frame")
                shine.Size = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 4)
                shine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, 1)
                shine.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.new(1, 1, 1)
                shine.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
                shine.BorderSizePixel = 0
                shine:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)
                shine.Parent = dot
                addCorner(shine, 2)
                local pulse
                if isNext then
                    -- the blue one softly pulses so you can spot it straight away
                    pcall(function()
                        pulse = TweenService:Create(ring, TweenInfo.new(0.8, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), {Thickness = 3, Transparency = 0.85})
                        pulse:Play()
                    end)
                end
                CP.rowRefs[CP.key(e.name)] = {dot = dot, ring = ring, pulse = pulse}
            end
            local nl = mkLabel(CP.listFrame, e.name, UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, y), UDim2.new(1, -198, 0, 28), 15, nameCol, FONT_B)
            if isFound or isNext then nl:SetAttribute("KeepText", true) end
            if CP.rowRefs[CP.key(e.name)] then CP.rowRefs[CP.key(e.name)].name = nl end
            nl.TextScaled = true
            local nlc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
            nlc.MaxTextSize = 15
            nlc.MinTextSize = 8
            nlc.Parent = nl
            if isSet then
                local active = SP.active and SP.active.sp == e
                local wp = mkButton(CP.listFrame, active and "CLEAR" or "WAYPOINT", UDim2.new(1, -172, 0, y), UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 28), active and BTN_RED or nil, active and BTN_RED_HOVER or nil, 14)
                local tp = mkButton(CP.listFrame, "TP", UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, y), UDim2.new(0, 64, 0, 28), nil, nil, 15)
                tp.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    local r = rootOf()
                    if r then pcall(function() r.CFrame = CFrame.new(e.x, e.y + 3, e.z) end) end
                end)
                wp.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    if SP.active and SP.active.sp == e then SP.clear() else SP.start(e) end
                    CP.rebuild()
                    rebuildSpots()
                end)
            else
                local setBtn = mkButton(CP.listFrame, "Set here", UDim2.new(1, -108, 0, y), UDim2.new(0, 108, 0, 28), nil, nil, 14)
                setBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
                    local r = rootOf()
                    if not r then return end
                    local pos = r.Position
                    e.x, e.y, e.z, e.unset = pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z, nil
                    CP.rebuild()
                    Settings.save()
                end)
            end
            y = y + ROW_H
        end
        CP.listFrame.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, y)
        CP.card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, LIST_Y + y + 8)
        CP.updateStatus()
        pcall(refreshGuiLook)
    end

    CP.stop = function()
        CP.running = false
        CP.loopId = CP.loopId + 1
        CP.dropWaypoint()
        CP.showBtn.Text = SHOW_TXT
        CP.rebuild()
        pcall(rebuildSpots)
    end

    -- one pass: measure, mark Found anything within 8 studs, and keep the live waypoint on the
    -- closest point that is still not found (it moves on by itself, one point at a time)
    CP.tick = function()
        if not CP.running then return end
        local r = rootOf()
        if not r then return end
        local pos = r.Position
        local changed = false
        for _, e in ipairs(S.chakra) do
            if e.x then
                local k = CP.key(e.name)
                local d = (Vector3.new(e.x, e.y, e.z) - pos).Magnitude
                CP.dist[k] = d
                if d <= CP.RADIUS and not CP.found[k] then
                    CP.found[k] = true
                    changed = true
                end
            end
        end

        local tgt = CP.target
        if tgt and CP.found[CP.key(tgt.name)] then
            CP.dropWaypoint()
            tgt = nil
            changed = true
        end
        -- the waypoint disappeared without you arriving (cleared by hand / replaced by another one)
        if tgt and not (SP.active and SP.active.sp == tgt) then
            CP.stop()
            return
        end

        if not tgt then
            local best, bestD
            for _, e in ipairs(S.chakra) do
                if e.x and not CP.found[CP.key(e.name)] then
                    local d = CP.dist[CP.key(e.name)]
                    if d and (not bestD or d < bestD) then best, bestD = e, d end
                end
            end
            if not best then
                -- nothing left to find
                CP.running = false
                CP.loopId = CP.loopId + 1
                CP.showBtn.Text = SHOW_TXT
                CP.rebuild()
                pcall(rebuildSpots)
                return
            end
            CP.target = best
            SP.start(best)
            changed = true
            pcall(rebuildSpots)
        end

        if changed then CP.rebuild() else CP.updateStatus() end
    end

    CP.showBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if CP.running then return end
        local got, total = CP.counts()
        if total > 0 and got >= total then
            CP.rebuild()
            flash(CP.showBtn, "All found - RESET", SHOW_TXT)
            return
        end
        CP.running = true
        CP.loopId = CP.loopId + 1
        local myId = CP.loopId
        CP.showBtn.Text = SHOW_TXT_ON
        pcall(CP.tick)
        task.spawn(function()
            while not stopped and CP.running and CP.loopId == myId do
                task.wait(0.2)
                if CP.running and CP.loopId == myId then pcall(CP.tick) end
            end
        end)
    end)

    -- PAUSE: the live waypoint disappears; press Show Closest Chakra Points to bring it back
    CP.pauseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if not CP.running then return end
        CP.stop()
        flash(CP.pauseBtn, "Paused", "PAUSE")
    end)

    -- Mark All: every Chakra Point counts as found (tracker ends, waypoint goes away)
    CP.markAllBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        for _, e in ipairs(S.chakra) do
            if e.x then CP.found[CP.key(e.name)] = true end
        end
        if CP.running then CP.stop() else CP.rebuild() end
        flash(CP.markAllBtn, "All marked as found!", "Mark All Chakra Points as Found")
    end)

    -- ---------- TP TO ALL POINTS logic ----------
    -- at every point: teleport to its exact spot, press E, wait 1 sec, press E again, and stay there
    -- until 5 sec have passed in total, then go to the next one. PAUSE freezes the timer, STOP ends it.
    CP.TA = {running = false, paused = false, token = 0}
    local TA = CP.TA
    local STAY, E_GAP = 5.0, 1.0
    local START_TXT = "TP TO ALL POINTS"

    local function pressE()
        pcall(function()
            VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
            task.wait(0.08)
            VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, Enum.KeyCode.E, false, game)
        end)
    end
    local function setProgress(frac)
        tween(CP.tpFill, 0.12, {Size = UDim2.new(math.clamp(frac, 0, 1), 0, 1, 0)})
    end
    local function tpIdle(msg, col, keepBar)
        TA.running, TA.paused = false, false
        CP.tpStartBtn.Text = START_TXT
        CP.tpPauseBtn.Text = "PAUSE"
        CP.tpStatus.Text = msg
        CP.tpStatus.TextColor3 = col or DIM
        if not keepBar then setProgress(0) end
    end

    CP.tpStartBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if TA.running then return end
        local list = {}
        for _, e in ipairs(CP.sorted()) do
            if e.x then list[#list + 1] = e end
        end
        if #list == 0 then return end
        TA.running, TA.paused = true, false
        TA.token = TA.token + 1
        local my = TA.token
        local function alive() return not stopped and TA.running and TA.token == my end
        task.spawn(function()
            for i, e in ipairs(list) do
                if not alive() then return end
                CP.tpStartBtn.Text = "TP-ing  " .. i .. " / " .. #list
                CP.tpStatus.TextColor3 = CP.WPCOL
                CP.tpStatus.Text = "Point " .. i .. " / " .. #list .. "  -  " .. e.name
                local target = CFrame.new(e.x, e.y, e.z)      -- exact spot of the Chakra Point
                local function goThere()
                    local r = rootOf()
                    if r then
                        pcall(function()
                            r.CFrame = target
                            r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
                            r.AssemblyAngularVelocity = Vector3.zero
                        end)
                    end
                    return r
                end
                -- wait here while paused, then teleport
                while alive() and TA.paused do task.wait(0.1) end
                if not alive() then return end
                goThere()
                CP.markFast(e)

                local active, last = 0, os.clock()
                local pressed2 = false
                pressE()                                        -- E #1 right on arrival
                local nextCheck, nextBar = 0.5, 0
                while alive() and active < STAY do
                    task.wait(0.1)
                    local now = os.clock()
                    if not TA.paused then active = active + (now - last) end
                    last = now
                    if not TA.paused then
                        if not pressed2 and active >= E_GAP then
                            pressed2 = true
                            pressE()                            -- E #2 after 1 sec
                        end
                        -- stay on the point for the whole 5 sec (only re-place you if you got pushed far off)
                        if active >= nextCheck then
                            nextCheck = active + 0.5
                            local r = rootOf()
                            if r and (r.Position - target.Position).Magnitude > 6 then goThere() end
                        end
                    end
                    if now >= nextBar then
                        nextBar = now + 0.25
                        setProgress((i - 1 + math.min(active / STAY, 1)) / #list)
                    end
                end
            end
            if alive() then
                setProgress(1)
                tpIdle("Done  -  visited all " .. #list .. " Chakra Points", CP.GREEN, true)
                CP.rebuild()
            end
        end)
    end)

    CP.tpPauseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if not TA.running then return end
        TA.paused = not TA.paused
        CP.tpPauseBtn.Text = TA.paused and "RESUME" or "PAUSE"
        if TA.paused then
            CP.tpStatus.TextColor3 = DIM
            CP.tpStatus.Text = "Paused  -  press RESUME to carry on"
        end
    end)

    CP.tpStopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        if not TA.running then return end
        TA.token = TA.token + 1
        tpIdle("Stopped", DIM)
        CP.rebuild()
    end)

    -- RESET: forget every Found mark so the whole route can be done again
    CP.resetBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
        CP.found = {}
        if CP.running then
            CP.dropWaypoint()
            pcall(CP.tick)
        end
        CP.rebuild()
        flash(CP.resetBtn, "Reset!", "RESET")
    end)

    CP.rebuild()

    -- always-on check: get within 8 studs of any Chakra Point and its circle turns green,
    -- even when the live waypoint is paused (the waypoint itself still only runs on Show)
    task.spawn(function()
        while not stopped do
            task.wait(0.8)
            pcall(function()
                if CP.running then return end   -- the live tracker already does this check
                local r = rootOf()
                if not r then return end
                local pos = r.Position
                local changed = false
                for _, e in ipairs(S.chakra) do
                    if e.x then
                        local k = CP.key(e.name)
                        if not CP.found[k] and (Vector3.new(e.x, e.y, e.z) - pos).Magnitude <= CP.RADIUS then
                            CP.found[k] = true
                            changed = true
                        end
                    end
                end
                if changed then CP.rebuild() end
            end)
        end
    end)
end)

-- ====================== QOL PAGE: SERVER HOP / REJOIN ======================
do
	local card = createCard(88, 23, QOLContent)
	cardHeader(card, "🌐  Server")
	local hopBtn = mkButton(card, "Server Hop", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 44), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 32), nil, nil, 16)
	local rejoinBtn = mkButton(card, "Rejoin", UDim2.new(0.5, 4, 0, 44), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 32), nil, nil, 16)

	hopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if not Settings.DX.serverHop then return end
		hopBtn.Text = "Hopping..."
		task.delay(6, function()
			if hopBtn.Parent then hopBtn.Text = "Server Hop" end
		end)
		task.spawn(Settings.DX.serverHop)
	end)

	rejoinBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		rejoinBtn.Text = "Rejoining..."
		task.delay(6, function()
			if rejoinBtn.Parent then rejoinBtn.Text = "Rejoin" end
		end)
		local placeId, jobId = game.PlaceId, game.JobId
		local failConn
		failConn = TeleportService.TeleportInitFailed:Connect(function(plr)
			if plr == LocalPlayer then
				if failConn then failConn:Disconnect() end
				pcall(function() TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, LocalPlayer) end)
			end
		end)
		task.delay(20, function()
			if failConn then failConn:Disconnect() end
		end)
		pcall(function()
			if #Players:GetPlayers() <= 1 then
				TeleportService:Teleport(placeId, LocalPlayer)
			else
				TeleportService:TeleportToPlaceInstance(placeId, jobId, LocalPlayer)
			end
		end)
	end)
end

-- ====================== QOL PAGE: AUTO BLOCK / PARRY ======================
-- Detects nearby attack animations and auto-blocks or auto-parries.
-- Place ABOVE Byakugan Auto Parrys. Custom mode + keys + delay + range.
pcall(function()
	local AB = {
		running = false, token = 0,
		mode = "both",           -- "block" | "parry" | "both"
		blockKey = Enum.KeyCode.F,
		parryKey = Enum.KeyCode.F,
		delay = 0.04,            -- reaction delay after attack seen (lower = faster)
		range = 50,              -- studs
		blockHold = 0.55,        -- how long to hold block
		cooldown = 0.18,         -- min time between reactions
	}
	Settings.AB = AB
	local BLUE = Color3.fromRGB(90, 210, 255)
	local GREEN = Color3.fromRGB(110, 235, 140)
	local DIM = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
	local CREAM = Color3.fromRGB(205, 235, 250)
	local FONT = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	local FONT_B = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
	local FIELD = Color3.fromRGB(18, 30, 40)
	local ATTACK_WORDS = {
		"attack", "punch", "slash", "swing", "heavy", "m1", "m2", "strike",
		"combo", "hit", "blow", "kick", "jab", "strong", "charge", "smash",
		"light", "air", "ground", "skill", "jutsu", "cast", "swing1", "swing2",
		"swing3", "atk", "fight", "combat", "blade", "claw", "upper", "hook",
		"barrage", "flurry", "c_attack", "l_attack", "r_attack", "action"
	}

	local function rootOf()
		local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
		return ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
	end

	-- fixed slot: sits above Fly / Noclip / Server (orders finalized in defer below)
	local card = createCard(380, 20, QOLContent)
	cardHeader(card, "🛡️  Auto Block / Parry")

	local function line(y)
		local f = Instance.new("Frame")
		f.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
		f.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y)
		f.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER_DIM
		f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
		f.BorderSizePixel = 0
		f.Parent = card
	end
	local function mkBox(y, text)
		local b = Instance.new("TextBox")
		b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 26)
		b.Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0, y)
		b.BackgroundColor3 = FIELD
		b.TextColor3 = CREAM
		b.Font = FONT_B
		b.TextSize = 15
		b.Text = text
		b.ClearTextOnFocus = true
		b.Parent = card
		addCorner(b, 5)
		addStroke(b, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
		return b
	end

	local desc = mkLabel(card, "This works — but ping difference makes it imperfect", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 42), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 18), 12, DIM, FONT)
	desc.TextWrapped = true
	desc.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

	-- Mode dropdown (Block / Parry / Both)
	mkLabel(card, "Mode", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 64), UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 22), 14, CREAM, FONT_B)
	local modeBtn = mkButton(card, "Both (Block + Parry)", UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 62), UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 26), nil, nil, 13)
	local modeDrop = Instance.new("Frame")
	modeDrop.Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 0)
	modeDrop.Position = UDim2.new(0, 70, 0, 90)
	modeDrop.BackgroundColor3 = FIELD
	modeDrop.BackgroundTransparency = 0.05
	modeDrop.BorderSizePixel = 0
	modeDrop.ClipsDescendants = true
	modeDrop.Visible = false
	modeDrop.ZIndex = 8
	modeDrop.Parent = card
	addCorner(modeDrop, 5)
	addStroke(modeDrop, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
	local mdList = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	mdList.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
	mdList.Parent = modeDrop
	local mdPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	mdPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 3)
	mdPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 3)
	mdPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 3)
	mdPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 3)
	mdPad.Parent = modeDrop

	local MODES = {
		{id = "block", label = "Block only"},
		{id = "parry", label = "Parry only"},
		{id = "both", label = "Both (Block + Parry)"},
	}
	local modeOpen = false
	local function setMode(id)
		AB.mode = id
		for _, m in ipairs(MODES) do
			if m.id == id then modeBtn.Text = m.label end
		end
		modeOpen = false
		modeDrop.Visible = false
		modeDrop.Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, 0)
	end
	for i, m in ipairs(MODES) do
		local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
		b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 24)
		b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103)
		b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		b.Text = "  " .. m.label
		b.TextColor3 = CREAM
		b.Font = FONT_B
		b.TextSize = 13
		b.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		b.AutoButtonColor = false
		b.LayoutOrder = i
		b.ZIndex = 9
		b.Parent = modeDrop
		addCorner(b, 4)
		addHover(b, Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103), Color3.fromRGB(66, 122, 148))
		b.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() setMode(m.id) end)
	end
	modeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		modeOpen = not modeOpen
		modeDrop.Visible = modeOpen
		modeDrop.Size = UDim2.new(1, -84, 0, modeOpen and (3 * 24 + 2 * 2 + 6) or 0)
	end)
	setMode("both")

	line(96)
	mkLabel(card, "⚙  Custom Settings", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 104), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 18), 14, CREAM, FONT_B)

	mkLabel(card, "Block key", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 128), UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 22), 13, CREAM, FONT)
	local blockKeyBox = mkBox(126, AB.blockKey.Name)
	blockKeyBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local t = tostring(blockKeyBox.Text or ""):upper():gsub("%s", "")
		local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[t] end)
		if ok and kc and #t >= 1 and #t <= 3 then AB.blockKey = kc end
		blockKeyBox.Text = AB.blockKey.Name
	end)

	mkLabel(card, "Parry key", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 156), UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 22), 13, CREAM, FONT)
	local parryKeyBox = mkBox(154, AB.parryKey.Name)
	parryKeyBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local t = tostring(parryKeyBox.Text or ""):upper():gsub("%s", "")
		local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[t] end)
		if ok and kc and #t >= 1 and #t <= 3 then AB.parryKey = kc end
		parryKeyBox.Text = AB.parryKey.Name
	end)

	mkLabel(card, "Reaction delay (sec)", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 184), UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 22), 13, CREAM, FONT)
	local delayBox = mkBox(182, string.format("%.2f", AB.delay))
	delayBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local v = tonumber(delayBox.Text)
		if v then AB.delay = math.clamp(v, 0, 0.8) end
		delayBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", AB.delay)
	end)

	mkLabel(card, "Detect range (studs)", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 212), UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 22), 13, CREAM, FONT)
	local rangeBox = mkBox(210, tostring(AB.range))
	rangeBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local v = tonumber(rangeBox.Text)
		if v then AB.range = math.clamp(math.floor(v), 10, 80) end
		rangeBox.Text = tostring(AB.range)
	end)

	mkLabel(card, "Block hold (sec)", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 240), UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 22), 13, CREAM, FONT)
	local holdBox = mkBox(238, string.format("%.2f", AB.blockHold))
	holdBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local v = tonumber(holdBox.Text)
		if v then AB.blockHold = math.clamp(v, 0.1, 2) end
		holdBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", AB.blockHold)
	end)

	-- saved with the rest of the GUI settings (Settings.save / load)
	Settings.ABsave = {
		get = function()
			return { mode = AB.mode, blockKey = AB.blockKey.Name, parryKey = AB.parryKey.Name,
				delay = AB.delay, range = AB.range, blockHold = AB.blockHold }
		end,
		apply = function(d)
			if type(d.blockKey) == "string" and Enum.KeyCode[d.blockKey] then AB.blockKey = Enum.KeyCode[d.blockKey] end
			if type(d.parryKey) == "string" and Enum.KeyCode[d.parryKey] then AB.parryKey = Enum.KeyCode[d.parryKey] end
			if tonumber(d.delay) then AB.delay = math.clamp(tonumber(d.delay), 0, 0.8) end
			if tonumber(d.range) then AB.range = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(d.range)), 10, 80) end
			if tonumber(d.blockHold) then AB.blockHold = math.clamp(tonumber(d.blockHold), 0.1, 2) end
			blockKeyBox.Text = AB.blockKey.Name
			parryKeyBox.Text = AB.parryKey.Name
			delayBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", AB.delay)
			rangeBox.Text = tostring(AB.range)
			holdBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", AB.blockHold)
			if d.mode == "block" or d.mode == "parry" or d.mode == "both" then setMode(d.mode) end
		end,
	}

	local startBtn = mkButton(card, "START", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 276), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 32), nil, nil, 15)
	local stopBtn = mkButton(card, "STOP", UDim2.new(0.5, 4, 0, 276), UDim2.new(0.5, -18, 0, 32), nil, nil, 15)
	local status = mkLabel(card, "Off  ·  watches nearby attack animations", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 316), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 18), 13, DIM, FONT)
	status:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
	local function setStatus(txt, col)
		status.Text = txt
		status.TextColor3 = col or DIM
	end

	local function tapKey(key)
		pcall(function()
			VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, key, false, game)
			task.wait(0.05)
			VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, key, false, game)
		end)
	end
	local function holdKey(key, secs)
		pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, key, false, game) end)
		task.wait(secs)
		pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, key, false, game) end)
	end

	local seen = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local hooked = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local pending = {}  -- queue of attack names detected this frame
	local function isAttack(name)
		name = tostring(name or ""):lower()
		if name == "" or name == "idle" or name:find("idle") or name:find("walk") or name:find("run") or name:find("jump") or name:find("fall") then
			return false
		end
		for _, w in ipairs(ATTACK_WORDS) do
			if name:find(w, 1, true) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end

	local function hookAnimator(animator, model)
		if not animator or hooked[animator] then return end
		hooked[animator] = true
		pcall(function()
			animator.AnimationPlayed:Connect(function(track)
				if not AB.running then return end
				if seen[track] then return end
				local nm = ""
				pcall(function()
					nm = (track.Animation and track.Animation.Name) or track.Name or ""
				end)
				if isAttack(nm) then
					seen[track] = true
					pending[#pending + 1] = nm
				end
			end)
		end)
	end

	local function scanAndHook()
		local r = rootOf()
		if not r then return end
		local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
		local parts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(r.Position, AB.range)
		local checked = {}
		for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
			local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			if model and model ~= myChar and not checked[model] then
				checked[model] = true
				local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local animator = hum and (hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator") or hum:FindFirstChild("Animator"))
				if animator then
					hookAnimator(animator, model)
					-- also catch already-playing tracks (poll backup)
					pcall(function()
						for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
							if not seen[track] and track.TimePosition < 0.5 then
								local nm = track.Animation and track.Animation.Name or track.Name
								if isAttack(nm) then
									seen[track] = true
									pending[#pending + 1] = nm
								end
							end
						end
					end)
				end
			end
		end
	end

	local function react(nm)
		local mode = AB.mode
		if mode == "block" or mode == "both" then
			-- start block almost immediately
			task.spawn(function()
				holdKey(AB.blockKey, AB.blockHold)
			end)
		end
		if mode == "parry" or mode == "both" then
			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(AB.delay)
				if AB.running then tapKey(AB.parryKey) end
			end)
		end
	end

	local function startRun()
		if AB.running then return end
		AB.running = true
		AB.token = AB.token + 1
		local my = AB.token
		startBtn.Text = "RUNNING"
		setStatus("Watching (fast detect)...", BLUE)
		pending = {}
		task.spawn(function()
			local last = 0
			local n = 0
			while AB.running and AB.token == my and not stopped do
				scanAndHook()
				if #pending > 0 and os.clock() - last > AB.cooldown then
					local nm = table.remove(pending, 1)
					-- clear rest of same burst
					pending = {}
					last = os.clock()
					n = n + 1
					setStatus("Attack (" .. tostring(nm) .. ")  ·  #" .. n, GREEN)
					react(nm)
				end
				task.wait(0.03)
			end
			startBtn.Text = "START"
			setStatus("Stopped", DIM)
		end)
	end

	startBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(startRun)
	stopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		AB.running = false
		AB.token = AB.token + 1
		startBtn.Text = "START"
		setStatus("Stopped", DIM)
	end)
	pcall(refreshGuiLook)

	-- FINAL QOL ORDER: Auto Block/Parry → Fly → Noclip → Server
	task.defer(function()
		task.wait(0.4)
		pcall(function()
			local abCard = nil
			local serverCard = nil
			for _, ch in ipairs(QOLContent:GetChildren()) do
				if ch:IsA("GuiObject") then
					for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
						if d:IsA("TextLabel") then
							if d.Text == "🛡️  Auto Block / Parry" or d.Text:find("Auto Block") then
								abCard = ch
							elseif d.Text == "🌐  Server" or d.Text == "Server" then
								serverCard = ch
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
			-- fixed sequential orders so list is stable
			if abCard then abCard.LayoutOrder = 20 end
			if HK.fly and HK.fly.card then HK.fly.card.LayoutOrder = 21 end
			if NoclipCard then NoclipCard.LayoutOrder = 22 end
			if serverCard then serverCard.LayoutOrder = 23 end
		end)
	end)
end)

-- ====================== QOL PAGE: AUTO BYAKUGAN BLOCKS ======================

-- FIRST  = punch (hold M1) -> block 3.2 sec -> repeat      SECOND = block 3.2 sec -> punch -> repeat
-- Loops forever until STOP.
pcall(function()
	local BB = {running = false, mode = nil, token = 0, clicks = 4, blockKey = Enum.KeyCode.F}
	Settings.BB = BB
	local BLUE = Color3.fromRGB(90, 210, 255)
	local BLOCK_SECS = 3.2
	-- how long M1 is held down for each option:  4 Punches = 1.90 sec,  5 Punches = 2.75 sec
	BB.holdSecs = {[4] = 1.90, [5] = 2.75}

	-- sit right under the Fly card (everything below moves down one)
	local fo = (HK.fly and HK.fly.card and HK.fly.card.LayoutOrder) or 7
	for _, ch in ipairs(QOLContent:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.LayoutOrder > fo and ch.LayoutOrder < 20 then ch.LayoutOrder = ch.LayoutOrder + 1 end
	end
	local card = createCard(300, fo + 1, QOLContent)
	cardHeader(card, "🥊  Auto Byakugan Blocks")

	local function line(y)
		local f = Instance.new("Frame")
		f.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
		f.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y)
		f.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER_DIM
		f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
		f.BorderSizePixel = 0
		f.Parent = card
	end

	-- clicks per round
	mkLabel(card, "Punches per round", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 46), UDim2.new(1, -200, 0, 26), 16, CREAM, FONT_B)
	local btn4 = mkButton(card, "", UDim2.new(1, -184, 0, 44), UDim2.new(0, 84, 0, 30), nil, nil, 14)   -- left
	local btn5 = mkButton(card, "", UDim2.new(1, -96, 0, 44), UDim2.new(0, 84, 0, 30), nil, nil, 14)    -- right
	local function refreshClicks()
		btn5.Text = (BB.clicks == 5 and "●  5 Punches" or "○  5 Punches")
		btn4.Text = (BB.clicks == 4 and "●  4 Punches" or "○  4 Punches")
	end
	btn5.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() BB.clicks = 5 refreshClicks() end)
	btn4.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() BB.clicks = 4 refreshClicks() end)
	refreshClicks()

	-- the two sections
	line(84)
	mkLabel(card, "FIRST", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 92), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 20), 17, CREAM, FONT_B)
	mkLabel(card, "Punch  ➜  Block 3.2s  ➜  repeat", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 112), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 18), 14, DIM, FONT)
	local firstBtn = mkButton(card, "START", UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 96), UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 32), nil, nil, 15)

	line(140)
	mkLabel(card, "SECOND", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 148), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 20), 17, CREAM, FONT_B)
	mkLabel(card, "Block 3.2s  ➜  Punch  ➜  repeat", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 168), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 18), 14, DIM, FONT)
	local secondBtn = mkButton(card, "START", UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 152), UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 32), nil, nil, 15)

	-- block key
	line(196)
	mkLabel(card, "Block key", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 206), UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 26), 16, CREAM, FONT_B)
	local keyBox = Instance.new("TextBox")
	keyBox.Size = UDim2.new(0, 48, 0, 28)
	keyBox.Position = UDim2.new(1, -62, 0, 205)
	keyBox.BackgroundColor3 = FIELD
	keyBox.TextColor3 = CREAM
	keyBox.Font = FONT_B
	keyBox.TextSize = 17
	keyBox.Text = BB.blockKey.Name
	keyBox.ClearTextOnFocus = true
	keyBox.Parent = card
	addCorner(keyBox, 5)
	addStroke(keyBox, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
	keyBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local t = tostring(keyBox.Text or ""):upper():gsub("%s", "")
		local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[t] end)
		if ok and kc and #t == 1 then BB.blockKey = kc end
		keyBox.Text = BB.blockKey.Name
	end)
	Settings.BBsave = {
		get = function() return { clicks = BB.clicks, blockKey = BB.blockKey.Name } end,
		apply = function(d)
			if d.clicks == 4 or d.clicks == 5 then BB.clicks = d.clicks refreshClicks() end
			if type(d.blockKey) == "string" and Enum.KeyCode[d.blockKey] then
				BB.blockKey = Enum.KeyCode[d.blockKey]
				keyBox.Text = BB.blockKey.Name
			end
		end,
	}

	local stopBtn = mkButton(card, "STOP", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 244), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 32), nil, nil, 16)
	local status = mkLabel(card, "Pick FIRST or SECOND to start  -  runs until STOP", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 278), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 18), 14, DIM, FONT)
	status:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
	local function setStatus(txt, col)
		status.Text = txt
		status.TextColor3 = col or DIM
	end
	local function refreshBtns()
		firstBtn.Text = (BB.running and BB.mode == "first") and "RUNNING" or "START"
		secondBtn.Text = (BB.running and BB.mode == "second") and "RUNNING" or "START"
	end

	-- ---- input helpers ----
	local function holdBlock()
		pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, BB.blockKey, false, game) end)
	end
	local function releaseBlock()
		pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, BB.blockKey, false, game) end)
	end
	-- a screen spot that is NOT on top of this GUI, so the punches never click our own buttons
	local function pickPoint()
		local vs = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
		local cands = {{0.5, 0.5}, {0.5, 0.72}, {0.82, 0.5}, {0.18, 0.5}, {0.5, 0.3}, {0.9, 0.85}, {0.1, 0.85}}
		for _, c in ipairs(cands) do
			local x, y = vs.X * c[1], vs.Y * c[2]
			local blocked = false
			local ok, objs = pcall(function() return ScreenGui.Parent:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(x, y) end)
			if ok and objs then
				for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
					if o:IsDescendantOf(ScreenGui) then blocked = true break end
				end
			end
			if not blocked then return x, y end
		end
		return vs.X * 0.97, vs.Y * 0.97
	end
	-- punching = HOLD the left mouse button (M1) down, then let go
	local function mouseDown()
		local x, y = BB.px or 400, BB.py or 300
		pcall(function()
			VirtualInputManager:SendMouseMoveEvent(x, y, game)
			VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, true, game, 1)
		end)
	end
	local function mouseUp()
		local x, y = BB.px or 400, BB.py or 300
		pcall(function() VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 0, false, game, 1) end)
	end

	local function startRun(mode)
		BB.running = true
		BB.mode = mode
		BB.token = BB.token + 1
		local my = BB.token
		BB.px, BB.py = pickPoint()
		refreshBtns()
		local function alive() return not stopped and BB.running and BB.token == my end
		task.spawn(function()
			local round = 0
			local function punch()
				local hold = BB.holdSecs[BB.clicks] or 1.9
				mouseDown()
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while alive() do
					local el = os.clock() - t0
					if el >= hold then break end
					setStatus("Round " .. round .. "  -  Punching (holding M1)  " .. string.format("%.1f", hold - el) .. "s", BLUE)
					task.wait(0.05)
				end
				-- FIRST: the block key goes down in the very same instant M1 is let go (no gap)
				if mode == "first" and alive() then holdBlock() end
				mouseUp()
			end
			local function block()
				holdBlock()
				local t0 = os.clock()
				local lastDown = t0
				while alive() do
					local el = os.clock() - t0
					if el >= BLOCK_SECS then break end
					setStatus("Round " .. round .. "  -  Blocking  " .. string.format("%.1f", BLOCK_SECS - el) .. "s", Color3.fromRGB(110, 235, 140))
					if os.clock() - lastDown > 0.5 then
						holdBlock()
						lastDown = os.clock()
					end
					task.wait(0.1)
				end
				releaseBlock()
			end
			while alive() do
				round = round + 1
				if mode == "first" then
					punch()
					if alive() then block() end
				else
					block()
					if alive() then punch() end
				end
			end
			releaseBlock()
			mouseUp()
		end)
	end

	firstBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() startRun("first") end)
	secondBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() startRun("second") end)
	stopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if not BB.running then return end
		BB.running = false
		BB.token = BB.token + 1
		releaseBlock()
		mouseUp()
		refreshBtns()
		setStatus("Stopped", DIM)
	end)
	pcall(refreshGuiLook)
end)

-- ====================== QOL PAGE: BYAKUGAN AUTO PARRYS ======================
-- FIRST  = right-click once (heavy punch) every 2.8 sec until STOP
-- SECOND = waits for a nearby heavy attack, then taps the parry key (perfect parry)
pcall(function()
	local BP = {running = false, mode = nil, token = 0, parryKey = Enum.KeyCode.F, delay = 0.20}
	Settings.BP = BP
	local BLUE = Color3.fromRGB(90, 210, 255)
	local GREEN = Color3.fromRGB(110, 235, 140)
	local HEAVY_EVERY = 2.8
	local RANGE = 35                                 -- studs: only attacks this close to you are watched
	local HEAVY_WORDS = {"heavy", "m2", "strong", "charge", "smash"}   -- attack animation names that count as heavy

	-- sit right above "Auto Byakugan Blocks" (right under Fly); everything below moves down one
	local fo = (HK.fly and HK.fly.card and HK.fly.card.LayoutOrder) or 7
	for _, ch in ipairs(QOLContent:GetChildren()) do
		if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.LayoutOrder > fo and ch.LayoutOrder < 20 then ch.LayoutOrder = ch.LayoutOrder + 1 end
	end
	local card = createCard(290, fo + 1, QOLContent)
	cardHeader(card, "🌀  Byakugan Auto Parrys")

	local function line(y)
		local f = Instance.new("Frame")
		f.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
		f.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y)
		f.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER_DIM
		f.BackgroundTransparency = 0.6
		f.BorderSizePixel = 0
		f.Parent = card
	end

	mkLabel(card, "FIRST", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 44), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 20), 17, CREAM, FONT_B)
	mkLabel(card, "Right-click (heavy punch) every 2.8s", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 64), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 18), 14, DIM, FONT)
	local firstBtn = mkButton(card, "START", UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 48), UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 32), nil, nil, 15)

	line(90)
	mkLabel(card, "SECOND", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 98), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 20), 17, CREAM, FONT_B)
	mkLabel(card, "Waits for a heavy attack  ➜  Perfect Parry", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 118), UDim2.new(1, -130, 0, 18), 14, DIM, FONT)
	local secondBtn = mkButton(card, "START", UDim2.new(1, -110, 0, 102), UDim2.new(0, 96, 0, 32), nil, nil, 15)

	line(146)
	local function mkBox(y, text)
		local b = Instance.new("TextBox")
		b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 60, 0, 28)
		b.Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0, y)
		b.BackgroundColor3 = FIELD
		b.TextColor3 = CREAM
		b.Font = FONT_B
		b.TextSize = 17
		b.Text = text
		b.ClearTextOnFocus = true
		b.Parent = card
		addCorner(b, 5)
		addStroke(b, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
		return b
	end
	mkLabel(card, "Parry key", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 156), UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 26), 16, CREAM, FONT_B)
	local keyBox = mkBox(155, BP.parryKey.Name)
	keyBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local t = tostring(keyBox.Text or ""):upper():gsub("%s", "")
		local ok, kc = pcall(function() return Enum.KeyCode[t] end)
		if ok and kc and #t == 1 then BP.parryKey = kc end
		keyBox.Text = BP.parryKey.Name
	end)
	mkLabel(card, "Parry delay (sec)", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 190), UDim2.new(1, -100, 0, 26), 16, CREAM, FONT_B)
	local delayBox = mkBox(189, string.format("%.2f", BP.delay))
	delayBox.FocusLost:Connect(function()
		local v = tonumber(delayBox.Text)
		if v then BP.delay = math.clamp(v, 0, 1.5) end
		delayBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", BP.delay)
	end)
	Settings.BPsave = {
		get = function() return { parryKey = BP.parryKey.Name, delay = BP.delay } end,
		apply = function(d)
			if type(d.parryKey) == "string" and Enum.KeyCode[d.parryKey] then
				BP.parryKey = Enum.KeyCode[d.parryKey]
				keyBox.Text = BP.parryKey.Name
			end
			if tonumber(d.delay) then
				BP.delay = math.clamp(tonumber(d.delay), 0, 1.5)
				delayBox.Text = string.format("%.2f", BP.delay)
			end
		end,
	}

	local stopBtn = mkButton(card, "STOP", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 228), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 32), nil, nil, 16)
	local status = mkLabel(card, "Pick FIRST or SECOND to start  -  runs until STOP", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 264), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 18), 14, DIM, FONT)
	status:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
	local function setStatus(txt, col)
		status.Text = txt
		status.TextColor3 = col or DIM
	end
	local function refreshBtns()
		firstBtn.Text = (BP.running and BP.mode == "first") and "RUNNING" or "START"
		secondBtn.Text = (BP.running and BP.mode == "second") and "RUNNING" or "START"
	end

	-- ---- input helpers ----
	-- a screen spot that is NOT on top of this GUI, so the clicks never hit our own buttons
	local function pickPoint()
		local vs = workspace.CurrentCamera.ViewportSize
		local cands = {{0.5, 0.5}, {0.5, 0.72}, {0.82, 0.5}, {0.18, 0.5}, {0.5, 0.3}, {0.9, 0.85}, {0.1, 0.85}}
		for _, c in ipairs(cands) do
			local x, y = vs.X * c[1], vs.Y * c[2]
			local blocked = false
			local ok, objs = pcall(function() return ScreenGui.Parent:GetGuiObjectsAtPosition(x, y) end)
			if ok and objs then
				for _, o in ipairs(objs) do
					if o:IsDescendantOf(ScreenGui) then blocked = true break end
				end
			end
			if not blocked then return x, y end
		end
		return vs.X * 0.97, vs.Y * 0.97
	end
	local function rightClick()
		local x, y = BP.px or 400, BP.py or 300
		pcall(function()
			VirtualInputManager:SendMouseMoveEvent(x, y, game)
			VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 1, true, game, 1)     -- 1 = right mouse button
			task.wait(0.05)
			VirtualInputManager:SendMouseButtonEvent(x, y, 1, false, game, 1)
		end)
	end
	local function tapParry()
		pcall(function()
			VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(true, BP.parryKey, false, game)
			task.wait(0.06)
			VirtualInputManager:SendKeyEvent(false, BP.parryKey, false, game)
		end)
	end

	-- heavy attack watcher: looks at animations on anything with a Humanoid near you (players + NPCs)
	local seen = setmetatable({}, {__mode = "k"})
	local function isHeavy(name)
		name = tostring(name or ""):lower()
		for _, w in ipairs(HEAVY_WORDS) do
			if name:find(w, 1, true) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end
	local function findHeavy()
		local r = rootOf()
		if not r then return nil end
		local parts = workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(r.Position, RANGE)
		local checked = {}
		local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
		for _, part in ipairs(parts) do
			local model = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
			if model and model ~= myChar and not checked[model] then
				checked[model] = true
				local hum = model:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
				local animator = hum and hum:FindFirstChildOfClass("Animator")
				if animator then
					for _, track in ipairs(animator:GetPlayingAnimationTracks()) do
						if not seen[track] and track.TimePosition < 0.4 then
							local nm = track.Animation and track.Animation.Name or track.Name
							if isHeavy(nm) or isHeavy(track.Name) then
								seen[track] = true
								return nm, model
							end
						end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function startRun(mode)
		BP.running = true
		BP.mode = mode
		BP.token = BP.token + 1
		local my = BP.token
		BP.px, BP.py = pickPoint()
		refreshBtns()
		local function alive() return not stopped and BP.running and BP.token == my end
		task.spawn(function()
			if mode == "first" then
				local n = 0
				while alive() do
					n = n + 1
					rightClick()                                  -- one right click = one heavy punch
					local t0 = os.clock()
					while alive() do
						local el = os.clock() - t0
						if el >= HEAVY_EVERY then break end
						setStatus("Heavy punch #" .. n .. "  -  next in " .. string.format("%.1f", HEAVY_EVERY - el) .. "s", BLUE)
						task.wait(0.1)
					end
				end
			else
				local lastParry = 0
				local parries = 0
				setStatus("Waiting for a heavy attack...", BLUE)
				while alive() do
					local nm = findHeavy()
					if nm and os.clock() - lastParry > 0.5 then
						lastParry = os.clock()
						parries = parries + 1
						setStatus("Heavy attack (" .. tostring(nm) .. ")  -  parried x" .. parries, GREEN)
						task.spawn(function()
							task.wait(BP.delay)
							if alive() then tapParry() end
						end)
					end
					task.wait(0.08)
				end
			end
		end)
	end

	firstBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() startRun("first") end)
	secondBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() startRun("second") end)
	stopBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if not BP.running then return end
		BP.running = false
		BP.token = BP.token + 1
		refreshBtns()
		setStatus("Stopped", DIM)
	end)
	pcall(refreshGuiLook)
end)

-- ====================== BACKGROUND LOOP (stats / timers / safety) ======================
task.spawn(function()
	local wasActive = false
	local lastTick = os.clock()
	while not stopped do
		pcall(function()
			local now = os.clock()
			if MacroActive then stats.activeSecs = stats.activeSecs + (now - lastTick)end
			lastTick = now
			if MacroActive and not wasActive then resetSellTimer() end
			wasActive = MacroActive

			refreshStats()
			rareStatus.Text = "Rare fruits on map: " .. rareCount

			if S.autoSell then
				if MacroActive then
					local left = math.max(0, math.floor(nextSellAt - now))
					sellSub.Text = string.format("Next sell in %02d:%02d", math.floor(left / 60), left % 60)
				else
					sellSub.Text = "Waits for the farm loop to be ON"
				end
			else
				sellSub.Text = SELL_SUB_IDLE
			end

		end)
		task.wait(1)
	end
end)

-- ====================== SAVE / LOAD / STOP ======================
Settings.DX.getSave = function()
	local ok, res = pcall(function()
		S.spotsAll[PLACE_KEY] = S.spots
		return {
			antiAfk = S.antiAfk, autoSell = S.autoSell, sellMins = S.sellMins,
			rareAlert = S.rareAlert, fps = S.fps,
			hideKey = HK.hide.key and HK.hide.key.Name or "NONE",
			spots = S.spotsAll,
			chakra = {[PLACE_KEY] = S.chakra},
		}
	end)
	if ok then return res end
	return nil
end

Settings.DX.applySave = function(d)
	if d.antiAfk ~= nil then antiSw.set(d.antiAfk == true) end
	if tonumber(d.sellMins) then
		S.sellMins = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(d.sellMins)), 1, 60)
		sellStep.refresh()
	end
	if d.autoSell == true then sellSw.set(true) end
	if d.rareAlert == true then rareSw.set(true) end
	if type(d.hideKey) == "string" then
		if d.hideKey == "NONE" then
			HK.hide.key = nil
		else
			pcall(function() HK.hide.key = Enum.KeyCode[d.hideKey] end)
		end
		showHotkey(HK.hide)
	end
	if type(d.spots) == "table" then
		S.spotsAll = d.spots
		local list = S.spotsAll[PLACE_KEY]
		local clean = {}
		if type(list) == "table" then
			for _, sp in ipairs(list) do
				if type(sp) == "table" and type(sp.name) == "string" and tonumber(sp.x) and tonumber(sp.y) and tonumber(sp.z) then
					clean[#clean + 1] = {name = sp.name, x = tonumber(sp.x), y = tonumber(sp.y), z = tonumber(sp.z)}
				end
			end
		end
		S.spots = clean
		rebuildSpots()
	end
	if type(d.chakra) == "table" and type(d.chakra[PLACE_KEY]) == "table" then
		local list = {}
		for _, e in ipairs(d.chakra[PLACE_KEY]) do
			if type(e) == "table" and type(e.name) == "string" and tonumber(e.x) and tonumber(e.y) and tonumber(e.z) then
				list[#list + 1] = {name = e.name, x = tonumber(e.x), y = tonumber(e.y), z = tonumber(e.z)}
			end
		end
		S.chakra = list
		if Settings.CP and Settings.CP.rebuild then pcall(Settings.CP.rebuild) end
	end
	if d.fps == true then
		task.delay(2, function()
			if not stopped then fpsSw.set(true) end
		end)
	end
end

Settings.DX.stop = function()
	stopped = true
	pcall(SP.clear)
	Settings.DX.cycleHook = nil
	Settings.DX.failsafePause = false
	Settings.DX.onPicked = nil
	pcall(setAntiAfk, false)
	for _, c in ipairs(conns) do
		pcall(function() c:Disconnect() end)
	end
	conns = {}
	if S.fps then pcall(fpsOff) end
end

-- default state: anti-AFK on
antiSw.set(true)

end, function(errX)
	warn("[DannyGui] extras failed to load: " .. tostring(errX))
	print("[DannyGui] extras failed to load: " .. tostring(errX))
end)

-- ====================== SAVED SETTINGS ======================
Settings.snapshot = function()
	local snap = {
		summonKey = SummonKey.Name,
		failsafe = FailsafeMode,
		fruitEsp = ESPEnabled,
		playerEsp = PlayerEspEnabled,
		noclip = HK.noclip.on == true,
		nofog = HK.nofog.on == true,
		light = HK.light.on == true,
		lightLevel = HK.light.level,
		infJump = HK.infjump.on == true,
		infJumpMode = HK.infjump.mode,
		infZoom = HK.infzoom.on == true,
		infZoomMode = HK.infzoom.mode,
		noFallDamage = HK.nofall.on == true,
		fly = HK.fly.on == true,
		flySpeed = HK.fly.speed,
		macro = false, -- never restore farm across sessions (kick/leave resets it)
		playerEspRange = PlayerEspRange,
		extras = (Settings.DX.getSave and Settings.DX.getSave()) or nil,
		espShowName = EspOpts.name,
		espShowOutline = EspOpts.outline,
		espShowStuds = EspOpts.studs,
		espShowHealth = EspOpts.health,
		espShowTeam = EspOpts.team,
		keys = {
			esp = HK.esp.key and HK.esp.key.Name or nil,
			fly = HK.fly.key and HK.fly.key.Name or nil,
			noclip = HK.noclip.key and HK.noclip.key.Name or nil,
			nofog = HK.nofog.key and HK.nofog.key.Name or nil,
			infjump = HK.infjump.key and HK.infjump.key.Name or nil,
			infzoom = HK.infzoom.key and HK.infzoom.key.Name or nil,
			nofall = HK.nofall.key and HK.nofall.key.Name or nil,
			light = HK.light.key and HK.light.key.Name or nil,
		},
		ab = Settings.ABsave and Settings.ABsave.get() or nil,
		bb = Settings.BBsave and Settings.BBsave.get() or nil,
		bp = Settings.BPsave and Settings.BPsave.get() or nil,
		av = Settings.AVsave and Settings.AVsave.get() or nil,
	}
	-- anything that exists in the last saved file but could not be read this session (a block failed to
	-- load, a future field...) is carried over, so a partial load can never wipe saved settings
	local prev = Settings.prevData
	if type(prev) == "table" then
		for k, v in pairs(prev) do
			if snap[k] == nil then snap[k] = v end
		end
	end
	return snap
end

Settings.save = function()
	if Settings.loading or Settings.stopped then return end
	pcall(function()
		local ok, snap = pcall(Settings.snapshot)
		if not ok or type(snap) ~= "table" then return end
		local ok2, text = pcall(function() return HttpService:JSONEncode(snap) end)
		if not ok2 or text == Settings.lastText then return end -- nothing changed: no disk write
		if Settings.writeJson(Settings.file, snap) then Settings.lastText = text end
	end)
end

env.DannyGuiStop = function()
	MacroActive = false
	runId = runId + (1)
	Settings.stopped = true
	pcall(function() Settings.save() end)
	pcall(releaseF)
	pcall(clearAll)
	pcall(clearEsp)
	pcall(clearPlayerEsp)
	pcall(clearNoclip)
	pcall(clearNofog)
	pcall(HK.light.clear)
	pcall(HK.infjump.clear)
	pcall(HK.infzoom.clear)
	pcall(HK.nofall.clear)
	pcall(HK.fly.clear)
	pcall(clearHotkeys)
	if Settings.DX.stop then pcall(Settings.DX.stop) end
end

-- ====================== BUTTONS ======================
MainToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	MacroActive = not MacroActive
	setToggle(MainToggle, MainKnob, MacroActive)
	if MacroActive then
		clearEsp()
		runKeyLoop()
	else
		runId = runId + (1)
		releaseF()
		clearAll()
		startEsp()
	end
end)

ESPToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	ESPEnabled = not ESPEnabled
	setToggle(ESPToggle, ESPKnob, ESPEnabled)

	if not ESPEnabled then
		for obj, hl in pairs(activeHighlights) do
			if typeof(hl) ~= "boolean" and hl then
				hl:Destroy()
				activeHighlights[obj] = true
			end
		end
	else
		for obj, _ in pairs(activeHighlights) do
			if obj and obj.Parent then
				local hl = Instance.new("Highlight")
				hl.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
				hl.FillTransparency = 0.4
				styleFruitHighlight(hl, obj)
				hl.Adornee = obj
				hl.Parent = ScreenGui
				activeHighlights[obj] = hl
			end
		end
	end

	if not MacroActive then
		if ESPEnabled then startEsp() else clearEsp() end
	end
	Settings.save()
end)

ChangeBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	if changingKeybind then return end
	changingKeybind = true
	KeyBox.Text = "..."
	KeyBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 200, 50)

	local connection
	connection = UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input, gp)
		if gp then return end
		if input.UserInputType == Enum.UserInputType.Keyboard then
			SummonKey = input.KeyCode
			KeyBox.Text = input.KeyCode.Name
			KeyBox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
			changingKeybind = false
			connection:Disconnect()
			Settings.save()
		end
	end)
end)

do
	local t0 = os.clock()
	while not Settings._midReady and os.clock() - t0 < 15 do
		task.wait(0.05)
	end
end
PlayerEspToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	local state = not PlayerEspEnabled
	setPlayerEsp(state)
	setToggle(PlayerEspToggle, PlayerEspKnob, state)
end)
