	HK.fly.set(not HK.fly.on)
end)

NoclipToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setNoclip(not HK.noclip.on)
end)

NofogToggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	setNofog(not HK.nofog.on)
end)
HK.light.toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	HK.light.set(not HK.light.on)
end)
HK.infjump.toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	HK.infjump.set(not HK.infjump.on)
end)
HK.infzoom.toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	HK.infzoom.set(not HK.infzoom.on)
end)
HK.nofall.toggle.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
	HK.nofall.set(not HK.nofall.on)
end)

local closing = false
xpcall(function()
	-- the real close routine (only runs after the person presses Yes)
	local function doClose()
		if closing then return end
		closing = true
		-- save the player's custom settings FIRST, before anything gets cleared
		pcall(Settings.save)
		MacroActive = false
		runId = runId + (1)
		releaseF()
		clearAll()
		clearEsp()
		clearPlayerEsp()
		clearNoclip()
		clearNofog()
		HK.infjump.clear()
		pcall(HK.infzoom.clear)
		pcall(HK.nofall.clear)
		HK.fly.clear()
		clearHotkeys()
		if Settings.DX.stop then pcall(Settings.DX.stop) end
		local out = TweenService:Create(Main, TweenInfo.new(0.35, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.In), {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, -(Main.AbsoluteSize.X + 60), Main.Position.Y.Scale, Main.Position.Y.Offset),
		})
		out:Play()
		out.Completed:Wait()
		ScreenGui:Destroy()
	end
	Settings.DX.closeNow = doClose

	-- confirm box that pops up just outside the right side of the GUI, on top of everything
	-- (own helpers: the ones from the Teleports section are not visible down here)
	local PFONT = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
	local P_BTN, P_BTN_HOVER = Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103), Color3.fromRGB(66, 122, 148)
	local P_RED, P_RED_HOVER = Color3.fromRGB(170, 45, 45), Color3.fromRGB(215, 65, 65)
	local function mkLabel(parent, text, pos, size, ts, color, font)
		local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		l.Position = pos
		l.Size = size
		l.Text = text
		l.TextColor3 = color
		l.Font = font
		l.TextSize = ts
		l.Parent = parent
		return l
	end
	local function mkButton(parent, text, pos, size, color, hover, ts)
		local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
		b.Size = size
		b.Position = pos
		b.BackgroundColor3 = color
		b.Text = text
		b.TextColor3 = CREAM
		b.Font = PFONT
		b.TextSize = ts
		b.AutoButtonColor = false
		b.Parent = parent
		addCorner(b, 5)
		addStroke(b, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
		addHover(b, color, hover)
		return b
	end

	-- built like the main Danny GUI panel (same gradient, ornate border, title font, divider and
	-- buttons) so the GUI Settings color / transparency apply to it exactly like every other part
	local ASK_W, ASK_H = 270, 150
	local ask = Instance.new("Frame")
	ask.Name = "ClosePrompt"
	ask.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0, 0)
	ask.Size = UDim2.new(0, ASK_W, 0, ASK_H)
	ask.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
	ask.BackgroundTransparency = 0.08
	ask.BorderSizePixel = 0
	ask.Active = true
	ask.Visible = false
	ask.Parent = ScreenGui
	addCorner(ask, 8)
	addGradient(ask, Color3.fromRGB(40, 68, 84), Color3.fromRGB(12, 19, 28), 90)

	local askTitle = mkLabel(ask, "Close Danny GUI?", UDim2.new(0, 12, 0, 12), UDim2.new(1, -24, 0, 34), 28, CREAM, Enum.Font.Fondamento)
	askTitle.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	askTitle.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	askTitle.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.5
	askTitle.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12)
	addDivider(ask, 54, 22)

	local askText = mkLabel(ask, "Are you sure you want to close it?", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 62), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 34), 16, ICE, Enum.Font.SourceSans)
	askText.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Center
	askText.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	askText.TextWrapped = true
	askText.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.None
	local yesBtn = mkButton(ask, "Yes", UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 104), UDim2.new(0.5, -24, 0, 32), P_RED, P_RED_HOVER, 17)
	local noBtn = mkButton(ask, "No", UDim2.new(0.5, 6, 0, 104), UDim2.new(0.5, -24, 0, 32), P_BTN, P_BTN_HOVER, 17)
	local askBorder = addOrnateBorder(ask, 8, true)
	for _, d in ipairs(ask:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("GuiObject") then d.ZIndex = 201 end
	end
	ask.ZIndex = 200
	askBorder.ZIndex = 210
	for _, d in ipairs(askBorder:GetDescendants()) do
		if d:IsA("GuiObject") then d.ZIndex = 210 end
	end

	local function showAsk()
		if closing then return end
		-- just outside the right edge of the Danny GUI, level with the X button
		local cy = CloseBtn.AbsolutePosition.Y
		local GAP = 8 -- pixels between the menu panel and the popup; lower = further left, higher = further right
		local x = Panel.AbsolutePosition.X + Panel.AbsoluteSize.X + GAP
		local sw = ScreenGui.AbsoluteSize.X
		if sw > 0 and x + ASK_W > sw - 4 then x = math.max(4, sw - ASK_W - 4) end -- keep it on screen
		ask.Position = UDim2.new(0, x - 16, 0, cy)
		ask.Visible = true
		TweenService:Create(ask, TweenInfo.new(0.2, Enum.EasingStyle.Quint, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
			Position = UDim2.new(0, x, 0, cy),
		}):Play()
	end

	CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if ask.Visible then ask.Visible = false else showAsk() end
	end)
	noBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() ask.Visible = false end)
	yesBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		ask.Visible = false
		doClose()
	end)
end, function(errC)
	warn("[DannyGui] close prompt failed: " .. tostring(errC))
	print("[DannyGui] close prompt failed: " .. tostring(errC))
	-- fallback: X still closes (and saves) directly if the prompt could not be built
	CloseBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		if Settings.DX.closeNow then Settings.DX.closeNow() end
	end)
end)

-- Init toggles
setToggle(MainToggle, MainKnob, false)
setToggle(ESPToggle, ESPKnob, true)
setToggle(PlayerEspToggle, PlayerEspKnob, false)
setToggle(HK.fly.toggle, HK.fly.knob, false)
setToggle(NoclipToggle, NoclipKnob, false)
setToggle(NofogToggle, NofogKnob, false)
setToggle(HK.light.toggle, HK.light.knob, false)
setToggle(HK.infjump.toggle, HK.infjump.knob, false)
setToggle(HK.infzoom.toggle, HK.infzoom.knob, false)
setToggle(HK.nofall.toggle, HK.nofall.knob, false)

-- load the saved settings
do
	local data = Settings.readJson(Settings.file)
	Settings.prevData = data

	if type(data) == "table" then
		pcall(function()
			if data.summonKey and Enum.KeyCode[data.summonKey] then
				SummonKey = Enum.KeyCode[data.summonKey]
				KeyBox.Text = data.summonKey
			end
		end)

		if data.failsafe == "kick" or data.failsafe == "hop" then
			selectFailsafe(data.failsafe)
		end

		if type(data.keys) == "table" then
			for id, name in pairs(data.keys) do
				pcall(function()
					local hk = HK[id]
					if hk and type(name) == "string" and Enum.KeyCode[name] then
						hk.key = Enum.KeyCode[name]
						showHotkey(hk)
					end
				end)
			end
		end

		if tonumber(data.flySpeed) then
			HK.fly.setSpeed(tonumber(data.flySpeed), true)
		end
		if tonumber(data.playerEspRange) then
			HK.esp.setRange(tonumber(data.playerEspRange), true)
		end
		if data.espShowName ~= nil then EspOpts.name = data.espShowName == true end
		if data.espShowOutline ~= nil then EspOpts.outline = data.espShowOutline == true end
		if data.espShowStuds ~= nil then EspOpts.studs = data.espShowStuds == true end
		if data.espShowHealth ~= nil then EspOpts.health = data.espShowHealth == true end
		if data.espShowTeam ~= nil then EspOpts.team = data.espShowTeam == true end
		if data.fruitEsp == false then
			ESPEnabled = false
			setToggle(ESPToggle, ESPKnob, false)
		end
		if data.playerEsp == true then
			setPlayerEsp(true)
			setToggle(PlayerEspToggle, PlayerEspKnob, true)
		end
		if data.noclip == true then setNoclip(true) end
		if data.nofog == true then setNofog(true) end
		if tonumber(data.lightLevel) then HK.light.setLevel(tonumber(data.lightLevel), true) end
		if data.light == true then HK.light.set(true) end
		if data.infJumpMode == "tap" or data.infJumpMode == "hold" then HK.infjump.setMode(data.infJumpMode, true) end
		if data.infJump == true then HK.infjump.set(true) end
		if data.infZoomMode == "normal" or data.infZoomMode == "walls" then HK.infzoom.setMode(data.infZoomMode, true) end
		if data.infZoom == true then HK.infzoom.set(true) end
		if data.noFallDamage == true then HK.nofall.set(true) end
		if type(data.extras) == "table" and Settings.DX.applySave then pcall(Settings.DX.applySave, data.extras) end
		if type(data.ab) == "table" and Settings.ABsave then pcall(Settings.ABsave.apply, data.ab) end
		if type(data.bb) == "table" and Settings.BBsave then pcall(Settings.BBsave.apply, data.bb) end
		if type(data.bp) == "table" and Settings.BPsave then pcall(Settings.BPsave.apply, data.bp) end
		if type(data.av) == "table" and Settings.AVsave then pcall(Settings.AVsave.apply, data.av) end

		-- Fly and the Fruit Summoning Loop need your character, so they come back a moment after load
		local function whenReady(fn)
			task.spawn(function()
				local t0 = os.clock()
				while not (LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")) and os.clock() - t0 < 15 do
					task.wait(0.5)
				end
				task.wait(2.5)
				if not Settings.stopped then pcall(fn) end
			end)
		end
		if data.fly == true then whenReady(function() HK.fly.set(true) end) end
		-- Fruit farm is session-only: never auto-start after kick / leave / rejoin
		-- (old saves may still have macro=true; ignore it)
	end
end

		  custom = "<b>Boss Drops:</b> (Best drops to get from this boss)\n\n• <b>Ghostly Bracelet:</b> A vital quest item dropped upon defeat. You can take this drop to a hidden room behind a wall near Windy Planes 2 to complete a girl's quest, which rewards you with a tracker fragment. It is also required for Otsutsuki players to obtain blood weapons." },
		{ name = "Haku Boss", icon = "❄️", loc = "Mirror Realm", alias = {"haku"}, minHP = 500,
		  custom = "I was not able to put a tracker on this boss since you need the Haku Bloodline. However, I have set the location and waypoint for where to start the fight.\n\n<b>Boss Drops:</b> (Best drops to get from this boss)\n\n• <b>Ice Mirror / Sensatsu Suishou Scroll:</b> A rare sub-ability technique scroll that permanently unlocks Ice Style moves like Crystal Ice Mirrors and Thousand Flying Water Needles.\n\n• <b>Haku's Mask:</b> A rare unique cosmetic vanity mask modeled after the Yuki clan hunter-nin face wear.\n\n• <b>Legendary Loot:</b> Offers a chance to drop high-tier server-wide rewards, including upgrade gems, specialized weapon schematics, rings, and surgical spoons." },
		{ name = "One-Tailed Shukaku", group = "beast", icon = "🏜️", loc = "Shukaku's Embrace", diff = "Event", alias = {"shukaku"},
		  custom = "<b>Boss Drops:</b> (Best drops to get from this boss)\n\n• <b>Shukaku Tail:</b> A scroll that lets you summon the Shukaku boss manually at the Beast Tamer NPC.\n\n• <b>Shukaku Soul:</b> Allows an Uzumaki or Senju to seal the beast inside you, making you a Jinchuriki with 4 transformation stages and Magnetism moves.\n\n• <b>Senju Progression Milestone:</b> A completion requirement for the Senju questline to unlock Deep Forest Emergence.\n\n• <b>Legendary Loot:</b> Drops rare gear, weapons, and gem schematics." },
		{ name = "Two-Tailed Matatabi", group = "beast", icon = "🔥", loc = "Matatabi's Embrace", alias = {"matatabi"},
		  custom = "<b>Boss Drops:</b> (Best drops to get from this boss)\n\n• <b>Matatabi Tail:</b> A scroll that lets you summon the Matatabi boss manually at the Beast Tamer NPC.\n\n• <b>Matatabi Soul:</b> Allows an Uzumaki or Senju to seal the beast inside you, making you a Jinchuriki with transformation stages, blue fire burn damage, and high-mobility attacks.\n\n• <b>Legendary Loot:</b> Drops rare gear, weapons, and gem schematics." },
		{ name = "Three-Tailed Isobu", group = "beast", icon = "🐢", loc = "Isobu's Grotto", alias = {"isobu"},
		  custom = "<b>Boss Drops:</b> (Best drops to get from this boss)\n\n• <b>Isobu Tail:</b> A scroll that lets you summon the Isobu boss manually at the Beast Tamer NPC.\n\n• <b>Isobu Soul:</b> Allows an Uzumaki or Senju to seal the beast inside you, making you a Jinchuriki with transformation stages, defensive coral stun moves, and water damage buffs.\n\n• <b>Legendary Loot:</b> Drops rare gear, weapons, and gem schematics." },
	}
	for _, b in ipairs(BOSSES) do b.key = b.alias[1] end

	-- ---------- name matching ----------
	local function normName(s)
		s = string.lower(tostring(s or ""))
		s = s:gsub("[^%w]", "")
		s = s:gsub("%d+$", "")
		s = s:gsub("boss", "")
		return s
	end
	local aliasMap = {}
	for i, b in ipairs(BOSSES) do
		for _, a in ipairs(b.alias) do aliasMap[normName(a)] = i end
	end
	local function bossIndexFor(nm, loose)
		if not nm or nm == "" then return nil end
		local k = normName(nm)
		local hit = aliasMap[k]
		if hit then return hit end
		if loose then
			-- loose match (only bosses flagged fuzzy): the name just has to CONTAIN the alias,
			-- e.g. "Barbarian_Sitting", "BarbarianNPC", "The Barbarian King"
			for i, b in ipairs(BOSSES) do
				if b.fuzzy then
					for _, a in ipairs(b.alias) do
						if #a >= 7 and string.find(k, normName(a), 1, true) then return i end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	-- ---------- tracker state ----------
	local ST = {}
	for i = 1, #BOSSES do ST[i] = { recs = {}, alive = false, everSeen = false } end
	local reg = setmetatable({}, { __mode = "k" }) -- humanoid -> rec
	local bigNpcs = {}                              -- unknown high-HP NPCs (name finder)
	local scanDone = false
	local dirtySave = false

	local function rootPos()
		local c = LocalPlayer.Character
		local r = c and c:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		return r and r.Position or nil
	end
	local function pivotPos(model)
		local ok, p = pcall(function() return model:GetPivot().Position end)
		return ok and p or nil
	end
	local function streamingOn()
		local ok, v = pcall(function() return workspace.StreamingEnabled end)
		return ok and v == true
	end
	-- distance (studs) around a boss spawn spot inside which a missing boss really means "not spawned"
	local function verifyRadius()
		local ok, r = pcall(function()
			if not workspace.StreamingEnabled then return math.huge end
			local tr = workspace.StreamingTargetRadius
			local mr = workspace.StreamingMinRadius
			return math.max(mr, math.min(tr * 0.5, 400))
		end)
		if ok and r then return r end
		return 150
	end
	local function fmt(sec)
		sec = math.max(0, math.floor(sec))
		return string.format("%d:%02d", math.floor(sec / 60), sec % 60)
	end

	-- ---------- saved spawn spots (so "not there" can be verified later) ----------
	local function saveHomes()
		local wrote = false
		pcall(function()
			if not writefile then return end
			local data = { place = game.PlaceId, homes = {}, tps = {} }
			for i, b in ipairs(BOSSES) do
				local h = ST[i].home
				if h then data.homes[b.key] = { h.X, h.Y, h.Z } end
				local t = ST[i].tp
				if t then data.tps[b.key] = { t.X, t.Y, t.Z } end
			end
			local prev = Settings.readJson(SAVE_FILE)
			if type(prev) == "table" then
				local others = type(prev.others) == "table" and prev.others or {}
				if prev.place and prev.place ~= game.PlaceId then
					others[tostring(prev.place)] = { homes = prev.homes, tps = prev.tps }
				end
				others[tostring(game.PlaceId)] = nil
				data.others = others
			end
			wrote = Settings.writeJson(SAVE_FILE, data)
		end)
		return wrote
	end
	pcall(function()
		do
			local data = Settings.readJson(SAVE_FILE)
			if type(data) == "table" and data.place ~= game.PlaceId and type(data.others) == "table" then
				data = data.others[tostring(game.PlaceId)]
			end
			if type(data) == "table" and (data.place == game.PlaceId or data.place == nil) and type(data.homes) == "table" then
				for i, b in ipairs(BOSSES) do
					local h = data.homes[b.key]
					if type(h) == "table" and tonumber(h[1]) and tonumber(h[2]) and tonumber(h[3]) then
						ST[i].home = Vector3.new(tonumber(h[1]), tonumber(h[2]), tonumber(h[3]))
					end
					local t = type(data.tps) == "table" and data.tps[b.key]
					if type(t) == "table" and tonumber(t[1]) and tonumber(t[2]) and tonumber(t[3]) then
						ST[i].tp = Vector3.new(tonumber(t[1]), tonumber(t[2]), tonumber(t[3]))
					end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	-- ---------- raw "timer-looking" data the game puts on a boss (shown as RAW, never interpreted) ----------
	local RAW_WORDS = {"despawn", "respawn", "spawn", "timer", "expire", "lifetime", "cooldown", "countdown"}
	local function looksTimer(k)
		k = string.lower(tostring(k))
		for _, w in ipairs(RAW_WORDS) do
			if string.find(k, w, 1, true) then return true end
		end
		return false
	end
	local function readRaw(model, hum)
		local out = {}
		pcall(function()
			for _, inst in ipairs({ model, hum }) do
				for k, v in pairs(inst:GetAttributes()) do
					if looksTimer(k) and #out < 3 then out[#out + 1] = tostring(k) .. "=" .. tostring(v) end
				end
			end
			for _, ch in ipairs(model:GetChildren()) do
				if #out >= 3 then break end
				if ch:IsA("ValueBase") and looksTimer(ch.Name) then
					out[#out + 1] = ch.Name .. "=" .. tostring(ch.Value)
				end
			end
		end)
		if #out == 0 then return nil end
		return table.concat(out, ", ")
	end

	-- ---------- spawn-spot check ----------
	-- Bosses flagged atHome (Barbarian, Manda, Barbarit The Rose) also exist as personal QUEST copies
	-- somewhere else. Once you saved the exact spawn spot, only a boss standing at that spot counts.
	local HOME_R = 60 -- studs around the saved spawn spot
	local function gated(idx)
		return idx and BOSSES[idx].atHome and ST[idx].home ~= nil
	end
	local function atSpawn(rec)
		if not gated(rec.idx) then return true end
		if rec.homeOk then return true end
		local p = pivotPos(rec.model)
		if p and (p - ST[rec.idx].home).Magnitude <= HOME_R then
			rec.homeOk = true -- once accepted it stays accepted (the boss may chase you while fighting)
			return true
		end
		return false
	end

	-- ---------- live humanoid tracking ----------
	local onGone, onDied
	onDied = function(rec)
		rec.dead = true
		if rec.idx and atSpawn(rec) then
			local st = ST[rec.idx]
			local now = os.time()
			st.goneKind = "died"
			st.goneAt = now
			st.deadAt = st.deadAt or now
		end
	end
	onGone = function(rec)
		if rec.gone then return end
		rec.gone = true
		reg[rec.hum] = nil
		pcall(function() if rec.c1 then rec.c1:Disconnect() end end)
		pcall(function() if rec.c2 then rec.c2:Disconnect() end end)
		if not rec.idx then
			bigNpcs[rec.hum] = nil
			return
		end
		local st = ST[rec.idx]
		st.recs[rec.hum] = nil
		if gated(rec.idx) and not rec.homeOk then return end -- a quest copy elsewhere: ignore
		for _, o in pairs(st.recs) do
			if not gated(o.idx) or o.homeOk then return end -- another real copy is still loaded
		end
		local now = os.time()
		local hp = 1
		pcall(function() hp = rec.hum.Health end)
		if rec.stale then
			st.goneKind = "died_unknown"
			st.goneAt = now
		elseif rec.dead or hp <= 0 then
			st.goneKind = "died"
			st.goneAt = now
			st.deadAt = st.deadAt or now
		else
			local pp = rootPos()
			if pp and rec.lastPos and (pp - rec.lastPos).Magnitude <= verifyRadius() then
				-- it vanished while you were close to it: real despawn
				st.goneKind = "despawned"
				st.goneAt = now
				st.deadAt = st.deadAt or now
			else
				-- you were far away / teleported: probably just streamed out
				st.goneKind = "streamed"
				st.goneAt = now
			end
		end
		st.aliveSince = nil
	end

	local function registerHumanoid(hum, forceIdx)
		if not running then return end
		if reg[hum] then
			-- already known as a nameless big NPC, but we now know which boss it is
			if forceIdx and not reg[hum].idx then
				pcall(function() if reg[hum].c1 then reg[hum].c1:Disconnect() end end)
				pcall(function() if reg[hum].c2 then reg[hum].c2:Disconnect() end end)
				bigNpcs[hum] = nil
				reg[hum] = nil
			else
				return
			end
		end
		local model = hum.Parent
		if not model or not model:IsA("Model") then return end
		if Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(model) then return end
		local idx = forceIdx or bossIndexFor(model.Name)
		if not idx then
			local okD, dn = pcall(function() return hum.DisplayName end)
			if okD then idx = bossIndexFor(dn) end
		end
		if not idx then
			-- loose name match on the model, its display name and the models above it (a few levels)
			idx = bossIndexFor(model.Name, true)
			if not idx then
				local okD, dn = pcall(function() return hum.DisplayName end)
				if okD then idx = bossIndexFor(dn, true) end
			end
			local anc, lv = model.Parent, 0
			while not idx and anc and anc ~= workspace and lv < 4 do
				if anc:IsA("Model") then idx = bossIndexFor(anc.Name, true) end
				anc = anc.Parent
				lv = lv + 1
			end
		end
		local mh = 0
		pcall(function() mh = hum.MaxHealth end)
		if idx and BOSSES[idx].minHP and mh < BOSSES[idx].minHP then idx = nil end
		if not idx and mh < BIG_HP then return end
		local hp0 = 1
		pcall(function() hp0 = hum.Health end)
		if not idx and hp0 <= 0 then return end
		local rec = { hum = hum, model = model, idx = idx, mh = mh, lastPos = pivotPos(model), stale = hp0 <= 0 }
		reg[hum] = rec
		if idx then ST[idx].recs[hum] = rec else bigNpcs[hum] = rec end
		rec.c1 = hum.AncestryChanged:Connect(function()
			if not hum:IsDescendantOf(workspace) then onGone(rec) end
		end)
		rec.c2 = hum.Died:Connect(function() onDied(rec) end)
	end

	local wsConn = workspace.DescendantAdded:Connect(function(inst)
		if inst.ClassName == "Humanoid" then
			task.defer(function() pcall(registerHumanoid, inst) end)
		end
	end)

	-- one gentle, time-sliced pass over containers only (never GetDescendants on the whole map)
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(4)
		local queue, depthOf = { workspace }, { [workspace] = 0 }
		local qi, visited, lastY = 1, 0, os.clock()
		while running and qi <= #queue and visited < 30000 do
			local inst = queue[qi]
			qi = qi + 1
			local d = depthOf[inst] or 0
			local ok, kids = pcall(function() return inst:GetChildren() end)
			if ok and kids then
				for _, c in ipairs(kids) do
					local cn = c.ClassName
					if cn == "Humanoid" then
						pcall(registerHumanoid, c)
					elseif (cn == "Model" or cn == "Folder") and d < 8 then
						if not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(c) then
							queue[#queue + 1] = c
							depthOf[c] = d + 1
						end
					end
				end
			end
			visited = visited + 1
			if os.clock() - lastY > 0.002 then
				task.wait()
				lastY = os.clock()
			end
		end
		scanDone = qi > #queue -- true only if the pass finished (not cut off by the size cap)
		queue, depthOf = nil, nil
	end)

	-- ---------- far-away checking ----------
	-- Asks the game to load the area around each saved boss spawn spot (up to DETECT_RANGE studs away),
	-- so the boss can show ALIVE (or a real DEAD) long before you walk up to it.
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(8)
		while running do
			local pp = rootPos()
			if TRACK_FAR and pp and streamingOn() then
				for i, st in ipairs(ST) do
					if not running then break end
					if st.home and not st.alive and (pp - st.home).Magnitude <= DETECT_RANGE
						and os.time() - (st.farTry or 0) >= 8 then
						st.farTry = os.time()
						local ok = pcall(function() LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(st.home, 2) end)
						if ok then
							task.wait(1.3) -- let the boss register if it streamed in
							if not st.alive then st.farCheckAt = os.time() end
						end
						task.wait(0.5)
					end
				end
			end
			task.wait(3)
		end
	end)

	-- ---------- spawn-spot fallback ----------
	-- If you marked a boss's exact spawn and stand near it but the tracker still can not find it by name
	-- (odd model name, boss sitting, lower max HP...), take the biggest NPC standing at that spot.
	task.spawn(function()
		task.wait(10)
		while running do
			local pp = rootPos()
			if pp then
				for i, b in ipairs(BOSSES) do
					local st = ST[i]
					if b.near and st.home and not st.alive and (pp - st.home).Magnitude <= math.min(DETECT_RANGE, 350) then
						pcall(function()
							local op = OverlapParams.new()
							op.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
							local ex = {}
							for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
								if plr.Character then ex[#ex + 1] = plr.Character end
							end
							op.FilterDescendantsInstances = ex
							local best, bestMH = nil, 0
							for _, part in ipairs(workspace:GetPartBoundsInRadius(st.home, b.near, op)) do
								local m = part:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
								local hum = m and m:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
								if hum and not Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(m) then
									local other = bossIndexFor(m.Name) or bossIndexFor(m.Name, true)
									if not other or other == i then
										if hum.MaxHealth > bestMH and hum.Health > 0 then best, bestMH = hum, hum.MaxHealth end
									end
								end
							end
							if best then registerHumanoid(best, i) end
						end)
					end
				end
			end
			task.wait(3)
		end
	end)

	-- ---------- state refresh (every 0.5 s, cheap) ----------
	local function refreshStates()
		local now = os.time()
		local pp = rootPos()
		for i, st in ipairs(ST) do
			local aliveRec, deadRec
			for hum, rec in pairs(st.recs) do
				local hp, mh = 0, 1
				pcall(function() hp = hum.Health; mh = hum.MaxHealth end)
				if not atSpawn(rec) then
					-- not at the saved spawn spot (quest boss copy): ignored
				elseif hp > 0 then
					aliveRec = aliveRec or rec
					local p = pivotPos(rec.model)
					if p then rec.lastPos = p end
					rec.hp, rec.mh = hp, mh
				else
					deadRec = rec
				end
			end
			if aliveRec then
				if not st.alive then
					st.alive = true
					st.aliveSince = now
					st.deadAt, st.goneKind, st.goneAt = nil, nil, nil
				end
				st.everSeen = true
				st.hp, st.mh = aliveRec.hp, aliveRec.mh
				st.pos = aliveRec.lastPos
				st.dist = (pp and aliveRec.lastPos) and (pp - aliveRec.lastPos).Magnitude or nil
				st.lastSeenAlive = now
				st.raw = readRaw(aliveRec.model, aliveRec.hum)
				if not st.home and not BOSSES[i].atHome and aliveRec.lastPos and aliveRec.mh and aliveRec.mh > 0
					and aliveRec.hp / aliveRec.mh >= 0.95 then
					st.home = aliveRec.lastPos -- learned: boss seen at full HP = its spawn spot
					dirtySave = true
				end
			else
				st.alive = false
				st.hp, st.dist = nil, nil
				if deadRec and st.goneKind ~= "died" and st.goneKind ~= "died_unknown" then
					st.goneAt = now
					if deadRec.stale then
						st.goneKind = "died_unknown"
					else
						st.goneKind = "died"
						st.deadAt = st.deadAt or now
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if dirtySave then
			dirtySave = false
			saveHomes()
		end
	end

	-- ---------- what to show for each boss ----------
	local function classify(i)
		local st = ST[i]
		local now = os.time()
		if st.alive then
			local parts = { "Alive" }
			if st.hp and st.mh and st.mh > 0 then
				parts[#parts + 1] = string.format("%d%% HP", math.floor(st.hp / st.mh * 100 + 0.5))
			end
			if st.dist then parts[#parts + 1] = string.format("%d studs away", math.floor(st.dist + 0.5)) end
			local t = "⏱ Seen alive " .. fmt(now - (st.aliveSince or now)) .. " (est. - counted from when you first saw it)\nDespawn timer: not exposed by the game"
			if st.raw then t = t .. "\nGame data (raw): " .. st.raw end
			return { kind = "alive", status = table.concat(parts, "  •  "), timer = t }
		end

		local gk = st.goneKind
		if gk == "died_unknown" then
			local el = now - (st.goneAt or now)
			if el > RESPAWN_MAX then
				return { kind = "grey", status = "Seen defeated " .. fmt(el) .. " ago - may be back",
					timer = "Past the guide's 30 min window.\nGo near its spawn spot to check." }
			end
			return { kind = "red", status = "Defeated (you saw the body)",
				timer = "Time of death unknown (first seen " .. fmt(el) .. " ago).\nRespawn est.: up to 30 min (guide - estimate)" }
		end
		if (gk == "died" or gk == "despawned") and st.deadAt then
			local el = now - st.deadAt
			local what = (gk == "died") and "Defeated " or "Despawned "
			if el <= RESPAWN_MAX then
				local t
				if el < RESPAWN_MIN then
					t = "Respawn est.: in " .. fmt(RESPAWN_MIN - el) .. " to " .. fmt(RESPAWN_MAX - el)
				else
					t = "Respawn est.: any moment, latest in " .. fmt(RESPAWN_MAX - el)
				end
				t = t .. "\n(guide says 15-30 min - estimate, not game data)"
				return { kind = "red", status = what .. fmt(el) .. " ago", timer = t }
			end
			return { kind = "grey", status = what .. fmt(el) .. " ago - may be back",
				timer = "Past the guide's 30 min window.\nGo near its spawn spot to check." }
		end

		if not st.everSeen and not st.home and streamingOn() == false and scanDone then
			return { kind = "red", status = "Not spawned (whole map is loaded)",
				timer = "Respawn: unknown (guides say 15-30 min)" }
		end

		if st.home then
			local pp = rootPos()
			if not streamingOn() and scanDone then
				return { kind = "red", status = "Not spawned (whole map is loaded)",
					timer = "Respawn: unknown (guides say 15-30 min)" }
			end
			if pp then
				local d = (pp - st.home).Magnitude
				local r = verifyRadius()
				if d <= r then
					return { kind = "red", status = "Not spawned - nothing at its spawn spot",
						timer = "You are in render range of its spawn spot.\nRespawn: unknown (guides say 15-30 min)" }
				end
				if st.farCheckAt and now - st.farCheckAt <= 12 and d <= DETECT_RANGE + 50 then
					return { kind = "red", status = "Not spawned - checked at its spawn spot",
						timer = "Checked from " .. math.floor(d) .. " studs away.\nRespawn: unknown (guides say 15-30 min)" }
				end
				if gk == "streamed" and st.lastSeenAlive then
					return { kind = "grey", status = "Out of range",
						timer = "Last seen alive " .. fmt(now - st.lastSeenAlive) .. " ago - can't tell since.\nGo within ~" .. math.floor(r) .. " studs of its spawn spot." }
				end
				return { kind = "grey", status = "Unknown - too far to render",
					timer = "Go within ~" .. math.floor(r) .. " studs of its spawn spot to check.\nGrey does not mean dead." }
			end
		end

		if gk == "streamed" and st.lastSeenAlive then
			return { kind = "grey", status = "Out of range",
				timer = "Last seen alive " .. fmt(now - st.lastSeenAlive) .. " ago - can't tell since.\nWalk back within render range to update." }
		end
		return { kind = "grey", status = "Unknown - not seen yet",
			timer = "Not loaded on your client (StreamingEnabled).\nGet within render range of it to check." }
	end

	-- ====================== UI ======================
	local function measure(text, size, font, width)
		local plain = text:gsub("<[^>]+>", "")
		local ok, v = pcall(function() return TextService:GetTextSize(plain, size, font, Vector2.new(width, 10000)) end)
		if ok and v then return math.ceil(v.Y) end
		return (math.ceil(#plain / math.max(1, math.floor(width / (size * 0.48)))) + 1) * (size + 2)
	end
	local function mkText(parent, text, pos, size, ts, color, font)
		local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		l.Position = pos
		l.Size = size
		l.Text = text
		l.TextColor3 = color or DIMC
		l.Font = font or FONT_N
		l.TextSize = ts or 14
		l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		l.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		l.Parent = parent
		return l
	end
	local function cardHead(card, text)
		local hdr = mkText(card, text, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 5), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 26), 19, CREAM, FONT_S)
		hdr.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		hdr.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		local line = Instance.new("Frame")
		line.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
		line.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 33)
		line.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER
		line.BorderSizePixel = 0
		line.Parent = card
		addFadeLine(line, 0)
		return hdr
	end
	local function circle(parent, size, pos, color)
		local f = Instance.new("Frame")
		f.Size = UDim2.new(0, size, 0, size)
		f.Position = pos
		f.BackgroundColor3 = color
		f.BorderSizePixel = 0
		f.Parent = parent
		addCorner(f, size)
		return mark(f)
	end
	local function backButton(parent)
		local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
		b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 24)
		b.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 14)
		b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(31, 49, 61)
		b.Text = "‹  Back"
		b.TextColor3 = CREAM
		b.Font = FONT_S
		b.TextSize = 17
		b.AutoButtonColor = false
		b.Parent = parent
		addCorner(b, 5)
		addStroke(b, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.2)
		addHover(b, Color3.fromRGB(31, 49, 61), Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103))
		return b
	end

	-- ---------- the Bosses page ----------
	local BossesPage = Instance.new("Frame")
	BossesPage.Name = "BossesPage"
	BossesPage.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
	BossesPage.Position = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 0)
	BossesPage.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	BossesPage.BorderSizePixel = 0
	BossesPage.Visible = false
	BossesPage.Parent = Pages
	pageFrames.bosses = BossesPage

	backButton(BossesPage).MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showPage("home") end)

	local head = mkText(BossesPage, "Bosses", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 44), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 28), 25, CREAM, FONT_S)
	head.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	head.TextStrokeTransparency = 0.6
	head.TextStrokeColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(10, 10, 12)
	local desc = mkText(BossesPage, "Live status, timers & info - tap a boss", UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 74), UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 20), 14, Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255), FONT_N)
	desc.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
	desc:SetAttribute("KeepText", true)
	addDivider(BossesPage, 98, 18)

	local BossesScroll = Instance.new("ScrollingFrame")
	BossesScroll.Name = "BossesScroll"
	BossesScroll.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 1, -118)
	BossesScroll.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 110)
	BossesScroll.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	BossesScroll.BorderSizePixel = 0
	BossesScroll.ScrollBarThickness = 3
	BossesScroll.ScrollBarImageColor3 = SILVER
	BossesScroll.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0)
	BossesScroll.AutomaticCanvasSize = Enum.AutomaticSize.Y
	BossesScroll.ScrollingDirection = Enum.ScrollingDirection.Y
	BossesScroll.Parent = BossesPage
	local bl = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	bl.Padding = UDim.new(0, 7)
	bl.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	bl.Parent = BossesScroll
	local bp = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	bp.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 2)
	bp.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 5)
	bp.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 2)
	bp.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 8)
	bp.Parent = BossesScroll

	local INNER_W = 245 -- text width inside a card (panel 312 - scroll margins - card padding)

	-- ---------- legend / honesty card ----------
	do
		local legend = {
			{ GREEN, "Alive - the boss is loaded on your client. Shows its HP and how far away it is." },
			{ REDC, "Dead - you saw it die or vanish, or it is missing from its known spawn spot while you are close enough for it to render." },
			{ GREY, "Unknown - too far away to render, or never seen. Grey does NOT mean dead." },
		}
		local y = 42
		local rows = {}
		for _, row in ipairs(legend) do
			local h = measure(row[2], 13, FONT_N, INNER_W - 24)
			rows[#rows + 1] = { row[1], row[2], y, h }
			y = y + h + 8
		end
		local note = "Timers marked est. come from guides, not from the game. The game exposes no boss timer that this tracker can read, so nothing here is an exact countdown."
		local nh = measure(note, 13, FONT_N, INNER_W)
		local card = createCard(y + nh + 14, 1, BossesScroll, true)
		cardHead(card, "👹  Live Boss Tracker")
		for _, r in ipairs(rows) do
			circle(card, 10, UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, r[3] + 3), r[1])
			local l = mkText(card, r[2], UDim2.new(0, 36, 0, r[3]), UDim2.new(1, -50, 0, r[4]), 13, DIMC, FONT_N)
			l.TextWrapped = true
		end
		local nl = mkText(card, note, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, nh), 13, Color3.fromRGB(190, 200, 210), FONT_N)
		nl.TextWrapped = true
	end

	-- ---------- one card per boss ----------
	local cards = {}
	local function buildBossCard(i, b)
		local card = createCard(CARD_H, (b.group == "beast" and 200 or 10) + i, BossesScroll)
		local c = { card = card, kind = nil, expanded = false }

		-- status dot (LEFT of the name) with a soft pulsing halo
		c.halo = circle(card, 22, UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 9), GREY)
		c.halo.BackgroundTransparency = 0.82
		c.dot = circle(card, 12, UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, 14), GREY)

		local title = mkText(card, b.name, UDim2.new(0, 42, 0, 8), UDim2.new(1, -140, 0, 24), 19, CREAM, FONT_S)
		title.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		title.TextScaled = true
		local tsc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
		tsc.MaxTextSize = 19
		tsc.MinTextSize = 11
		tsc.Parent = title

		-- status chip (right)
		local chip = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		chip.Size = UDim2.new(0, 84, 0, 20)
		chip.Position = UDim2.new(1, -96, 0, 10)
		chip.BackgroundColor3 = CHIP_BG
		chip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		chip.Text = "UNKNOWN"
		chip.TextColor3 = GREY
		chip.Font = FONT_BD
		chip.TextSize = 12
		chip.Parent = card
		mark(chip)
		c.chip = chip

		mkText(card, "📍 " .. (b.loc or "Location not added yet"), UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 33), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 16), 13, DIMC, FONT_N).TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

		c.status = mkText(card, "Unknown - not seen yet", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 51), UDim2.new(1, -44, 0, 18), 15, GREY, FONT_S)
		c.status.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		mark(c.status)

		c.timer = mkText(card, "", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 70), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 50), 13, DIMC, FONT_N)
		c.timer.TextWrapped = true

		-- arrow drawn from two bars (no font symbol), flips up/down when the card opens
		local arrow = Instance.new("Frame")
		arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		arrow.BorderSizePixel = 0
		arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 18, 0, 18)
		arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -30, 0, 50)
		arrow.Parent = card
		for _, side in ipairs({ -1, 1 }) do
			local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
			bar.AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0.5)
			bar.Size = UDim2.new(0, 11, 0, 2)
			bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 9 + side * 3.6, 0, 8.5)
			bar.Rotation = -side * 45
			bar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(205, 215, 225)
			bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
			bar.Parent = arrow
			addCorner(bar, 1)
		end

		-- expandable guide info (hidden by the card clipping while collapsed)
		local parts = {}
		if b.access then parts[#parts + 1] = "<b>Where / how to reach:</b> " .. b.access end
		if b.attacks then parts[#parts + 1] = "<b>Attacks:</b> " .. b.attacks end
		if b.drops then parts[#parts + 1] = "<b>Drops:</b> " .. b.drops end
		local noInfo = #parts == 0
		if noInfo then parts[1] = "<i>Guide info for this boss has not been added yet.</i>" end
		local rich = table.concat(parts, "\n\n")
		if b.custom then
			rich = b.custom
		elseif b.note then rich = rich .. "\n\n<i>" .. b.note .. "</i>" end
		local tH = measure(rich, 14, FONT_N, INNER_W) + (b.custom and 22 or 6) -- extra room: bold text wraps a bit wider
		local sepLine = Instance.new("Frame")
		sepLine.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
		sepLine.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, CARD_H + 1)
		sepLine.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER
		sepLine.BorderSizePixel = 0
		sepLine.Parent = card
		addFadeLine(sepLine, 0)

		local base = CARD_H + 10
		local info = mkText(card, rich, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, base), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, tH), 14, Color3.fromRGB(205, 215, 225), FONT_N)
		info.RichText = true
		info.TextWrapped = true
		-- expanded height: just the guide text with even padding (no spawn/raw/extra TP rows)
		c.fullH = base + tH + 14

		-- pulse (only while alive)
		c.pulse = TweenService:Create(c.halo, TweenInfo.new(1.1, Enum.EasingStyle.Sine, Enum.EasingDirection.InOut, -1, true), { BackgroundTransparency = 0.92 })

		-- click the collapsed part of the card to open / close the guide info
		local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
		btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, CARD_H)
		btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		btn.Text = ""
		btn.AutoButtonColor = false
		btn.Parent = card
		btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			c.expanded = not c.expanded
			tween(arrow, 0.28, { Rotation = c.expanded and 180 or 0 })
			tween(card, 0.28, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, c.expanded and c.fullH or CARD_H) })
		end)

		-- clean WAYPOINT button (same glowing beam + guide line as the Teleports page)
		c.wp = { name = b.name }
		local row = Instance.new("Frame")
		row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		row.BorderSizePixel = 0
		row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28)
		row.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 124)
		row.Parent = card
		-- Buttons are built exactly like the Teleports page buttons (static BTN / BTN_RED color, silver
		-- stroke, see-through hover overlay) and are NEVER recolored at runtime, so the GUI theme
		-- (color, saturation, brightness, transparency) controls them like every other button.
		local function bossBtn(text, pos, size, color, hover, scaled)
			local bb = Instance.new("TextButton")
			bb.Size = size
			bb.Position = pos
			bb.BackgroundColor3 = color
			bb.Text = text
			bb.TextColor3 = CREAM
			bb.Font = FONT_S
			bb.TextSize = 15
			bb.AutoButtonColor = false
			if scaled then
				bb.TextScaled = true
				local sc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
				sc.MaxTextSize = 15
				sc.MinTextSize = 10
				sc.Parent = bb
			end
			bb.Parent = row
			addCorner(bb, 5)
			addStroke(bb, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
			addHover(bb, color, hover)
			return bb
		end
		local WP_POS, WP_SIZE = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.62, -3, 0, 28)
		local wpb = bossBtn("WAYPOINT", WP_POS, WP_SIZE, BTN, BTN_HOVER, true)          -- normal
		local wpr = bossBtn("CLEAR WAYPOINT", WP_POS, WP_SIZE, BTN_RED, BTN_RED_HOVER, true) -- active (red, like Teleports)
		wpr.Visible = false
		c.wpBtn = wpb
		local function wpShown() return c.wpActive and wpr or wpb end
		c.setWp = function(active, has)
			c.wpActive, c.wpHas = active, has
			wpr.Visible = active and true or false
			wpb.Visible = not active
			wpb.Text = "WAYPOINT"
			wpb.TextTransparency = has and 0 or 0.45 -- dimmed (not recolored) when no spot is known yet
		end
		local function onWp()
			local SP = Settings.SP
			if not SP then
				wpShown().Text = "Waypoint not available"
				return
			end
			if SP.active and SP.active.sp == c.wp then
				SP.clear()
				c.setWp(false, c.wpHas)
				return
			end
			local st = ST[i]
			local pos = (st.alive and st.pos) or st.home or st.tp
			if not pos then
				wpb.Text = "Mark a spawn spot first"
				task.delay(1.8, function() c.setWp(c.wpActive, c.wpHas) end)
				return
			end
			c.wp.x, c.wp.y, c.wp.z = pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z
			local cp = Settings.nearestChakra and Settings.nearestChakra(pos)
			c.wpBase = c.wpBase or c.wp.name or ""
			c.wp.name = cp and ("TAKE " .. cp:upper() .. " → " .. c.wpBase) or c.wpBase
			SP.start(c.wp)
			c.setWp(true, true)
			if cp then
				wpr.Text = "TAKE " .. cp:upper()
				task.delay(5, function()
					if c.wpActive then wpr.Text = "CLEAR WAYPOINT" end
				end)
			end
		end
		wpb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(onWp)
		wpr.MouseButton1Click:Connect(onWp)

		-- TP button (right): teleports to the spot you saved. First tap saves where you stand.
		local tpb = bossBtn("SET TP", UDim2.new(0.62, 3, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.38, -3, 0, 28), BTN, BTN_HOVER, false)
		c.tpBtn = tpb
		c.setTp = function(has)
			c.tpHas = has
			tpb.Text = has and "TP" or "SET TP"
			tpb.TextTransparency = has and 0 or 0.45
		end
		local function playerRoot()
			local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
			return ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		end
		tpb.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			local st = ST[i]
			local root = playerRoot()
			if not root then return end
			if st.tp then
				-- safe long-distance TP: stream the target area in, hold the character still until the
				-- ground there has loaded, then release (no lag, no falling through the floor)
				if c.tping then return end
				c.tping = true
				local target = st.tp
				-- Never anchors the character (anchoring is what could lock you in place). While the ground at
				-- the target loads, you are pinned there every frame with zero velocity; a hard timer ends it.
				local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
				local params = RaycastParams.new()
				params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
				params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { ch }
				local done = false
				local conn
				local t0 = os.clock()
				local groundAt, lastRay
				local function finish()
					if done then return end
					done = true
					if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
					pcall(function() root.Anchored = false end)
					pcall(function() root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
					c.tping = false
				end
				pcall(function() root.CFrame = CFrame.new(target) end)
				conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
					if done then return end
					local r = playerRoot()
					if not r then finish() return end
					pcall(function()
						r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
						r.CFrame = CFrame.new(target)
					end)
					local nowRay = os.clock() -- [perf] ground check 10x/sec instead of every frame
				if lastRay and nowRay - lastRay < 0.1 then return end
				lastRay = nowRay
				local okHit, hit = pcall(function()
						return workspace:Raycast(target + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), params)
					end)
					if okHit and hit then
						groundAt = groundAt or os.clock()
						if os.clock() - groundAt > 0.25 then finish() end
					elseif os.clock() - t0 > 4 then
						finish()
					end
				end)
				task.delay(5, finish) -- hard stop, no matter what
				task.spawn(function() -- may yield; kept off the main flow so it can never hold you
					pcall(function()
						if LocalPlayer.RequestStreamAroundAsync then LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(target, 1.5) end
					end)
				end)
			else
				st.tp = root.Position
				saveHomes()
				c.setTp(true)
				tpb.Text = "✔ Saved"
				task.delay(1.2, function() c.setTp(c.tpHas) end)
			end
		end)

		cards[i] = c
	end
	local function sectionHeader(text, order)
		local h = mkText(BossesScroll, text, UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 26), 19, CREAM, FONT_S)
		h.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		h.LayoutOrder = order
		return h
	end
	sectionHeader("👹  Bosses", 5)
	sectionHeader("🦊  Tailed Beasts", 199)
	-- LAZY: build boss cards on first open (or in background) to avoid execute freeze
	local bossesBuilt = false
	local function ensureBossCards()
		if bossesBuilt then return end
		bossesBuilt = true
		task.spawn(function()
			for i, b in ipairs(BOSSES) do
				pcall(buildBossCard, i, b)
				if i % 2 == 0 then task.wait() end
			end
		end)
	end
	-- kick off in background shortly after load
	task.defer(function()
		task.wait(0.5)
		ensureBossCards()
	end)
	-- also when the bosses page is shown
	do
		local prevShow = showPage
		-- hook once via Settings
		Settings._ensureBossCards = ensureBossCards
	end

	local npcRows = {} -- "Big NPCs Near You" box removed

	-- ---------- respawn info card ----------
	do
		local txt = "<b>Respawn times:</b> guides disagree (one says every 15-30 min, another 30-45 min). Neither is confirmed, so this tracker uses 15-30 min only as a labeled estimate.\n\n<b>Bosses are per server.</b> A boss dead on one server can be alive on another.\n\n<b>Some bosses only appear after you trigger them</b> (golem, key, NPC). Grey is normal until then."
		local h = measure(txt, 13, FONT_N, INNER_W) + 4
		local card = createCard(h + 48, 310, BossesScroll, true)
		cardHead(card, "ℹ️  How bosses work")
		local l = mkText(card, txt, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 42), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, h), 13, Color3.fromRGB(205, 215, 225), FONT_N)
		l.RichText = true
		l.TextWrapped = true
	end

	-- ---------- refreshing the UI ----------
	local function applyKind(c, kind)
		c.kind = kind
		local col = (kind == "alive" and GREEN) or (kind == "red" and REDC) or GREY
		c.dot.BackgroundColor3 = col
		c.halo.BackgroundColor3 = col
		c.chip.Text = (kind == "alive" and "ALIVE") or (kind == "red" and "DEAD") or "UNKNOWN"
		c.chip.TextColor3 = col
		c.status.TextColor3 = col
		if kind == "alive" then
			c.halo.BackgroundTransparency = 0.5
			c.pulse:Play()
		else
			c.pulse:Cancel()
			c.halo.BackgroundTransparency = 0.82
		end
	end

	local function updateBossUI()
		for i, c in ipairs(cards) do
			local r = classify(i)
			if c.kind ~= r.kind then applyKind(c, r.kind) end
			do
				local st0 = ST[i]
				local act = Settings.SP ~= nil and Settings.SP.active ~= nil and Settings.SP.active.sp == c.wp
				local has = ((st0.alive and st0.pos) or st0.home or st0.tp) ~= nil
				if c.wpActive ~= act or c.wpHas ~= has then c.setWp(act, has) end
				local hasTp = st0.tp ~= nil
				if c.tpHas ~= hasTp then c.setTp(hasTp) end
			end
			if c.statusTxt ~= r.status then
				c.status.Text = r.status
				c.statusTxt = r.status
			end
			if c.timerTxt ~= r.timer then
				c.timer.Text = r.timer
				c.timerTxt = r.timer
			end
		end
	end

	-- ====================== HOME BOX (like Teleports) ======================
	local homeSub
	do
		local feat = HomePage:FindFirstChild("HomeFeatures")
		local parent = feat or HomePage
		local y = 492
		if feat then
			-- put the Bosses box right under "Fruit Summoning (AFK)" and push the boxes below it down one slot
			local fruitY
			for _, ch in ipairs(feat:GetChildren()) do
				if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.LayoutOrder == 1 and ch.Size.Y.Offset == 60 then fruitY = ch.Position.Y.Offset end
			end
			if fruitY then
				y = fruitY + 66
				for _, ch in ipairs(feat:GetChildren()) do
					if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.Position.Y.Offset >= y then
						ch.Position = UDim2.new(ch.Position.X.Scale, ch.Position.X.Offset, 0, ch.Position.Y.Offset + 66)
					end
				end
				feat.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, feat.CanvasSize.Y.Offset + 66)
			else
				y = (feat.CanvasSize.Y.Offset - 10) + 6
			end
		end
		local card = createCard(60, 7, parent, true)
		card.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, y)
		card.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 60)
		local stroke = card:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

		local slot = Instance.new("Frame")
		slot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50)
		slot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -25)
		slot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 30, 40)
		slot.BorderSizePixel = 0
		slot.Parent = card
		addCorner(slot, 6)
		mark(addStroke(slot, WHITE, 1.2, 0.5))
		local icon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		icon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		icon.Text = "👹"
		icon.TextSize = 26
		icon.Parent = slot

		local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 24)
		t.Position = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 7)
		t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		t.Text = "Bosses"
		t.TextColor3 = CREAM
		t.Font = FONT_S
		t.TextSize = 19
		t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		t.Parent = card

		homeSub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		homeSub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -122, 0, 28)
		homeSub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 29)
		homeSub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		homeSub.Text = "Live status, timers & boss info"
		homeSub.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
		homeSub.Font = FONT_N
		homeSub.TextSize = 14
		homeSub.TextWrapped = true
		homeSub.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		homeSub.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		homeSub.Parent = card

		local arrow = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 1, 0)
		arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 0)
		arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		arrow.Text = "›"
		arrow.TextColor3 = CREAM
		arrow.Font = FONT_BD
		arrow.TextSize = 34
		arrow.Parent = card

		local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
		btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		btn.Text = ""
		btn.AutoButtonColor = false
		btn.Parent = card
		btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			tween(stroke, 0.2, { Thickness = 2, Transparency = 0.12 })
			tween(arrow, 0.2, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -29, 0, 0) })
		end)
		btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			tween(stroke, 0.2, { Thickness = 1.2, Transparency = 0.55 })
			tween(arrow, 0.2, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 0) })
		end)
		btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showPage("bosses") end)

		if feat then feat.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(feat.CanvasSize.Y.Offset, y + 60 + 10)) end
	end

	-- ---------- main loop ----------
	task.spawn(function()
		local seenGui, tick = false, 0
		while running do
			if ScreenGui.Parent then
				seenGui = true
			elseif seenGui then
				break -- GUI was destroyed (re-executed)
			end
			pcall(refreshStates)
			if BossesPage.Visible then pcall(updateBossUI) end
			tick = tick + 1
			if tick % 4 == 0 then
				pcall(function()
					local n = 0
					for _, st in ipairs(ST) do if st.alive then n = n + 1 end end
					homeSub.Text = n > 0 and ("● " .. n .. " alive near you  •  " .. #BOSSES .. " tracked") or "Live status, timers & boss info"
				end)
			end
			task.wait(0.5)
		end
	end)

	-- clean shutdown when the GUI is re-executed / stopped
	local prevStop = Settings.DX.stop
	Settings.DX.stop = function()
		running = false
		pcall(function() wsConn:Disconnect() end)
		for hum, rec in pairs(reg) do
			pcall(function() if rec.c1 then rec.c1:Disconnect() end end)
			pcall(function() if rec.c2 then rec.c2:Disconnect() end end)
		end
		if prevStop then pcall(prevStop) end
	end
end, function(e) warn("[DannyGui] bosses error: " .. tostring(e)) end)

	-- Feed every NPC card into the home search bar (Settings.QuestSearch is read live by the search bar).

	local QST = {}
	local MULTI_LISTS = {
		foodmerchant = {"Sorythia", "Durana", "Rain", "Snow", "Rouge Food Merchant", "Fort Melo"},
		innkeeper = {"Sorythia", "Durana", "Rain", "Snow", "Akatsuki Hideout", "Fort Melo"},
		cp_zetsu = {"Combat Hall", "Rose Bay", "Chakra's Edge", "Rooftop Balcony"},

		npc_medic = {"Sorythia", "Durana", "Rain", "Snow", "Akatsuki Hideout", "Fort Melo", "Lava Light Town"},
		npc_merchant = {"Sorythia", "Durana", "Rain", "Snow", "Akatsuki Hideout", "Fort Melo", "Lava Light Town"},
		npc_judiciousjailor = {"Biyo Bay", "Fort Mello", "Training Grounds", "Passageway"},
		npc_kurenai = {"The Expanse Tree", "Desert Bones", "Windy Plains 2"},
		npc_exiledkunoichi = {"Windy Plains 2", "The Expanse", "Passageway"},
		-- Unstable Rifts: spread around the map. Add / remove / rename slots here.
		cp_unstablerift = {"Sorythia", "The Expanse", "Fort Mello", "Training Grounds", "Durana Village", "Oasis", "Chakra's Edge", "Rain Village", "Rosebay", "Snow Village", "Snow 3"},
	}
	local MULTI_KEYS = { foodmerchant = true, innkeeper = true, cp_zetsu = true, cp_unstablerift = true, npc_chef = true, npc_medic = true, npc_merchant = true, npc_judiciousjailor = true, npc_kurenai = true, npc_exiledkunoichi = true }
	for i, q in ipairs(QUESTS) do
		if MULTI_KEYS[q.key] then
			local villages = {}
			for _, vn in ipairs(MULTI_LISTS[q.key] or {}) do
				villages[vn] = { tp = nil, wp = nil }
			end
			QST[i] = { done = false, tp = nil, wp = nil, villages = villages, villageOrder = MULTI_LISTS[q.key] }
		else
			QST[i] = { done = false, tp = nil, wp = nil }
		end
	end

	local function saveQuests()
		pcall(function()
			if not writefile then return end
			local data = { place = game.PlaceId, tps = {}, wps = {}, multi = {}, done = {} }
			for i, q in ipairs(QUESTS) do
				local st = QST[i]
				if st.tp then data.tps[q.key] = { st.tp.X, st.tp.Y, st.tp.Z } end
				if st.wp then data.wps[q.key] = { st.wp.X, st.wp.Y, st.wp.Z } end
				if st.villages then
					data.multi[q.key] = {}
					for vn, v in pairs(st.villages) do
						data.multi[q.key][vn] = {
							tp = v.tp and { v.tp.X, v.tp.Y, v.tp.Z } or nil,
							wp = v.wp and { v.wp.X, v.wp.Y, v.wp.Z } or nil,
						}
					end
				end
				if st.done then data.done[q.key] = true end
			end
			-- keep the saved spots of other places of this game instead of overwriting them
			local prev = Settings.readJson(SAVE_FILE_Q)
			if type(prev) == "table" then
				local others = type(prev.others) == "table" and prev.others or {}
				if prev.place and prev.place ~= game.PlaceId then
					others[tostring(prev.place)] = { tps = prev.tps, wps = prev.wps, multi = prev.multi, done = prev.done }
				end
				others[tostring(game.PlaceId)] = nil
				data.others = others
			end
			Settings.writeJson(SAVE_FILE_Q, data)
		end)
	end
	pcall(function()
		do
			local data = Settings.readJson(SAVE_FILE_Q)
			if type(data) == "table" and data.place ~= game.PlaceId and type(data.others) == "table" then
				data = data.others[tostring(game.PlaceId)]
			end
			if type(data) == "table" and (data.place == game.PlaceId or data.place == nil) then
				for i, q in ipairs(QUESTS) do
					local t = data.tps and data.tps[q.key]
					if type(t) == "table" and tonumber(t[1]) then
						QST[i].tp = Vector3.new(tonumber(t[1]), tonumber(t[2]), tonumber(t[3]))
					end
					local w = data.wps and data.wps[q.key]
					if type(w) == "table" and tonumber(w[1]) then
						QST[i].wp = Vector3.new(tonumber(w[1]), tonumber(w[2]), tonumber(w[3]))
					end
					if data.multi and data.multi[q.key] and QST[i].villages then
						for vn, pack in pairs(data.multi[q.key]) do
							if QST[i].villages[vn] and type(pack) == "table" then
								if type(pack.tp) == "table" and tonumber(pack.tp[1]) then
									QST[i].villages[vn].tp = Vector3.new(tonumber(pack.tp[1]), tonumber(pack.tp[2]), tonumber(pack.tp[3]))
								end
								if type(pack.wp) == "table" and tonumber(pack.wp[1]) then
									QST[i].villages[vn].wp = Vector3.new(tonumber(pack.wp[1]), tonumber(pack.wp[2]), tonumber(pack.wp[3]))
								end
							end
						end
					end
					if data.done and data.done[q.key] then QST[i].done = true end
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	-- BAKED-IN LOCATIONS. Paste the output of EXPORT COORDS here so every spot is remembered inside the script.
	-- Only fills spots your saved file has not already set.
	local QUEST_DEFAULTS = {
		-- single: ["key"] = { tp = {x,y,z}, wp = {x,y,z} },
		-- multi:  ["key"] = { ["Village"] = { tp = {x,y,z}, wp = {x,y,z} } },
	}
	pcall(function()
		local function v3(t) if type(t) == "table" and tonumber(t[1]) then return Vector3.new(t[1], t[2], t[3]) end end
		for i, q in ipairs(QUESTS) do
			local d = QUEST_DEFAULTS[q.key]
			if d then
				if QST[i].villages then
					for vn, pack in pairs(d) do
						local v = QST[i].villages[vn]
						if v and type(pack) == "table" then
							v.tp = v.tp or v3(pack.tp)
							v.wp = v.wp or v3(pack.wp)
						end
					end
				else
					QST[i].tp = QST[i].tp or v3(d.tp)
					QST[i].wp = QST[i].wp or v3(d.wp)
				end
			end
		end
	end)

	local function measure(text, size, font, width)
		local plain = tostring(text or ""):gsub("<[^>]+>", "")
		local ok, v = pcall(function() return TextService:GetTextSize(plain, size, font, Vector2.new(width, 10000)) end)
		if ok and v then return math.ceil(v.Y) end
		return 40
	end
	local function mkText(parent, text, pos, size, ts, color, font)
		local l = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		l.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		l.Position = pos
		l.Size = size
		l.Text = text
		l.TextColor3 = color or DIMC
		l.Font = font or FONT_N
		l.TextSize = ts or 14
		l.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		l.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		l.Parent = parent
		return l
	end
	local function cardHead(card, text)
		local hdr = mkText(card, text, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 5), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 26), 19, CREAM, FONT_S)
		hdr.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		hdr.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		local line = Instance.new("Frame")
		line.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 1)
		line.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 33)
		line.BackgroundColor3 = SILVER
		line.BorderSizePixel = 0
		line.Parent = card
		addFadeLine(line, 0)
		return hdr
	end
	local function backButton(parent)
		local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
		b.Size = UDim2.new(0, 74, 0, 24)

		b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(31, 49, 61)
		b.Text = "‹  Back"
		b.TextColor3 = CREAM
		b.Font = FONT_S
		b.TextSize = 17
		b.AutoButtonColor = false
		b.Parent = parent
		addCorner(b, 5)
		addStroke(b, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.2)
		addHover(b, Color3.fromRGB(31, 49, 61), Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103))
		return b
	end

	local cards = {}

	task.spawn(function()
