	end)
	local function buildQuestCard(i, q)
		local orderBase = (q.cat == "One-time" and 10) or (q.cat == "Repeatable" and 100) or (q.sec == "quest" and 1000) or (q.sec == "train" and 2000) or (q.sec == "shop" and 3000) or (q.cat == "Clan Progression" and 150) or (q.cat == "Boss Access" and 200) or (q.cat == "Clan" and 300) or 400
		local card = createCard(CARD_H, orderBase + i, QuestsScroll)
		local c = { card = card, expanded = false }
		local SIMPLE = not SHOW_SETUP_UI
		local WPL = q.setup and "SET WP" or "SET MARKER"
		local NEARWP = q.setup and "WP NEAREST" or "MARKER NEAREST"

		local title = mkText(card, q.icon .. "  " .. q.name, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 8), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 24), 18, CREAM, FONT_S)
		title.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
		title.TextScaled = true
		local tsc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
		tsc.MaxTextSize = 18
		tsc.MinTextSize = 11
		tsc.Parent = title

		local chip = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		chip.Size = UDim2.new(0, 78, 0, 20)
		chip.Position = UDim2.new(1, -90, 0, 10)
		chip.BackgroundColor3 = CHIP_BG
		chip.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		chip.Text = QST[i].done and "DONE" or "OPEN"
		chip.Visible = false -- OPEN / DONE tag removed from the GUI (code kept)
		chip.TextColor3 = QST[i].done and GREEN or GREY
		chip.Font = FONT_BD
		chip.TextSize = 12
		chip.Parent = card
		mark(chip)
		c.chip = chip

		mkText(card, "📍 " .. (q.loc or "Location unknown – SET TP yourself"), UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 33), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 16), 13, DIMC, FONT_N).TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

		local startL = mkText(card, q.start or "", UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 52), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 40), 13, Color3.fromRGB(205, 215, 225), FONT_N)
		startL.TextWrapped = true
		startL.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd

		local rich = "<b>Rewards:</b> " .. (q.rewards or "—")
		if q.note then rich = rich .. "\n\n<i>" .. q.note .. "</i>" end
		if q.about then rich = q.about .. "\n\n" .. rich end
		local tH = measure(rich, 14, FONT_N, INNER_W) + 8
		local base = CARD_H + 8
		local info = mkText(card, rich, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, base), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, tH), 14, Color3.fromRGB(205, 215, 225), FONT_N)
		info.RichText = true
		info.TextWrapped = true
		c.fullH = base + tH + 14

		local row = Instance.new("Frame")
		row.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		row.BorderSizePixel = 0
		row.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28)
		row.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 112)
		row.Parent = card

		local function qBtn(text, pos, size, color, hover, scaled)
			local bb = Instance.new("TextButton")
			bb.Size = size
			bb.Position = pos
			bb.BackgroundColor3 = color
			bb.Text = text
			bb.TextColor3 = CREAM
			bb.Font = FONT_S
			bb.TextSize = 14
			bb.AutoButtonColor = false
			if scaled then
				bb.TextScaled = true
				local sc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
				sc.MaxTextSize = 14
				sc.MinTextSize = 10
				sc.Parent = bb
			end
			bb.Parent = row
			addCorner(bb, 5)
			addStroke(bb, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
			addHover(bb, color, hover)
			return bb
		end

		-- Manual buttons: single OR 4-village (Food Merchant / InnKeeper)
		c.wp = { name = q.name }

		local function doTeleport(target)
			local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
			local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
			if not root or not target then return end
			if c.tping then return end
			c.tping = true
			local params = RaycastParams.new()
			params.FilterType = Enum.RaycastFilterType.Exclude
			params.FilterDescendantsInstances = { ch }
			local done = false
			local conn
			local t0 = os.clock()
			local groundAt, lastRay
			local function finish()
				if done then return end
				done = true
				if conn then conn:Disconnect() end
				pcall(function() root.Anchored = false end)
				pcall(function() root.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero end)
				c.tping = false
			end
			pcall(function() root.CFrame = CFrame.new(target) end)
			conn = RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
				if done then return end
				local r = LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not r then finish() return end
				pcall(function()
					r.AssemblyLinearVelocity = Vector3.zero
					r.CFrame = CFrame.new(target)
				end)
				local nowRay = os.clock() -- [perf] ground check 10x/sec instead of every frame
				if lastRay and nowRay - lastRay < 0.1 then return end
				lastRay = nowRay
				local okHit, hit = pcall(function()
					return workspace:Raycast(target + Vector3.new(0, 4, 0), Vector3.new(0, -60, 0), params)
				end)
				if okHit and hit then
					groundAt = groundAt or os.clock()
					if os.clock() - groundAt > 0.25 then finish() end
				elseif os.clock() - t0 > 4 then
					finish()
				end
			end)
			task.delay(5, finish)
			task.spawn(function()
				pcall(function()
					if LocalPlayer.RequestStreamAroundAsync then LocalPlayer:RequestStreamAroundAsync(target, 1.5) end
				end)
			end)
		end

		-- Closest Chakra Point to an NPC spot: every Waypoint says TAKE <name>
		local function chakraHint(pos)
			if not (pos and Settings.nearestChakra) then return nil end
			return Settings.nearestChakra(pos)
		end

		-- Simple row: TP (left) | WAYPOINT (right). While a waypoint is on, the button says Cancel; click it to stop.
		local function buildSimpleRow(getTp, getWp)
			local sr = Instance.new("Frame")
			sr.Name = "SimpleRow"
			sr.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			sr.BorderSizePixel = 0
			sr.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28)
			sr.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 112)
			sr.ZIndex = 3
			sr.Parent = card

			local function sBtn(text, xs, xw, color, hover)
				local bb = Instance.new("TextButton")
				bb.Size = UDim2.new(xw, -2, 1, 0)
				bb.Position = UDim2.new(xs, 1, 0, 0)
				bb.BackgroundColor3 = color
				bb.Text = text
				bb.TextColor3 = CREAM
				bb.Font = FONT_S
				bb.TextSize = 14
				bb.TextScaled = true
				bb.AutoButtonColor = false
				bb.ZIndex = 3
				local sc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
				sc.MaxTextSize = 14
				sc.MinTextSize = 10
				sc.Parent = bb
				bb.Parent = sr
				addCorner(bb, 5)
				addStroke(bb, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
				addHover(bb, color, hover)
				return bb
			end
			local tpB = sBtn("Teleport", 0, 0.40, BTN, BTN_HOVER)
			local wpB = sBtn("Waypoint", 0.40, 0.60, BTN, BTN_HOVER)
			local wpObj = { name = q.name }
			-- live state: this button is "on" only while ITS waypoint is the active one, so starting any other
			-- waypoint (anywhere in the GUI) flips this one back to WAYPOINT automatically
			local function isOn()
				local SP = Settings.SP
				return SP ~= nil and SP.active ~= nil and SP.active.sp == wpObj
			end
			local function setWpOff()
				wpB.Text = "Waypoint"
			end
			wpRefreshers[#wpRefreshers + 1] = function()
				if not wpB.Parent then return end
				local t = wpB.Text
				if t == "Waypoint" or t == "Cancel" then
					local want = isOn() and "Cancel" or "Waypoint"
					if t ~= want then wpB.Text = want end
				end
			end

			tpB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local pos, vn = getTp()
				if not pos then
					tpB.Text = "Not set"
					task.delay(1.2, function() tpB.Text = "Teleport" end)
					return
				end
				doTeleport(pos)
				if vn then
					tpB.Text = "→ " .. vn
					task.delay(1.5, function() tpB.Text = "Teleport" end)
				end
			end)
			wpB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				if isOn() then
					-- second click = Cancel
					local SP = Settings.SP
					if SP and SP.clear then SP.clear() end
					setWpOff()
					return
				end
				local pos, vn = getWp()
				if not pos then
					wpB.Text = "Not set"
					task.delay(1.2, function() wpB.Text = "Waypoint" end)
					return
				end
				wpObj.x, wpObj.y, wpObj.z = pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z
				wpObj.name = vn and (q.name .. " · " .. vn) or q.name
				local cp = chakraHint(pos)
				if cp then wpObj.name = "TAKE " .. cp:upper() .. " → " .. wpObj.name end
				local SP = Settings.SP
				if SP and SP.start then SP.start(wpObj) end -- SP.start clears whatever waypoint was active before
				if cp then
					wpB.Text = "TAKE " .. cp:upper()
					task.delay(5, function() wpB.Text = isOn() and "Cancel" or "Waypoint" end)
				else
					wpB.Text = "Cancel"
				end
			end)
		end

		-- Zetsu / Unstable Rift row: TP (nearest) | WAYPOINT (nearest) | SPOTS dropdown (pick a location by hand).
		-- autoAll = true (Unstable Rift): WAYPOINT guides you to the closest saved rift, then the next closest, until all are visited.
		-- The WAYPOINT button text always stays "Waypoint"; click it again to stop, click again to start.
		local function buildLocRow(VILLS, autoAll)
			local sr = Instance.new("Frame")
			sr.Name = "LocRow"
			sr.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			sr.BorderSizePixel = 0
			sr.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28)
			sr.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 112)
			sr.ZIndex = 3
			sr.Parent = card

			local function sBtn(parent, text, xs, xw, color, hover, ts)
				local bb = Instance.new("TextButton")
				bb.Size = UDim2.new(xw, -2, 1, 0)
				bb.Position = UDim2.new(xs, 1, 0, 0)
				bb.BackgroundColor3 = color
				bb.Text = text
				bb.TextColor3 = CREAM
				bb.Font = FONT_S
				bb.TextSize = ts or 14
				bb.TextScaled = true
				bb.AutoButtonColor = false
				bb.ZIndex = 3
				local sc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
				sc.MaxTextSize = ts or 14
				sc.MinTextSize = 9
				sc.Parent = bb
				bb.Parent = parent
				addCorner(bb, 5)
				addStroke(bb, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
				addHover(bb, color, hover)
				return bb
			end
			local tpB = sBtn(sr, "Teleport", 0, 0.40, BTN, BTN_HOVER)
			local wpB = sBtn(sr, "Waypoint", 0.40, 0.44, BTN, BTN_HOVER)
			local ddB = sBtn(sr, "▼", 0.84, 0.16, BTN, BTN_HOVER)

			local function spotPos(vn, kind)
				local v = QST[i].villages[vn]
				if not v then return nil end
				if kind == "wp" then return v.wp or v.tp end
				return v.tp or v.wp
			end
			local function nearestFrom(kind, visited)
				local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
				local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not root then return nil end
				local bestPos, bestName, bestD
				for _, vn in ipairs(VILLS) do
					if not (visited and visited[vn]) then
						local p = spotPos(vn, kind)
						if p then
							local d = (p - root.Position).Magnitude
							if not bestD or d < bestD then bestD, bestPos, bestName = d, p, vn end
						end
					end
				end
				return bestPos, bestName
			end
			local function flash(btn, txt, back)
				btn.Text = txt
				task.delay(1.3, function() btn.Text = back end)
			end

			local wpObj = { name = q.name }
			local running = false
			local runId = 0
			local function mineActive()
				local SP = Settings.SP
				return SP and SP.active ~= nil and SP.active.sp == wpObj
			end
			local function stopAll()
				runId = runId + 1
				running = false
				local SP = Settings.SP
				if SP and SP.clear and mineActive() then SP.clear() end
			end

			tpB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local pos, vn = nearestFrom("tp")
				if not pos then flash(tpB, "Not set", "Teleport") return end
				doTeleport(pos)
				flash(tpB, "→ " .. vn, "Teleport")
			end)

			wpB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local SP = Settings.SP
				if not (SP and SP.start) then return end
				if running or mineActive() then
					stopAll()
					return
				end
				local pos, vn = nearestFrom("wp")
				if not pos then flash(wpB, "Not set", "Waypoint") return end
				if not autoAll then
					wpObj.x, wpObj.y, wpObj.z = pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z
					wpObj.name = q.name .. " · " .. vn
					local cp = chakraHint(pos)
					if cp then
						wpObj.name = "TAKE " .. cp:upper() .. " → " .. wpObj.name
						flash(wpB, "TAKE " .. cp:upper(), "Waypoint")
					end
					SP.start(wpObj)
					return
				end
				-- Unstable Rift: guide to the closest, then the next closest, until every saved rift is visited
				running = true
				runId = runId + 1
				local myId = runId
				local visited = {}
				local curPos, curName
				local function startNext()
					local p, n = nearestFrom("wp", visited)
					if not p then return false end
					curPos, curName = p, n
					wpObj.x, wpObj.y, wpObj.z = p.X, p.Y, p.Z
					wpObj.name = q.name .. " · " .. n
					local rcp = chakraHint(p)
					if rcp then
						wpObj.name = "TAKE " .. rcp:upper() .. " → " .. wpObj.name
						flash(wpB, "TAKE " .. rcp:upper(), "Waypoint")
					end
					SP.start(wpObj)
					return true
				end
				if not startNext() then running = false return end
				task.spawn(function()
					local allDone = false
					while runId == myId do
						task.wait(0.4)
						if runId ~= myId then break end
						local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
						local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
						local act = SP.active
						local stillMine = act ~= nil and act.sp == wpObj
						local arrived = root ~= nil and curPos ~= nil and (root.Position - curPos).Magnitude <= (stillMine and 12 or 16)
						if arrived then
							visited[curName] = true
							if not startNext() then
								allDone = true
								break
							end
						elseif not stillMine then
							break -- waypoint was cleared / replaced by something else
						end
					end
					if runId == myId then
						running = false
						if mineActive() and SP.clear then SP.clear() end
						if allDone then flash(wpB, "ALL DONE", "Waypoint") end
					end
				end)
			end)

			-- ===== dropdown: names of every location, each with its own TP + WP =====
			local ENTRY_H, ENTRY_GAP = 28, 4
			local LIST_Y = CARD_H - 2
			local listH = #VILLS * (ENTRY_H + ENTRY_GAP) + 4
			local dropOpen = false
			local list = Instance.new("Frame")
			list.Name = "LocList"
			list.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			list.BorderSizePixel = 0
			list.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, listH)
			list.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, LIST_Y)
			list.ZIndex = 3
			list.Visible = false
			list.Parent = card

			local locObjs = {}
			for vi, vn in ipairs(VILLS) do
				local y = (vi - 1) * (ENTRY_H + ENTRY_GAP)
				local set = spotPos(vn, "tp") ~= nil
				local lab = mkText(list, vn, UDim2.new(0, 2, 0, y), UDim2.new(0.46, -4, 0, ENTRY_H), 14, set and CREAM or GREY, FONT_S)
				lab.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
				lab.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
				lab.ZIndex = 3
				local ef = Instance.new("Frame")
				ef.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				ef.BorderSizePixel = 0
				ef.Size = UDim2.new(0.54, 0, 0, ENTRY_H)
				ef.Position = UDim2.new(0.46, 0, 0, y)
				ef.ZIndex = 3
				ef.Parent = list
				local eTp = sBtn(ef, "Teleport", 0, 0.45, BTN, BTN_HOVER, 13)
				local eWp = sBtn(ef, "WP", 0.45, 0.55, BTN, BTN_HOVER, 13)
				local eObj = { name = q.name .. " · " .. vn }
				locObjs[vn] = eObj
				eTp.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					local p = spotPos(vn, "tp")
					if not p then flash(eTp, "Not set", "Teleport") return end
					doTeleport(p)
				end)
				eWp.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					local SP = Settings.SP
					if not (SP and SP.start) then return end
					if SP.active and SP.active.sp == eObj then
						SP.clear()
						return
					end
					local p = spotPos(vn, "wp")
					if not p then flash(eWp, "Not set", "WP") return end
					runId = runId + 1 -- a manual pick stops the auto-run
					running = false
					eObj.x, eObj.y, eObj.z = p.X, p.Y, p.Z
					local cp = chakraHint(p)
					eObj.name = (cp and ("TAKE " .. cp:upper() .. " → ") or "") .. q.name .. " · " .. vn
					if cp then flash(eWp, "TAKE " .. cp:upper(), "WP") end
					SP.start(eObj)
				end)
			end

			c.relayout = function()
				local dd = dropOpen and listH or 0
				info.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, base + dd)
				tween(card, 0.28, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, (c.expanded and c.fullH or CARD_H) + dd) })
			end
			ddB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				dropOpen = not dropOpen
				list.Visible = dropOpen
				ddB.Text = dropOpen and "▲" or "▼"
				c.relayout()
			end)
		end

		local function addExpandHit()
			local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
			hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, CARD_H - 36)
			hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			hit.Text = ""
			hit.AutoButtonColor = false
			hit.ZIndex = 2
			hit.Parent = card
			hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				c.expanded = not c.expanded
				if c.relayout then
					c.relayout()
				else
					tween(card, 0.28, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, c.expanded and c.fullH or CARD_H) })
				end
			end)
		end

		local isMulti = MULTI_KEYS[q.key] == true and QST[i].villages ~= nil

		if isMulti then
			-- Tall card: per-NPC village list × (label + 4 buttons)
			local VILLS = QST[i].villageOrder or MULTI_LISTS[q.key] or {"Sorythia", "Durana", "Rain", "Snow"}
			local rowH = 52
			local topY = 148
			if not SIMPLE then
				if q.setup then
					local belowY = topY + #VILLS * rowH + 6
					info.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, belowY)
					card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, belowY + tH + 14)
					c.fullH = belowY + tH + 14
				else
					card.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, topY + #VILLS * rowH + 10)
					c.fullH = topY + #VILLS * rowH + 10 + 40
				end
			end

			-- hide the single-row parent frame buttons by not using `row` for single
			row.Visible = false

			-- ===== NEAREST row: TP / MARKER to whichever saved spot is closest to you =====
			local function nearestSpot(kind)
				local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
				local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not root then return nil end
				local bestPos, bestName, bestD
				for _, vn2 in ipairs(VILLS) do
					local v = QST[i].villages[vn2]
					local p
					if kind == "wp" then p = v.wp or v.tp else p = v.tp or v.wp end
					if p then
						local d = (p - root.Position).Magnitude
						if not bestD or d < bestD then bestD, bestPos, bestName = d, p, vn2 end
					end
				end
				return bestPos, bestName
			end

			local nearRow = Instance.new("Frame")
			nearRow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			nearRow.BorderSizePixel = 0
			nearRow.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28)
			nearRow.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 112)
			nearRow.Parent = card
			nearRow.Visible = not SIMPLE

			local function nBtn(text, xScale, xSize)
				local bb = Instance.new("TextButton")
				bb.Size = UDim2.new(xSize, -2, 1, 0)
				bb.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, 1, 0, 0)
				bb.BackgroundColor3 = BTN
				bb.Text = text
				bb.TextColor3 = CREAM
				bb.Font = FONT_S
				bb.TextSize = 13
				bb.TextScaled = true
				bb.AutoButtonColor = false
				local sc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
				sc.MaxTextSize = 13
				sc.MinTextSize = 9
				sc.Parent = bb
				bb.Parent = nearRow
				addCorner(bb, 5)
				addStroke(bb, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
				addHover(bb, BTN, BTN_HOVER)
				return bb
			end
			local nearTpB = nBtn("TP NEAREST", 0, 0.5)
			local nearWpB = nBtn(NEARWP, 0.5, 0.5)
			local nearObj = { name = q.name .. " · nearest" }

			nearTpB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local pos, vn2 = nearestSpot("tp")
				if not pos then
					nearTpB.Text = "Set a TP first"
					task.delay(1.2, function() nearTpB.Text = "TP NEAREST" end)
					return
				end
				doTeleport(pos)
				nearTpB.Text = "→ " .. vn2
				task.delay(1.5, function() nearTpB.Text = "TP NEAREST" end)
			end)
			nearWpB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local pos, vn2 = nearestSpot("wp")
				if not pos then
					nearWpB.Text = "Set a spot first"
					task.delay(1.2, function() nearWpB.Text = NEARWP end)
					return
				end
				nearObj.x, nearObj.y, nearObj.z = pos.X, pos.Y, pos.Z
				nearObj.name = q.name .. " · " .. vn2
				local SP = Settings.SP
				if SP and SP.start then SP.start(nearObj) end
				nearWpB.Text = "Marker → " .. vn2
				task.delay(1.5, function() nearWpB.Text = NEARWP end)
			end)

			local wpObjs = {}
			for vi, vn in ipairs(VILLS) do
				local y = topY + (vi - 1) * rowH
				local lab = mkText(card, vn, UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y), UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 16), 13, CREAM, FONT_S)
				lab.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Center
				lab.Visible = not SIMPLE

				local rframe = Instance.new("Frame")
				rframe.BackgroundTransparency = 1
				rframe.BorderSizePixel = 0
				rframe.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28)
				rframe.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, y + 18)
				rframe.Parent = card
				rframe.Visible = not SIMPLE

				local function mBtn(text, xScale, xSize, color, hover)
					local bb = Instance.new("TextButton")
					bb.Size = UDim2.new(xSize, -2, 1, 0)
					bb.Position = UDim2.new(xScale, 1, 0, 0)
					bb.BackgroundColor3 = color
					bb.Text = text
					bb.TextColor3 = CREAM
					bb.Font = FONT_S
					bb.TextSize = 12
					bb.TextScaled = true
					bb.AutoButtonColor = false
					local sc = Instance.new("UITextSizeConstraint")
					sc.MaxTextSize = 12
					sc.MinTextSize = 9
					sc.Parent = bb
					bb.Parent = rframe
					addCorner(bb, 5)
					addStroke(bb, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.3)
					addHover(bb, color, hover)
					return bb
				end

				local setTpB = mBtn("SET TP", 0, 0.24, BTN, BTN_HOVER)
				local tpB = mBtn("TELEPORT", 0.24, 0.26, BTN, BTN_HOVER)
				local setWpB = mBtn(WPL, 0.50, 0.28, BTN, BTN_HOVER)
				local clrB = mBtn("CLEAR", 0.78, 0.22, BTN_RED, BTN_RED_HOVER)

				local wpObj = { name = q.name .. " · " .. vn }
				wpObjs[vn] = wpObj

				local function refreshRow()
					local v = QST[i].villages[vn]
					setTpB.Text = v.tp and "SET TP ✓" or "SET TP"
					tpB.TextTransparency = v.tp and 0 or 0.45
					setWpB.Text = v.wp and (WPL .. " ✓") or WPL
				end

				setTpB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
					local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if not root then return end
					QST[i].villages[vn].tp = root.Position
					saveQuests()
					setTpB.Text = "Saved!"
					task.delay(1, refreshRow)
				end)
				tpB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					local v = QST[i].villages[vn]
					if not v.tp then
						tpB.Text = "Set first"
						task.delay(1.2, refreshRow)
						return
					end
					doTeleport(v.tp)
				end)
				setWpB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
					local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
					if not root then return end
					local v = QST[i].villages[vn]
					v.wp = root.Position
					saveQuests()
					wpObj.x, wpObj.y, wpObj.z = v.wp.X, v.wp.Y, v.wp.Z
					local SP = Settings.SP
					if SP and SP.start then SP.start(wpObj) end
					setWpB.Text = "Marker On"
					task.delay(1, refreshRow)
				end)
				clrB.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
					local SP = Settings.SP
					if SP and SP.clear then SP.clear() end
					QST[i].villages[vn].wp = nil
					saveQuests()
					refreshRow()
				end)
				refreshRow()
			end

			-- no expand hit covering buttons
			if SIMPLE then
				buildLocRow(VILLS, q.key == "cp_unstablerift")
				addExpandHit()
			elseif not q.setup then
				c.fullH = topY + #VILLS * rowH + 10
			end
		else
			-- Single NPC: SET TP | TELEPORT | SET MARKER | CLEAR
			local setTpBtn = qBtn("SET TP", UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.24, -2, 0, 28), BTN, BTN_HOVER, true)
			local tpBtn = qBtn("TELEPORT", UDim2.new(0.24, 1, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.26, -2, 0, 28), BTN, BTN_HOVER, true)
			local setWpBtn = qBtn(WPL, UDim2.new(0.50, 1, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.28, -2, 0, 28), BTN, BTN_HOVER, true)
			local clrWpBtn = qBtn("CLEAR", UDim2.new(0.78, 1, 0, 0), UDim2.new(0.22, -2, 0, 28), BTN_RED, BTN_RED_HOVER, true)

			local function refreshBtns()
				local st = QST[i]
				setTpBtn.Text = st.tp and "SET TP ✓" or "SET TP"
				tpBtn.TextTransparency = st.tp and 0 or 0.45
				setWpBtn.Text = st.wp and (WPL .. " ✓") or WPL
			end
			c.refreshBtns = refreshBtns

			setTpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
				local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not root then return end
				QST[i].tp = root.Position
				saveQuests()
				setTpBtn.Text = "Saved!"
				task.delay(1, refreshBtns)
			end)
			tpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local st = QST[i]
				if not st.tp then
					tpBtn.Text = "Set TP first"
					task.delay(1.2, refreshBtns)
					return
				end
				doTeleport(st.tp)
			end)
			setWpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local ch = LocalPlayer.Character
				local root = ch and ch:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
				if not root then return end
				local st = QST[i]
				st.wp = root.Position
				saveQuests()
				c.wp.x, c.wp.y, c.wp.z = st.wp.X, st.wp.Y, st.wp.Z
				local SP = Settings.SP
				if SP and SP.start then SP.start(c.wp) end
				setWpBtn.Text = "Marker On"
				task.delay(1, refreshBtns)
			end)
			clrWpBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				local SP = Settings.SP
				if SP and SP.clear then SP.clear() end
				QST[i].wp = nil
				saveQuests()
				refreshBtns()
			end)

			local hit = Instance.new("TextButton")
			hit.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, CARD_H - 36)
			hit.BackgroundTransparency = 1
			hit.Text = ""
			hit.AutoButtonColor = false
			hit.ZIndex = 2
			hit.Parent = card
			hit.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
				c.expanded = not c.expanded
				tween(card, 0.28, { Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, c.expanded and c.fullH or CARD_H) })
			end)
			refreshBtns()
			if SIMPLE then
				row.Visible = false
				buildSimpleRow(function() return QST[i].tp or QST[i].wp end, function() return QST[i].wp or QST[i].tp end)
			end
		end

		cards[i] = c
	end

	sectionHeader("⭐  One Time Quests", 5)
	sectionHeader("🔁  Repeatable Quests", 90)
	sectionHeader("🧬  Clan Progression NPC", 140)
	sectionHeader("📜  Quest NPCs", 999)
	sectionHeader("🎓  Trainers and progression", 1999)
	sectionHeader("🛒  Shops and services", 2999)
	-- Boss Access / Clan / Core Village sections are removed from the GUI for now (their data stays in QUESTS above)
	local HIDDEN_QUEST_CATS = { ["Boss Access"] = true, ["Clan"] = true, ["Core"] = true }

	-- LAZY: build quest cards in background / on first open
	local questsBuilt = false
	local function ensureQuestCards()
		if questsBuilt then return end
		questsBuilt = true
		task.spawn(function()
			for i, q in ipairs(QUESTS) do
				if not HIDDEN_QUEST_CATS[q.cat] then
					pcall(buildQuestCard, i, q)
				end
				if i % 3 == 0 then task.wait() end
			end
		end)
	end
	Settings._ensureQuestCards = ensureQuestCards
	task.defer(function()
		task.wait(0.8)
		ensureQuestCards()
	end)

	-- ===== SEARCH BAR (pinned above the list, stays put while you scroll) =====
	-- Type keywords (NPC name, location, reward, quest words...) and the list scrolls to the best match.
	-- Press Enter to jump to the next match.
	do
		local sbar = Instance.new("Frame")
		sbar.Name = "QuestSearchBar"
		sbar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0, 28)
		sbar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0, 106)
		sbar.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(31, 49, 61)
		sbar.BorderSizePixel = 0
		sbar.ZIndex = 5
		sbar.Parent = QuestsPage
		addCorner(sbar, 14)
		local sst = addStroke(sbar, Color3.fromRGB(180, 190, 200), 1, 0.55)
		sst.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border

		local mag = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		mag.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 1, 0)
		mag.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
		mag.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		mag.Text = "🔍"
		mag.TextSize = 14
		mag.ZIndex = 6
		mag.Parent = sbar

		local sbox = Instance.new("TextBox")
		sbox.Name = "QuestSearchBox"
		sbox.Size = UDim2.new(1, -110, 1, 0)
		sbox.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 0)
		sbox.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		sbox.BorderSizePixel = 0
		sbox.Text = ""
		sbox.PlaceholderText = "Search NPCs / quests..."
		sbox.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 175, 185)
		sbox.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
		sbox.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		sbox.TextSize = 15
		sbox.ClearTextOnFocus = false
		sbox.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		sbox.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		sbox.ZIndex = 6
		sbox.Parent = sbar

		local cnt = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		cnt.Size = UDim2.new(0, 46, 1, 0)
		cnt.Position = UDim2.new(1, -74, 0, 0)
		cnt.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		cnt.Text = ""
		cnt.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 175, 185)
		cnt.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		cnt.TextSize = 13
		cnt.ZIndex = 6
		cnt.Parent = sbar

		local sclear = Instance.new("TextButton")
		sclear.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
		sclear.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0.5, -11)
		sclear.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		sclear.Text = "✕"
		sclear.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 175, 185)
		sclear.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
		sclear.TextSize = 14
		sclear.AutoButtonColor = false
		sclear.Visible = false
		sclear.ZIndex = 6
		sclear.Parent = sbar

		local function plain(t)
			return (tostring(t or ""):gsub("<[^>]+>", ""):lower())
		end
		local haystacks = {}
		for i, q in ipairs(QUESTS) do
			if cards[i] then
				haystacks[i] = {
					name = plain(q.name),
					rest = plain(table.concat({ q.key or "", q.loc or "", q.start or "", q.about or "", q.rewards or "", q.note or "", q.cat or "" }, " ")),
					order = cards[i].card.LayoutOrder,
				}
			end
		end

		local matches, mIdx = {}, 0
		local function runSearch(text)
			matches, mIdx = {}, 0
			local words = {}
			for w in plain(text):gmatch("%S+") do words[#words + 1] = w end
			if #words == 0 then return end
			for i, h in pairs(haystacks) do
				local score, ok = 0, true
				for _, w in ipairs(words) do
					if h.name:find(w, 1, true) then
						score = score + 10
						if h.name:sub(1, #w) == w then score = score + 3 end
					elseif h.rest:find(w, 1, true) then
						score = score + 1
					else
						ok = false
						break
					end
				end
				if ok then matches[#matches + 1] = { i = i, score = score, order = h.order } end
			end
			table.sort(matches, function(a, b)
				if a.score ~= b.score then return a.score > b.score end
				return a.order < b.order
			end)
		end

		local function jump(idx)
			local m = matches[idx]
			if not m then return end
			mIdx = idx
			cnt.Text = idx .. "/" .. #matches
			local card = cards[m.i].card
			local info = TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			local target = QuestsScroll.CanvasPosition.Y + (card.AbsolutePosition.Y - QuestsScroll.AbsolutePosition.Y) - 4
			TweenService:Create(QuestsScroll, info, { CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, target)) }):Play()
			local stroke = card:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if stroke then
				local t0, a0 = stroke.Thickness, stroke.Transparency
				tween(stroke, 0.2, { Thickness = 3, Transparency = 0 })
				task.delay(1.4, function()
					if stroke.Parent then tween(stroke, 0.45, { Thickness = t0, Transparency = a0 }) end
				end)
			end
		end

		local ver = 0
		sbox:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(function()
			sclear.Visible = sbox.Text ~= ""
			ver = ver + 1
			local my = ver
			task.delay(0.3, function()
				if my ~= ver then return end
				runSearch(sbox.Text)
				if sbox.Text == "" then
					cnt.Text = ""
				elseif #matches == 0 then
					cnt.Text = "none"
				else
					jump(1)
				end
			end)
		end)
		sbox.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
			if enter and #matches > 0 then
				jump(mIdx % #matches + 1)
			end
		end)
		sclear.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			sbox.Text = ""
			cnt.Text = ""
		end)
	end

	-- EXPORT COORDS: prints every saved TP / marker as a Lua table (and copies it) so it can be baked in
	local exportBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	exportBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 112, 0, 24)
	exportBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -128, 0, 14)
	exportBtn.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(31, 49, 61)
	exportBtn.Text = "EXPORT COORDS"
	exportBtn.TextColor3 = CREAM
	exportBtn.Font = FONT_S
	exportBtn.TextSize = 14
	exportBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	exportBtn.Parent = QuestsPage
	exportBtn.Visible = false
	addCorner(exportBtn, 5)
	addStroke(exportBtn, SILVER_DIM, 1, 0.2)
	addHover(exportBtn, Color3.fromRGB(31, 49, 61), Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103))
	exportBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		local function v3(v) return string.format("{%.2f, %.2f, %.2f}", v.X, v.Y, v.Z) end
		local function pk(tp, wp)
			local p = {}
			if tp then p[#p + 1] = "tp = " .. v3(tp) end
			if wp then p[#p + 1] = "wp = " .. v3(wp) end
			return "{ " .. table.concat(p, ", ") .. " }"
		end
		local out = { "local QUEST_DEFAULTS = {" }
		for i, q in ipairs(QUESTS) do
			local st = QST[i]
			if st.villages then
				local lines = {}
				for _, vn in ipairs(st.villageOrder or {}) do
					local v = st.villages[vn]
					if v and (v.tp or v.wp) then
						lines[#lines + 1] = string.format("\t\t[%q] = %s,", vn, pk(v.tp, v.wp))
					end
				end
				if #lines > 0 then
					out[#out + 1] = string.format("\t[%q] = {", q.key)
					for _, l in ipairs(lines) do out[#out + 1] = l end
					out[#out + 1] = "\t},"
				end
			elseif st.tp or st.wp then
				out[#out + 1] = string.format("\t[%q] = %s,", q.key, pk(st.tp, st.wp))
			end
		end
		out[#out + 1] = "}"
		local text = table.concat(out, "\n")
		print("[DannyGui] ===== SAVED QUEST COORDS =====\n" .. text)
		pcall(function() if setclipboard then setclipboard(text) end end)
		exportBtn.Text = #out > 2 and "COPIED + PRINTED" or "NOTHING SET"
		task.delay(1.5, function() exportBtn.Text = "EXPORT COORDS" end)
	end)

	-- HOME CARD under Bosses in Features
	task.defer(function()
		task.wait(0.5)
		local feat = HomePage:FindFirstChild("HomeFeatures")
		local parent = feat or HomePage
		local y = 558
		if feat then
			local bossesY
			for _, ch in ipairs(feat:GetChildren()) do
				if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.Size.Y.Offset == 60 then
					for _, d in ipairs(ch:GetDescendants()) do
						if d:IsA("TextLabel") and d.Text == "Bosses" then
							bossesY = ch.Position.Y.Offset
							break
						end
					end
					if bossesY then break end
				end
			end
			if bossesY then
				y = bossesY + 66
				for _, ch in ipairs(feat:GetChildren()) do
					if ch:IsA("GuiObject") and ch.Position.Y.Offset >= y then
						ch.Position = UDim2.new(ch.Position.X.Scale, ch.Position.X.Offset, 0, ch.Position.Y.Offset + 66)
					end
				end
				feat.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, feat.CanvasSize.Y.Offset + 66)
			else
				y = math.max(feat.CanvasSize.Y.Offset, 0) + 6
			end
		end
		local card = createCard(60, 8, parent, true)
		card.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, y)
		card.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 60)
		local stroke = card:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")

		local slot = Instance.new("Frame")
		slot.Size = UDim2.new(0, 50, 0, 50)
		slot.Position = UDim2.new(0, 14, 0.5, -25)
		slot.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(18, 30, 40)
		slot.BorderSizePixel = 0
		slot.Parent = card
		addCorner(slot, 6)
		mark(addStroke(slot, WHITE, 1.2, 0.5))
		local icon = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		icon.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		icon.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		icon.Text = "📜"
		icon.TextSize = 26
		icon.Parent = slot

		local t = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		t.Size = UDim2.new(1, -120, 0, 24)
		t.Position = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 7)
		t.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		t.Text = "NPC/Quests"
		t.TextColor3 = CREAM
		t.Font = FONT_S
		t.TextSize = 19
		t.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		t.Parent = card

		local sub = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		sub.Size = UDim2.new(1, -122, 0, 28)
		sub.Position = UDim2.new(0, 76, 0, 29)
		sub.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		sub.Text = "Shows You Every NPC And Their Quest"
		sub.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
		sub.Font = FONT_N
		sub.TextSize = 14
		sub.TextWrapped = true
		sub.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		sub.TextYAlignment = Enum.TextYAlignment.Top
		sub.Parent = card

		local arrow = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		arrow.Size = UDim2.new(0, 24, 1, 0)
		arrow.Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 0)
		arrow.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		arrow.Text = "›"
		arrow.TextColor3 = CREAM
		arrow.Font = FONT_BD
		arrow.TextSize = 34
		arrow.Parent = card

		local btn = Instance.new("TextButton")
		btn.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
		btn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		btn.Text = ""
		btn.AutoButtonColor = false
		btn.Parent = card
		btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function()
			tween(stroke, 0.2, { Thickness = 2, Transparency = 0.12 })
			tween(arrow, 0.2, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -29, 0, 0) })
		end)
		btn.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
			tween(stroke, 0.2, { Thickness = 1.2, Transparency = 0.55 })
			tween(arrow, 0.2, { Position = UDim2.new(1, -34, 0, 0) })
		end)
		btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function() showPage("npcquests") end)

		if feat then feat.CanvasSize = UDim2.new(0, 0, 0, math.max(feat.CanvasSize.Y.Offset, y + 60 + 10)) end
		-- this card is built after the GUI's startup theme pass, so run the pass again: it picks up the
		-- saved color, transparency and the rest of GUI SETTINGS exactly like the other home boxes
		pcall(refreshGuiLook)
	end)

	local prevStop = Settings.DX.stop
	Settings.DX.stop = function()
		runningQ = false
		if prevStop then pcall(prevStop) end
	end
end, function(e) warn("[DannyGui] npc quests error: " .. tostring(e)) end)

	local PAGE_TAGS = {farm = "Farm", qol = "QOL", teleportspectate = "View", teleports = "Teleports", guisettings = "GUI", bosses = "Bosses", npcquests = "Quests"}

		{"Two-Tailed Matatabi", "🔥", "bosses", "Two-Tailed Matatabi", "matatabi boss tailed beast alive dead respawn tracker"},
		{"Three-Tailed Isobu", "🐢", "bosses", "Three-Tailed Isobu", "isobu boss tailed beast alive dead respawn tracker"},
	}

	local MAX_ROWS, ROW_H = 10, 30
	local BAR_Y, BAR_H = 101, 28

	-- ---------- finding the page / card to jump to ----------
	local function scrollFor(page)
		if page == "farm" then return Content end
		if page == "qol" then return QOLContent end
		if page == "guisettings" then return GUISettingsPage:FindFirstChild("GuiSettingsScroll", true) end
		if page == "teleports" then local tpg = Pages:FindFirstChild("TeleportsPage"); return tpg and tpg:FindFirstChild("TeleportsScroll") end
		if page == "npcquests" then local qpg = Pages:FindFirstChild("NpcQuestsPage"); return qpg and qpg:FindFirstChild("QuestsScroll") end
		if page == "bosses" then local bpg = Pages:FindFirstChild("BossesPage"); return bpg and bpg:FindFirstChild("BossesScroll") end
		if page == "teleportspectate" then
			for _, c in ipairs(TeleportSpectatePage:GetChildren()) do
				if c:IsA("ScrollingFrame") then return c end
			end
		end
		return nil
	end

	local function clean(t)
		t = tostring(t or "")
		t = t:gsub("<[^>]+>", "")
		t = t:gsub("^[^%w%(]+", "")
		t = t:gsub("%s+$", "")
		return t:lower()
	end

	local function findCard(scroll, needle)
		if not scroll or not needle then return nil end
		local want = clean(needle)
		local partial = nil
		for _, card in ipairs(scroll:GetChildren()) do
			if card:IsA("GuiObject") then
				for _, d in ipairs(card:GetDescendants()) do
					if d:IsA("TextLabel") or d:IsA("TextButton") then
						local c = clean(d.Text)
						if c == want then return card end
						if not partial and c ~= "" and c:find(want, 1, true) then partial = card end
					end
				end
			end
		end
		return partial
	end

	local function goTo(e)
		showPage(e[3])
		if e[7] then
			-- a teleport spot / waypoint: open Teleports, scroll to it and flash it
			task.spawn(function()
				task.wait(0.2)
				if Settings.DX.jumpSpot then pcall(Settings.DX.jumpSpot, e[7]) end
			end)
			return
		end
		task.spawn(function()
			task.wait(0.15)
			local scroll = scrollFor(e[3])
			if not scroll then return end
			local info = TweenInfo.new(0.4, Enum.EasingStyle.Quart, Enum.EasingDirection.Out)
			local card = findCard(scroll, e[4])
			if not card then
				TweenService:Create(scroll, info, {CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, 0)}):Play()
				return
			end
			local target = scroll.CanvasPosition.Y + (card.AbsolutePosition.Y - scroll.AbsolutePosition.Y) - 4
			TweenService:Create(scroll, info, {CanvasPosition = Vector2.new(0, math.max(0, target))}):Play()
			-- brief glow so you can see where you landed
			local stroke = card:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke")
			if stroke then
				local t0, a0 = stroke.Thickness, stroke.Transparency
				tween(stroke, 0.2, {Thickness = 3, Transparency = 0})
				task.delay(1.2, function()
					if stroke.Parent then tween(stroke, 0.45, {Thickness = t0, Transparency = a0}) end
				end)
			end
		end)
	end

	-- ---------- the bar ----------
	local bar = Instance.new("Frame")
	bar.Name = "HomeSearchBar"
	bar.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, BAR_H)
	bar.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, BAR_Y)
	bar.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
	bar.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
	bar.BorderSizePixel = 0
	bar.ZIndex = 2
	bar.Parent = HomePage
	addCorner(bar, 14)
	addGradient(bar, Color3.fromRGB(35, 57, 70), Color3.fromRGB(24, 32, 42), 45)
	local barStroke = addStroke(bar, Color3.fromRGB(180, 190, 200), 1, 0.55)
	barStroke.ApplyStrokeMode = Enum.ApplyStrokeMode.Border
	barStroke:SetAttribute("KeepColor", true)

	local magnifier = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	magnifier.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 1, 0)
	magnifier.Position = UDim2.new(0, 8, 0, 0)
	magnifier.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	magnifier.Text = "🔍"
	magnifier.TextSize = 14
	magnifier.ZIndex = 3
	magnifier.Parent = bar

	local box = Instance.new("TextBox")
	box.Name = "SearchBox"
	box.Size = UDim2.new(1, -66, 1, 0)
	box.Position = UDim2.new(0, 34, 0, 0)
	box.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	box.BorderSizePixel = 0
	box.Text = ""
	box.PlaceholderText = "Search features..."
	box.PlaceholderColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 175, 185)
	box.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
	box.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	box.TextSize = 15
	box.ClearTextOnFocus = false
	box.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
	box.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
	box.ZIndex = 3
	box.Parent = bar

	-- placeholder follows whatever text color the GUI Settings theme gives the box
	local function syncPlaceholder()
		box.PlaceholderColor3 = box.TextColor3:Lerp(Color3.fromRGB(120, 130, 140), 0.5)
	end
	box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("TextColor3"):Connect(syncPlaceholder)
	syncPlaceholder()

	local clearBtn = Instance.new("TextButton")
	clearBtn.Size = UDim2.new(0, 22, 0, 22)
	clearBtn.Position = UDim2.new(1, -28, 0.5, -11)
	clearBtn.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	clearBtn.Text = "✕"
	clearBtn.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(165, 175, 185)
	clearBtn.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
	clearBtn.TextSize = 14
	clearBtn.AutoButtonColor = false
	clearBtn.Visible = false
	clearBtn.ZIndex = 3
	clearBtn.Parent = bar

	-- ---------- results dropdown ----------
	local drop = Instance.new("Frame")
	drop.Name = "HomeSearchResults"
	drop.Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, 38)
	drop.Position = UDim2.new(0, 16, 0, BAR_Y + BAR_H + 4)
	drop.BackgroundColor3 = WHITE
	drop.BackgroundTransparency = 0.1
	drop.BorderSizePixel = 0
	drop.Visible = false
	drop.Active = true
	drop.ClipsDescendants = true
	drop.ZIndex = 40
	drop.Parent = HomePage
	addCorner(drop, 10)
	-- exact same base as the other cards, so color / saturation / brightness / transparency
	-- from GUI Settings change it exactly like them
	addGradient(drop, Color3.fromRGB(35, 57, 70), Color3.fromRGB(24, 32, 42), 45)
	mark(addStroke(drop, WHITE, 1.2, 0.55))
	local function accent() return FeaturesLabel.TextColor3 end -- themed accent text color

	-- show/hide the list; when the GUI is very transparent, also tuck the home cards away
	-- underneath so the results stay readable
	local function setDropVisible(v)
		drop.Visible = v
		local hide = v and drop.BackgroundTransparency > 0.3
		for _, c in ipairs(HomePage:GetChildren()) do
			if c == FeaturesLabel or c.Name == "FeaturesLine" or c.Name == "HomeFeatures" then
				c.Visible = not hide
			end
		end
	end

	local dropPad = Instance.new("UIPadding")
	dropPad.PaddingTop = UDim.new(0, 4)
	dropPad.PaddingBottom = UDim.new(0, 4)
	dropPad.PaddingLeft = UDim.new(0, 4)
	dropPad.PaddingRight = UDim.new(0, 4)
	dropPad.Parent = drop

	local dropList = Instance.new("UIListLayout")
	dropList.Padding = UDim.new(0, 2)
	dropList.SortOrder = Enum.SortOrder.LayoutOrder
	dropList.Parent = drop

	local empty = Instance.new("TextLabel")
	empty.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, 30)
	empty.BackgroundTransparency = 1
	empty.Text = "No matches"
	empty.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
	empty.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
	empty.TextSize = 15
	empty.Visible = false
	empty.ZIndex = 41
	empty.Parent = drop

	local rows = {}
	local results = {}
	local sel = 0

	local function setSel(i)
		sel = i
		for j, r in ipairs(rows) do
			tween(r.btn, 0.1, {BackgroundTransparency = (j == i and r.btn.Visible) and 0.45 or 1})
		end
	end

	for i = 1, MAX_ROWS do
		local b = Instance.new("TextButton")
		b.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 0, ROW_H)
		b.BackgroundColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(45, 83, 103)
		b.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		b.BorderSizePixel = 0
		b.Text = ""
		b.AutoButtonColor = false
		b.LayoutOrder = i
		b.Visible = false
		b.ZIndex = 41
		b.Parent = drop
		addCorner(b, 7)

		local ic = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		ic.Size = UDim2.new(0, 26, 1, 0)
		ic.Position = UDim2.new(0, 4, 0, 0)
		ic.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		ic.TextSize = 16
		ic.ZIndex = 42
		ic.Parent = b

		local nm = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		nm.Size = UDim2.new(1, -100, 1, 0)
		nm.Position = UDim2.new(0, 32, 0, 0)
		nm.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		nm.RichText = true
		nm.TextColor3 = CREAM
		nm.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSansSemibold
		nm.TextSize = 15
		nm.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Left
		nm.TextTruncate = Enum.TextTruncate.AtEnd
		nm.ZIndex = 42
		nm.Parent = b

		local tg = Instance.new("TextLabel")
		tg.Size = UDim2.new(0, 62, 1, 0)
		tg.Position = UDim2.new(1, -66, 0, 0)
		tg.BackgroundTransparency = 1
		tg.TextColor3 = Color3.fromRGB(150, 150, 158)
		tg.Font = Enum.Font.SourceSans
		tg.TextSize = 12
		tg.TextXAlignment = Enum.TextXAlignment.Right
		tg.ZIndex = 42
		tg.Parent = b

		rows[i] = {btn = b, icon = ic, name = nm, tag = tg}
	end

	-- ---------- matching ----------
	local function score(e, q)
		local name = e[1]:lower()
		if name == q then return 100 end
		if name:sub(1, #q) == q then return 90 end
		if name:find(" " .. q, 1, true) or name:find("(" .. q, 1, true) then return 80 end
		if name:find(q, 1, true) then return 70 end
		local hay = name .. " " .. (e[5] or ""):lower()
		local all = true
		for w in q:gmatch("%S+") do
			if not hay:find(w, 1, true) then all = false break end
		end
		if all then return 50 end
		local q2 = q:gsub("%s+", "")
		if #q2 >= 3 then -- forgiving match: letters in order ("fsl" -> Fruit Summoning Loop)
			local qi = 1
			for ci = 1, #name do
				if name:sub(ci, ci) == q2:sub(qi, qi) then
					qi = qi + 1
					if qi > #q2 then return 20 end
				end
			end
		end
		return 0
	end

	local function esc(s)
		s = s:gsub("&", "&amp;")
		s = s:gsub("<", "&lt;")
		s = s:gsub(">", "&gt;")
		return s
	end

	local function highlight(name, q)
		local s, e = name:lower():find(q, 1, true)
		if not s then return esc(name) end
		local ac = accent()
		local col = string.format("rgb(%d,%d,%d)", math.floor(ac.R * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(ac.G * 255 + 0.5), math.floor(ac.B * 255 + 0.5))
		return esc(name:sub(1, s - 1)) .. '<font color="' .. col .. '"><b>' .. esc(name:sub(s, e)) .. '</b></font>' .. esc(name:sub(e + 1))
	end

	local function pick(e)
		box.Text = ""
		setDropVisible(false)
		box:ReleaseFocus()
		goTo(e)
	end

	for i, r in ipairs(rows) do
		r.btn.MouseEnter:Connect(function() setSel(i) end)
		r.btn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
			if results[i] then pick(results[i]) end
		end)
	end

	local function render()
		clearBtn.Visible = box.Text ~= ""
		local q = box.Text:lower()
		q = q:gsub("^%s+", "")
		q = q:gsub("%s+$", "")
		if q == "" then
			results = {}
			setDropVisible(false)
			return
		end
		local found = {}
		local all = {}
		local qs = Settings.QuestSearch
		if type(qs) == "table" then
			for _, e in ipairs(qs) do all[#all + 1] = e end
		end
		for _, e in ipairs(ENTRIES) do all[#all + 1] = e end
		-- every teleport spot / waypoint (built-in PVP ones and your own saved ones, live)
		local okS, spots = pcall(function() return Settings.DX.getSpots and Settings.DX.getSpots() end)
		if okS and type(spots) == "table" then
			for _, sp in ipairs(spots) do
				local pinned = Settings.DX.spotIsPinned and Settings.DX.spotIsPinned(sp)
				local nm = sp.name .. (sp.x == nil and "  (not set)" or "")
				all[#all + 1] = {nm, pinned and "⚔️" or "📍", "teleports", nil,
					"waypoint way point teleport tp spot pvp saved custom location", pinned and "PVP Spot" or "Saved", sp}
			end
		end
		for idx, e in ipairs(all) do
			local s = score(e, q)
			if s > 0 then found[#found + 1] = {e = e, s = s, i = idx} end
		end
		table.sort(found, function(a, b)
			if a.s ~= b.s then return a.s > b.s end
			return a.i < b.i
		end)
		results = {}
		for i = 1, math.min(MAX_ROWS, #found) do results[i] = found[i].e end
		for i, r in ipairs(rows) do
			local e = results[i]
			if e then
				r.btn.Visible = true
				r.icon.Text = e[2]
				r.name.Text = highlight(e[1], q)
				r.tag.Text = e[6] or PAGE_TAGS[e[3]] or ""
			else
				r.btn.Visible = false
			end
		end
		local n = #results
		empty.Visible = (n == 0)
		setDropVisible(true)
		local h = (n == 0) and 46 or (n * ROW_H + (n - 1) * 2 + 8)
		tween(drop, 0.12, {Size = UDim2.new(1, -32, 0, h)})
		setSel(n > 0 and 1 or 0)
	end

	box:GetPropertyChangedSignal("Text"):Connect(render)

	local overDrop = false
	drop.MouseEnter:Connect(function() overDrop = true end)
	drop.MouseLeave:Connect(function()
		overDrop = false
		if not box:IsFocused() then setDropVisible(false) end
	end)

	box.Focused:Connect(function()
		tween(barStroke, 0.18, {Thickness = 1.6, Transparency = 0.15, Color = accent()})
		render()
	end)
	box.FocusLost:Connect(function(enter)
		tween(barStroke, 0.22, {Thickness = 1, Transparency = 0.55, Color = Color3.fromRGB(180, 190, 200)})
		if enter and #results > 0 then
			pick(results[(sel > 0 and sel) or 1])
			return
		end
		task.delay(0.2, function()
			if not box:IsFocused() and not overDrop then setDropVisible(false) end
		end)
	end)

	clearBtn.MouseButton1Click:Connect(function()
		box.Text = ""
		box:CaptureFocus()
	end)

	-- Up / Down arrows move through the results while typing
	UserInputService.InputBegan:Connect(function(input)
		if not box:IsFocused() or #results == 0 then return end
		if input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Down then
			setSel(sel % #results + 1)
		elseif input.KeyCode == Enum.KeyCode.Up then
			setSel((sel - 2) % #results + 1)
		end
	end)
end, function(e) warn("[DannyGui] search bar error: " .. tostring(e)) end)

Settings.loading = false
-- Theme pass after a frame so instance tree is settled
task.defer(function()
	task.wait(0.08)
	pcall(function() refreshGuiLook() end)
end)

-- AUTOSAVE: every setting is checked every 2 seconds and written the moment anything differs from what is
-- on disk, so nothing depends on a button remembering to save. Also saves when you leave / teleport / close.
do
	task.defer(function() pcall(Settings.save) end)
	task.spawn(function()
		while not Settings.stopped do
			task.wait(2)
			if ScreenGui.Parent == nil and Settings.sawGui then break end
			if ScreenGui.Parent ~= nil then Settings.sawGui = true end
			Settings.save()
		end
	end)
	pcall(function()
		Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(function(p) if p == LocalPlayer then Settings.save() end end)
	end)
	pcall(function() LocalPlayer.OnTeleport:Connect(function() Settings.save() end) end)
	pcall(function() ScreenGui.Destroying:Connect(function() Settings.save() end) end)
end

-- Parent the GUI last + defer the heavy ESP scan (main lag fix)
-- ScreenGui already parented early; ensure it still is
if ScreenGui.Parent == nil then
	pcall(function() ScreenGui.Parent = PlayerGui end)
	if ScreenGui.Parent == nil then
		pcall(function() ScreenGui.Parent = CoreGui end)
	end
end

task.defer(function()
	task.wait(0.35) -- let mid UI + quests settle first
	pcall(function()
		if type(startEsp) == "function" then startEsp() end
	end)
end)
]==]
local f, cerr = loadstring(src)
if not f then
	warn("[DannyGui] COMPILE ERROR: " .. tostring(cerr))
	print("[DannyGui] COMPILE ERROR: " .. tostring(cerr))
	return
end
local ok, err = xpcall(f, function(e) return debug.traceback(tostring(e), 2) end)
if not ok then
	warn("[DannyGui] RUNTIME ERROR: " .. tostring(err))
	print("[DannyGui] RUNTIME ERROR: " .. tostring(err))
else
	print("[DannyGui] loaded OK")
end
